“The chief officer of the court, the captain of those who had arrested the young man (he had by this time lost his head, so that there was no trouble to be feared from *that* side), conferred with his colleagues and then prostrated himself upon the earth before the judge, begging to be allowed an explanation. The judge assumed a disturbed expression and bade him be brief. He arose and admitted with evident grief that the horse, in the excitement of the arrest, and in the darkness of the moment (for all this had passed in the night), had got loose and was lost.

===Chinese===

老夫愿传召守门的卫兵出堂作证，他们曾亲眼目睹老夫策马入关。卫兵随即被传唤到庭，一致证实老夫昨夜进城时，胯下骑乘的确实正是一匹完全符合适才描摹的神骏宝驹。老夫看得出来，法官的裁决天平已然倾向了老夫的正义诉求；公堂差役们自然心领神会顺水推舟；而老夫适时向法官保证绝不狮子大开口追索苛刻赔偿，更令他龙颜大悦。我说，只要将这匹坐骑物归原主，老夫便心满意足了，绝不追加索取半文折旧费或误工费。老夫反倒为自己能为公堂略尽绵薄之力而深感荣幸。
“此时法官满脸春风地冲老夫微笑致意，深表赞许；而让他下定决心的另一重考量，是省起即便老夫提出任何官府赔偿，掏的也绝非他自个儿的腰包，而是全由国库公款埋单；毫无疑问，这套算度虽未明言，在场的所有差役衙役心中亦是心照不宣。于是法官当堂下令将宝马发还给老夫，并极其赏脸地发表了如下谕旨——原话并非出自我口，老夫概不担责，但我很高兴法官大人能金口玉言：
“‘这位老实本分的客商，’法官说道，‘将自己的行踪交代得清清楚楚；本城在某种程度上委实欠他一个人情，盖因方才伏法的死囚窃取了他的坐骑，令他遭受了暂时的损失。然而正是由于他的坐骑被盗，才间接促成了凶犯的落网。因此，官府所能为他办到的最小补偿，便是以最快脚程将失物原样奉还。本官下令：将该坐骑连同全副华美鞍鞯，在先行喂饱照料并梳理齐整之后，如数发还失主！’
“老夫极谦卑地深深鞠躬，叩谢公堂青天大老爷的英明裁决。然而，平地又起波澜。
“公堂首领差官——即昨夜亲手生擒逃犯的骑兵统领（此时那犯人早被咔嚓斩了首，死无对证，倒也不虞从‘那一头’生出什么枝节）——同麾下同僚交头接耳一番之后，噗通一声五体投地跪倒在法官案前，苦苦哀求恩准他陈情。法官面露不悦之色，喝令他长话短说。差官爬起身来，面带万分悲戚之色承认：昨夜在抓捕的狂乱撕扯中，加之深更半夜伸手不见五指（全怪昨夜漆黑一片），那匹宝马竟挣脱了缰绳，彻底跑丢了！

===Original===

“Seeing the rising anger of the judge I hastily intervened. I said that I yielded to no one in my admiration for the Mounted Police of the Anti-Date force, the renown of whose efficiency had reached me even in my own distant land. I said that I would be the last to cause the least injustice or even pain. I begged that His Importance (for such was the simple title of a judge in that country) would overlook the unfortunate accident whereby my horse had been lost. I concluded by saying that I would be perfectly content with what we merchants called ‘a minimum valuation,’ that is, a payment of the price the horse would have fetched from what we merchants also call ‘a willing seller.’ In a phrase of which I confess I was secretly proud, I hinted that the doing of justice in this matter would not only be of no charge to the court, but even of some profit to them, seeing that there were certain to be fees of transfer, registration, and whatnot. As a layman I was ignorant of their amount, but I knew them to be attached to such affairs﻿—all out of the taxes.
“The judge, the officers of the court, and every lawyer present, the very sweepers were moved to action. Sundry papers were signed (to which I put the name of Ali﻿—it was the first that occurred to me). I was paid the sum of thirty pieces of gold, and after profound obeisances to all present, and especially to my benefactor the judge, I left the court, yet richer than I had entered it.
---
“My children, what next?
“It is a universal rule in commerce to follow your profits and cut your losses, and men of my profession have a sort of instinct which tells them how long the tide will be flowing with them and when it will turn. I decided that there was yet one more step for me to take.
“The arrest had taken place not far from the edge of the wood whence I had first perceived the city. There my horse, the evening before, had found good pasture. There had I loosened his saddle. There had he known an excellent place of repose. Thither did I wisely suppose my lost friend to have repaired. I sauntered therefore out of the city as though engaged upon no more than a stroll, and sure enough, a league away, under the trees which afforded a grateful shade, the noble beast was reclining, hampered only by his saddle.
“I loosened the girths. He was grateful, and our friendship was renewed.

===Chinese===

眼看法官脸上腾起雷霆之怒，老夫赶忙疾步上前抢话解围。我说，对于彼邦‘禁食椰枣特勤骑警队’的赫赫威名，老夫素来钦佩得五体投地，这支铁骑的无双神勇与精明强干，即便在老夫远隔万里的异域故土亦是如雷贯耳。老夫绝不愿为难任何忠良公差，更不愿平白教各位差爷受过。老夫恳请‘重臣显爵大人’（这正是彼邦法官极质朴的尊号）法外施恩，宽恕这桩致使宝马遗失的不幸变故。最后我表态说，老夫情愿接受咱们生意人所谓的‘保底估值’——也就是任何‘自愿买主’从‘自愿卖主’手中接盘所愿支付的公道市价。在随后一句老夫暗自引以为傲的妙语中，老夫巧妙暗示道：就此案秉公赔付，不仅不会教公堂破费半文公款，甚至还能让各位老爷小赚一笔，毕竟按律势必会衍生出各色过户费、登记费以及杂七杂八的规费嘛！老夫一介白丁虽算不清具体银两，却深知凡经办此等官司，皆有这类公帑陋规贴补上下——横竖全打百姓赋税里出嘛。
“法官、差官、在场的所有讼师状师，乃至公堂上的扫地杂役，瞬间齐齐被老夫的话打动，热火朝天地忙碌起来。转瞬间各色文书画押妥当（在签名画押处，老夫顺手签下了‘阿里’的大名——这是脑海里蹦出的第一个假名）。官府当场兑付了足足三十枚（30）金币现银；老夫向全堂上下、尤其向老夫的青天大恩人法官大人再三行过深鞠躬大礼之后，昂首挺胸踱出了公堂——怀里的金银比起进堂时生生又多了一大截！
---
“孩子们，接下去又该如何？
“在商场之中，一条放之四海而皆准的金科玉律便是：见好就收，扩大战果；而从事老夫这一行的巨擘，皆有一种野兽般的直觉，能嗅出财运大潮何时正顺风顺水，又将在何处掉头反转。我断定，眼前还有最后一着好戏留待我去唱完。
“昨夜擒获逃犯之处，距离老夫初次窥见城廓的那片林缘并不遥远。在那里，昨夜我那匹坐骑饱食了肥美水草。在那里，老夫曾为它松开了马鞍。在那里，它曾觅得一处极其舒适的栖息佳所。依循老夫的精明睿智，我料定我那失散的良友八成早已重返旧地了。于是老夫佯装若无其事闲庭信步般溜达出城；果不其然，行至一联盟之外，在那片投下宜人浓荫的茂林巨树下，那匹神骏的良驹正惬意地仰卧在青草丛中，唯独被背上的马鞍略微绊住了手脚。
“老夫上前为它彻底松解了马肚带。它感激不尽，我们的金兰之谊由此焕发新生。

===Original===

But though my affection was increased by such a recovery, I steeled my heart for what I purposed next to do.
“It is a maxim of all sound business that a thing should be sold as often as possible, and it was clear that I now had an opportunity of selling this charming creature for the third time. It was equally clear that, if I delayed, the opportunity would pass; for the story of my appearance in court would spread through the city, the officers would talk with their friends about the saddle and the description of the animal; I might even get into a difficult tangle with the authorities.
“But the whole of this propitious day was in the hand of Heaven. For, while yet the sun was high, there came upon me through the pasture a shepherd driving his sheep, and to him I told a tale that I had been sent by my master to sell the horse I was leading, and his saddle, to a certain dealer, who had already seen them and bargained for them. I had been given a writing with the name of the dealer in the neighbouring city, but I had lost the writing and could not remember the name or direction.
“The shepherd told me that he only went to that city from time to time, but he was well acquainted with it; the purchaser could be none other than Abd-ul-Eblis.
“The moment he pronounced this name I clapped my hands together and said: ‘Abd-ul-Eblis! That was the name!’ I thanked the shepherd for thus refreshing my memory, and I carefully walked by the beast’s side as should a mere servant by his master’s precious possession. I avoided the main gate (which I had now passed twice and where I might be too well known) and entered the city by a little postern. I found from inquiry of a blind man﻿—which was the more prudent﻿—the way to Abd-ul-Eblis’s stables.
“I made no plan of what I should do, for on those days when I am specially favoured by the Most High I leave His Power to guide me﻿ ﻿… and to guide also those with whom I do business. I went no farther than to tell the groom that I had come to find a purchaser for the horse﻿—not indeed in this city, where I had been told the market was poor, but in a place two days’ journey away, where the news of the famous beast’s coming had already been spread. I then wandered out into the streets to take the cool air of the evening. It was as I had expected.

===Chinese===

然而，尽管失而复得教我对这头牲口的怜爱倍增，老夫的心肠却如坚铁般冷硬，已然盘算妥了下一步的杀招。
“一切稳健经营的第一信条，便是将同一件家当尽可能反复变卖出手！眼下明摆着，老夫迎来了将这头惹人喜爱的神兽第三次转手倒卖的天赐良机！同样显而易见的是，稍有迟疑，良机便会稍纵即逝；因为老夫出庭的奇谈势必会传遍全城街巷，差役们亦会同狐朋狗友大肆嚼舌头议论马鞍与坐骑的样貌；老夫甚至很可能同官府陷入纠缠不清的凶险泥潭。
“然而这顺遂的大吉之日，自始至终皆操持在天意神掌之中。因为烈日当空之际，穿过草甸迎面走来一个赶羊的牧童；我便向他编造了一套瞎话，说家主打发我牵着这匹骏马与马鞍，前去交付城中某位早已相中并谈妥价钱的马贩。家主曾赐我一张写有临近市镇马贩名讳地址的字条，偏偏叫我给弄丢了，眼下记不得字号门路。
“牧童告诉我，他虽只是偶尔进城，对那市面却了如指掌；能吃得下这等名驹的买主，全城除了阿卜杜·埃布利斯（Abd-ul-Eblis），绝无第二号人物！
“他刚一吐露这尊大名，老夫便击掌叫绝道：‘阿卜杜·埃布利斯！正是这个大名！’我重谢牧童为老夫指点迷津，随后小心翼翼地牵马伴行，俨然一个忠心耿耿护送主家至宝的家奴。我特意绕开了主城门（老夫已然打那儿进出过两回，保不齐已被守卫混了个脸熟），转而从一处偏僻的小角门溜进了城内。为了万全起见，我向一个沿街瞎子打听路径——瞎子总不会日后认出老夫——顺顺当当摸到了阿卜杜·埃布利斯的豪华马厩。
“我胸无定算，因为在老夫深蒙至高主宰特殊眷顾的日子里，我向来全凭神力从旁指引……并顺带指引那些同老夫做买卖的冤大头。我进门只对马夫撂下一句话：说我奉命为这匹宝马寻觅买主——并非在这座据说市面疲软的本地市镇发售，而是打算运往两天（2）路程之外的重镇，那匹名驹即将抵境的盛名早已在彼地传得沸沸扬扬。撂下话后，我便大摇大摆踱步到大街上吹风纳凉。一切果不出老夫所料。

===Original===

When I returned to see that my horse had been well fed, Abd-ul-Eblis was present in the stable and eager to deal.
“He pointed out to me the advantages he enjoyed for disposing of horses, the dangers of the distant journey of which I had spoken to the groom, the possibility of what is called in the language of that country ‘a proposition.’ He showed me what, in my innocence, I might have forgotten, that it was not as though the horse was my own. That *I* could only be a gainer. That my master would be none the wiser. That I might pretend any accident to have taken place (for indeed such an accident was likely if I went on farther). He also was at the pains of repeating what I might have forgotten, that I was free to retain for myself some portion of the price, assuring me that he would keep silent upon the matter. In the end I promised to hand him over the horse for sixty pieces of gold.
“There are some men, my dear nephews, who even in these circumstances would have begun bargaining for a higher price. These are men who love the making of small sums and who do not understand the enormous weight of caprice and chance in human affairs. So far from attempting to get a higher price, I expressed my gratitude and said that for my part I was quite willing to take less, but that I somewhat feared my master’s anger and could not return to him without at least fifty pieces of gold, adding that I considered ten pieces a sufficient reward for myself. At the same time I advised Abdul not to sell the saddle *with* the horse, nor did I omit to remind him that horses of a light colour are more easily dyed than those of a darker hue.
“At these suggestions of mine he looked upon me mournfully for a few moments and then slowly counted out sixty pieces of gold. I took a long farewell of the kindly, patient, and beautiful animal, which had borne me to this fortune in the short space of one day, and then walked forth through the city into the evening, preferring the chance of a lodging in the forest to tempting further the singular Fate that had so far befriended me.
“The weather was warm, the neighbouring wood, as I knew by experience, hospitable. There would I spend the few hours of darkness, building myself a small fire to keep off the beasts and to cherish me.

===Chinese===

待我折回马厩探看坐骑是否吃饱喝足时，阿卜杜·埃布利斯本人早已急不可耐地候在马厩里，满脸巴望着谈成这桩大买卖。
“他向我极力铺陈自己在脱手良驹上的通天手段，大谈特谈老夫向马夫吹嘘的远行凶险，以及彼邦行话切口中所谓的‘分成买卖’。他热心开导我这个‘懵懂无知的老实人’，提醒我这马反正又不是老夫自个儿的私产。老夫横竖只赚不赔；家主蒙在鼓里压根不会起疑；老夫大可谎称半路上出了车祸意外（反正若是继续远行，出车祸也是早晚的事）。他甚至不辞辛劳地反复点醒老夫：老夫大可在马价里私吞扣留下一大笔暗佣，他保证守口如瓶绝不声张。最终，老夫答应以六十枚（60）金币的价码，将宝马正式交割予他。
“我亲爱的侄儿们，换作旁人，身处这等情势往往会贪得无厌地坐地起价。那是只知蝇头小利的市井短视之徒，压根参不透天意无常与命运机变在人间事务中那泰山压顶般的分量。老夫不仅没有讨价还价，反而对他感激涕零，表态说自个儿情愿再让步少收几文，只是唯恐家主震怒，若是兜里没有至少五十枚（50）金币回去交差恐难保全性命，并补充说自个儿只要能分润十枚（10）金币的犒赏便心满意足了。与此同时，老夫体贴地提点阿卜杜：切莫连同马鞍‘一道’发售；亦不忘叮嘱他浅毛色的烈马比起深毛色的牲口，更容易用药草染料掉包改换毛色。
“听闻老夫这番肺腑之言，他神色凄楚地凝视了老夫片刻，随后慢条斯理地当面如数点出了六十枚（60）足色金币。老夫深情依依地向那匹温顺、隐忍而俊美的神兽道了永别——正是它，在短短一天的一昼夜里，将老夫推上了这般万贯家产的巅峰！随后老夫昂首阔步穿城而过遁入夜色之中；比起继续挑衅那一路眷顾老夫的离奇命运，老夫情愿在莽莽荒林中幕天席地将就一夜。
“天色温煦燥热，凭着经验，身旁的密林待客向来厚道。老夫正好可以在林间消磨这几个时辰的夜色，生起一堆篝火驱赶野兽，好生抚慰我劳顿的身心。

===Original===

Thence, I did not doubt, I could the next morning, with now so satisfactory a capital, proceed to the re-edification of my fortune.
“I reached the wooded hill which overlooked the city. I recited my third night prayers. Before building my fire and disposing myself to sleep, I looked at the outline of the walls and domes and graceful minarets against the last of the evening, and I revolved in my mind that thought which shall ever be mine on my departure from any town. Let it also be yours, my dear children, in all your travels.
“For just as when you come to a new city of a morning, before you enter it, and after having prayed God, you should muse within yourself what sums of money you may hope to lift from its inhabitants; so when you leave any city at evening, never omit (after due thanks to your Creator!) to calculate what sums you have indeed subtracted from those to whom you bid farewell.”
As the old merchant ceased it was like the ending of a strain of solemn music, the echoes of which linger and continue in the memory. The strangely moving words he had uttered stirred a profound in the depths of their young souls, and they sat with bowed heads until the horrid outrage of the muezzin’s call murdered that sacred silence.
At the signal the lads rose and filed out on tiptoe leaving their uncle with his eyes closed and his lips murmuring in prayer.

===Chinese===

待到翌日拂晓，揣着这般令人满意的雄厚本钱，老夫毫不怀疑自己定能重整旗鼓，大举重建我的富贵江山！
“老夫攀上了俯瞰城廓的葱郁林冈。我虔诚诵读完夜间的第三道晚祷。在引燃篝火、宽衣就寝之前，我凭高凝视着暮色余晖下城墙、穹顶与秀美尖塔勾勒出的壮丽剪影，脑海中不住翻滚着那道每逢辞别任何一座城池时皆会油然而生的至理名言。我亲爱的孩子们，愿你们在日后浪迹天涯的万里征途中，亦将此奉为圭臬：
“盖因每逢清晨造访一座崭新的城邑，在踏入城门之前，于祷告真主之余，诸位心中皆当暗自盘算：此番能从城中百姓兜里捞走多少金银；同样，每逢黄昏辞别任何一座城邑之际，在向造物主虔诚谢恩之后，亦万万切莫忘记——好好核算一番：自己究竟从那些向你道别的顺民身上，搜刮掠夺走了多少真金白银！”
老商人语声渐歇，宛如一曲庄严肃穆的绝美乐章悄然落幕，余音袅袅在众人记忆深处久久回荡。他这番掷地有声的奇绝言辞，在侄儿们年幼的心灵深处激荡起难以言喻的惊涛骇浪；他们个个躬身垂首屏息凝神，直到宣礼塔上那凄厉刺耳的破空呼拜声，蛮横无理地将这神圣的死寂彻底撕碎。
听到这一号令，少年们纷纷起身，蹑手蹑脚屏息鱼贯退出房间；留下他们的叔父紧闭双目，双唇依然在虔敬地呢喃着祷词。

## IX *Al-Jamal Wa’l-Nakhl*, or “Camels and Dates” / 第九章 骆驼与椰枣
===Original===

When the hour of public execution had arrived the boys came timorously into their rich uncle’s presence, and seating themselves upon the expensive carpet at the feet of his divan, prepared to hear the continuation of his adventures.
That excellent old man began as follows:
“I warn you, my children, that the path to wealth, which (by the Mercy of Allah) I have been allowed to tread, is varied and difficult. Profit by my misadventures! Remain determined to enrich yourselves, even after the worst mishaps! Yea! After wealth and poverty (like mine) renewed wealth and (alas!) renewed poverty never despair. Still hold to gold and still determine your fate. Still thirst for money. But all the while most reverently worship Him the Supreme, the All-compelling, the Giver of Great bags of coin. No talent in the deception of individuals or the gulling of the crowd can of itself bring the great reward. The acquirement of those immense sums which are the chief glory of man, is, like all else, in the Hand of God.
“My brother, your worthy though impecunious father, has sufficiently grounded you in the essentials of our holy religion. You will not repine if you turn out to be one of the ninety-nine who end their lives in the gutter, rather than the blessed hundredth who attains, as I have attained, to the possession of a palace and of innumerable slaves.﻿ ﻿…”
Having so spoken the aged merchant bent for a moment in silent prayer and then proceeded:
“You will remember that at the conclusion of my last adventure I had reached a position, not of affluence, but at least of tolerable fortune. I was possessed of a train of camels, each heavily laden with two large panniers of dates, and drivers to conduct the whole.
“You will further remember how, on my arrival in Laknes, as I was anxious to make the best of my time I spoke freely to all of my merchandise, extolled its character, described how I intended to put it up for sale next day in the public markets, and spread abroad the name of Ishmaïl-of-Taftah which happened for the moment to be mine.
“The rumour spread (as I had intended it should). I strolled through the narrow streets of the town after sunset, and was glad to hear my arrival discussed, and my wares. I had promise for the morrow. I returned to my men.

===Chinese===

闹市公开行刑的时辰再次降临，孩子们战战兢兢地来到阔绰的叔父面前，盘腿坐在他长榻脚边昂贵的地毯上，准备恭听叔父后来的奇遇。
那位可敬的老人开口说道：
“孩子们，我得告诫你们：承蒙真主的仁慈（Mercy of Allah）恩准老夫踏上的这条生财大道，向来坎坷曲折、千难万险。务必从老夫的蹉跎厄运中汲取教益！即便遭遇再惨烈的浩劫，也要百折不挠誓要发家致富！不错！在富贵与贫贱之间（宛如老夫这般），重获富贵之后又（唉！）再度倾家荡产，却永远不可轻言放弃。牢牢咬定黄金，亲手主宰命运。对金钱永葆贪婪的饥渴。然而在此期间，务必时时刻刻以最虔诚的心志顶礼膜拜那位至高无上、统御乾坤、赐下大袋银钱的主宰。单凭欺瞒个人的雕虫小技或愚弄群氓的诡计，断难平白招致这等泼天富贵。攫取那些象征着人间最高荣耀的天文巨财，同世间万物一样，全在真主掌握之中。
“家兄——也就是诸位可敬却囊空如洗的生父——想必已向你们好生灌输过圣教大义的纲领要旨。倘若你们最终沦落为倒毙沟壑的那九十九个（99）倒霉蛋之一，而非像老夫这般荣膺为坐拥巍峨宫殿与无数奴仆的第100位（100）受造物主青睐的幸运儿，你们亦切莫心怀怨怼……
老商人言尽于此，躬身静默祷告了片刻，随后接着讲述道：
“想必诸位还记得，在上一回奇遇的末尾，老夫虽谈不上大富大贵，好歹也混入了一份颇为体面的家当。我坐拥一整支骆驼商队，每峰骆驼两侧皆驮运着两大箩筐（2）沉甸甸的椰枣，并配有沿途照拂押运的脚夫队伍。
“诸位当还记得，抵达成邑拉克内斯（Laknes）之际，因急于抢抓商机，我处处向人津津乐道兜售的货物，吹嘘其品相绝伦，宣称自己打算次日在市集公开发售，并满城散播老夫当时权且顶替的‘塔夫塔商人伊斯梅尔’的名头。
“正如老夫所愿，流言蜚语迅速传开。日落之后，我信步穿行在城中小巷里，欣喜地听闻满街都在津津乐道我的到来和我的货物。明日市面一片大好。我安心返回客栈歇息。

===Original===

“I had already spread out my bed upon the corner of the yard, when there came up a slave magnificently dressed, who bowed to the ground, and approaching my presence asked whether he had the honour and felicity to address the renowned merchant Ishmaïl. He bore an invitation from the greatest merchant in the city, whose name I had already heard half a dozen times in Taftah, and whom all the merchants there revered from afar for his enormous riches: a certain Yusouff ben Ahmed, also called ‘El-Zafari,’ or the Triumphant.
“Late as was the hour I purchased finery; with my last gold I hired a donkey of strange magnificence, and arrived at the palace of Yusouff, dressed in a fashion which I could ill afford, but which I regarded as an investment.
“I had expected to find within this palace that admirable simplicity of manner which is inseparable from really great wealth: Nor was I disappointed. The inner room to which I was led, encrusted everywhere with black marble, boasted no ornament save three white alabaster jars as tall as a man and of immense antiquity. They had formerly been the property of a young noble whom Yusouff had ruined, and *he* had them of the Sultan. In the midst shone the single pure flame of a massive silver lamp, rifled from the tomb of a saint. It now hung dependent from a chain of the same metal, the height of which was lost in the gloom of the lofty cupola.
“A fountain of scented water﻿—I could not name its odour precisely, but I guessed it to be Fior de Goyim﻿—plashed gently into a basin of porphyry at the end of the apartment.
“Yusouff and two other guests (who alone had been asked to meet me), rose from the exceedingly costly rugs of Persia whereon they had reclined, and gravely saluted me. The master of the house, after the first salutations and an invocation upon my head of the Mercy of Allah, told me that the feast was ready prepared, but that before summoning it he would ask me to honour the house and survey what poor ornaments he might be able to show me.
“I was expressly delighted at his tone. It was that which I had already heard to be native to princes of commerce. He had already acquired, in the few years that had elapsed since he had cleaned the streets for a living, a well-bred restraint of gesture, and when he spoke it was in the tone of one who thought negligible the whole world, including his guest.

===Chinese===

“老夫刚在庭院的一角铺展好卧榻，便见一名穿戴极其奢华的奴仆疾步走来，噗通一声五体投地拜倒在地；他战战兢兢凑到近前，探问自个儿是否有幸能面见享誉遐迩的大商贾伊斯梅尔大人。他呈递来一封出自本城首富手笔的请柬；那巨贾的名号老夫在塔夫塔（Taftah）便听过不下六回（6），那里的所有客商皆因其富可敌国的滔天财力而远隔万里对其敬若神明——那便是大名鼎鼎的优素福·本·艾哈迈德（Yusouff ben Ahmed），亦被称为‘胜利者扎法里’。
“时辰虽已不早，老夫依然连夜采办了一身锦绣行头；掏出仅剩的闲置金币，租赁了一头气派非凡的名贵坐骑毛驴；随后衣冠楚楚赶赴优素福的豪门王府——那身行头老夫本消受不起，但我将其视作一笔关键的投资。
“老夫原本料定在这等王府之内，必能领略到真正豪门巨贾身上那不可分割的质朴大雅之风；而我果真未曾失望。老夫被引至内堂，满室皆镶嵌着深邃的黑大理石，举目望去毫无庸俗点缀，唯独供奉着三只（3）高齐人顶、年代久远到难以考证的白雪花石膏古瓮。那原先是一位被优素福整得家破人亡的年轻贵族的私产，而那位贵族当年又是从苏丹手里获赐的。大堂正中，悬挂着一盏打圣徒陵墓里盗掘出来的沉重纯银油灯，散发着一缕幽微纯净的焰火。那银灯由一条同等银质的巨链悬空吊挂，链顶的高处隐没在穹顶幽深的阴影之中。
“在庭院的一隅，一座喷吐着芬芳香水的喷泉——老夫道不清那究竟是何等香型，猜度着八成是‘外邦之花’——正叮咚作响地轻轻落入一座紫斑岩的石盆之中。
“优素福以及另外两位（2）被特意请来作陪的贵客（今夜唯独邀了他们作陪），从他们斜倚着的昂贵波斯地毯上款款起身，神情肃穆地向老夫致意。在寒暄客套并以真主的仁慈（Mercy of Allah）向老夫当头祈福之后，庄园主人告知老夫盛宴已然齐备，但在传膳之前，烦请老夫赏脸过府巡视一番，权当评鉴他府上这几件微不足道的寒酸陈设。
“他的谦卑口吻令老夫格外受用。这正是老夫早有耳闻的商界巨室特有的腔调。自打他早年靠沿街扫马路糊口至今短短数年间，他已然练就了一副极具教养的沉稳气度；说话轻声细语。

===Original===

I prayed fervently, as I accompanied the leisurely steps of my great entertainer, that when I should have achieved a similar fortune I should myself as quickly acquire this distinctive manner of the great. I watched him narrowly in order to imitate (when I should have left his presence) those peculiar little details which mark affluence and are of such service in negotiation. He would often interpose words of his own into the midst of another’s sentence. It pleased him not to answer some repeated question. He would change the conversation at his pleasure without too much regard for what I might have been saying immediately before. He also turned to another guest, while I was addressing him and in every way showed his superiority.
“When we had sat down to meat I was further edified by the varied information, the extensive culture of my host. He would lead the talk on to some subject which he had recently acquired from his numerous secretaries, and dally upon it at a length which would have been tedious in one of lesser station. But all this was done with such an air of *money* that it was impossible to feel the slightest tedium, though his minute description of things which we all knew by heart extended more than once to a full quarter of an hour.
“During the progress of this divine repast I noted with pleasure that the distinguished master of the house never once introduced the subject of my affairs.
“I would have you remember, my dear nephews,” said Mahmoud at this point, “that nothing is less pleasing in a merchant, especially in one of approved success, than the introduction of profit and loss at a meal; for profit and loss are of such profound importance that their mere mention must distract from the legitimate pleasures of the table.
“It was not until a late hour, when the two other guests (whose insignificant names I have not attempted to retain) had arisen to depart, that affairs began.
“With the subtle tact of commercial genius my host retained me, gripping my arm. I ventured in the absence of any witness to say a few words upon what was nearest my heart: I asked him ‘*How were dates?*’
“To my delight he proved affable. He unbent in a degree unworthy of so small an occasion and listened with the greatest attention to my simple tale.

===Chinese===

那种低沉悦耳的语调，唯独在全天下拥有巨额财富的显贵口中方能领略得到。他引着老夫穿过大宅，漫不经心地向老夫展示那些挂毯、泥金手卷，以及大批装满未切割原钻的水晶宝匣。
“随后我们入席就座，那场盛宴委实奢华无匹；老夫留心到在整场宴席间，客人们连半句商贸买卖的字眼都未曾吐露。席间高谈阔论的全是诗词歌赋、各地清真寺砖瓦的繁复纹样，以及哲学典故——正如凡俗凡夫往往以为商贾巨擘在私下里无非是这等文雅高尚的模样。
“待到宴罢宾客散尽，优素福留下了老夫，将我引至一处更幽深狭小的精舍，那里仅铺着一张极狭长的草席，唯独墙角立着一只盛放凉水的陶壶。
“我们相对而坐，他亲手为我端上一杯清凉的泉水，开口说道：
“‘贤弟，方才在市井席间，老夫不便向你垂询这等凡俗之事，免得污了在座那些高雅文士的耳朵。然而眼下只剩你我二人，老夫便开门见山了：听闻你今日从塔夫塔（Taftah）押运着一支骆驼商队入城，车上全驮着货物？’
“老夫自然毫不隐瞒，直言告知他手头眼下不过区区几峰骆驼（老夫自无必要向他坦白自己在全天下连半文其他浮财都没剩）。老夫以漫不经心的轻佻语调暗示道，这么点数目充其量算不上什么商队，不过是老夫出门游历途中权作消遣的一桩微末玩物罢了。随后老夫顺嘴带出一句，说自个儿在驼背上——纯粹出于散心——驮运了区区一点‘椰枣’。
“一听到‘椰枣’二字，福星高照的优素福脸色骤然剧变。他起初垂下眼帘，流露出一副发自肺腑的深切忧虑之色；随后他抬起头来，目光焦灼而凝重地死死注视着我，开口说道：
“‘这本非老夫分内之事……老夫横加干涉，贤弟或许会心生不悦。’
“老夫忙不迭向他保证，老夫梦寐以求的莫过于能聆听这位深蒙上苍眷顾的高人指点迷津。老夫那点微薄的小本货物，究竟该如何脱手为上？老夫巴望听他回话的急切心情，委实难以言表！
“他长长地叹了一口粗气，摇了摇头，以一种老夫断无法生厌的熟稔口吻答道：
“‘我可怜的朋友啊……！’
“接着他又长叹一声，补充道：
“‘老夫真不知该如何给你拿主意了……实不相瞒，自打今儿起，椰枣在这全城上下怕是连半颗都卖不出去了！城里近来刚刚发生了一桩惊天大变故——确切说就是上周的事。咱们埃米尔的生母——也就是当朝太后——立下遗嘱，将她名下那片浩瀚无垠的皇家枣椰林悉数捐赠给了国家充公，立下严命每周将新鲜椰枣“免费”配发给全城所有百姓。官府勒令万民歌颂太后的仁德，市井草民自是欢天喜地。然而对于那些库里囤着大批椰枣的倒霉客商而言，这无异于灭顶之灾！他们的货转眼全成了烂泥。运到邻国境内的邻邦也断无销路，因为那边的律法认定吃椰枣会招致牙痛，刚刚颁布了一道名为《严禁椰枣令》的酷法，严苛之极。老夫自个儿（纯粹出于商界领袖的公德心）以倾家荡产的血亏天价，将大部分小商贩的库存全盘托底收购，免得这帮小本经营之人破产跳楼，以免引发市面恐慌。老夫为了天下公义，实则是割肉饲虎、舍生取义啊！’说到这里，他又是一声沉重的长叹，陷入了死寂。
“我亲爱的侄儿们，你们尽可想象这一记晴天霹雳对于你们那位不幸的叔父而言是何等致命！那些装满椰枣的箩筐（每峰骆驼各驮两筐），除了这几头牲口本身之外，可是老夫在世间的全副家当啊！

===Original===

I told him frankly that I had with me at the moment but few camels (I was under no necessity to confess that I had not another asset in the world). I suggested by my negligent tone that such a number could hardly be called a caravan and was little more than a distraction with which I amused myself on my travels. I then dropped the fact that I had loaded them﻿—more as a pastime than anything else﻿—with a few *dates*.
“At this second mention of the word ‘*dates*’ the face of Yusouff-the-Blessed suddenly changed. He at first cast his eyes down in an expression of real concern. Then, looking up at me anxiously and steadily, he said:
“ ‘This is no affair of mine.﻿ ﻿… You may resent my interference.’
“I assured him that I desired nothing more than a hint from one so favoured of Heaven. How I had better dispose of my trifling merchandise? I was more anxious to hear his reply than it is possible to say!
“He sighed heavily, shook his head, and answered with a certain familiarity that I could not resent:
“ ‘My poor friend﻿ ﻿… !’
“He then sighed again and added:
“ ‘I really do not see how I can advise you.﻿ ﻿… The truth is that dates will from henceforth be almost unsaleable here. There has lately taken place﻿—indeed it was but last week﻿—an extraordinary thing. The mother of our Emir﻿—the dowager﻿—has left by will the whole of her immense date groves in trust to the nation with orders that regular weekly distribution shall be made *free* to all the citizens. We are bidden praise her generosity and the masses are of course delighted. But it is ruin for the poor merchants whose stock of dates are now so much dross. They cannot sell to our neighbours in the country over the border, for these hold dates to be evil from their effect in giving the toothache. Their new law, called the Date Prohibition Act, is of the most rigorous kind. I have myself (from a sense of public duty) bought up the greater portion at a ruinous loss to prevent the failure of smaller men and to avoid a panic. I have sacrificed myself to the public good.’ He sighed heavily once more and was silent.
“You may imagine, my dear nephews, the effect of this news upon your unfortunate uncle! The panniers of dates (two for each camel) were, save the animals themselves, all that I had in the world.

===Chinese===

老夫历尽千辛万苦、忍受无尽饥寒与生死险阻横穿不毛荒原，恰恰正是听信了此地乃是全天下椰枣销路最畅、行情最火爆的宝地；而如今老夫前有绝境后有追兵，孑然一身流落异乡，唯一的孤注一掷竟落得血本无归……我回想起不久前那段凄惨赤贫的流浪岁月，一想到眼前那些烂在手里的椰枣和一片漆黑的末路，浑身止不住打起冷战！前方是严刑峻法封杀椰枣的邻邦，身后是咬牙切齿的海捕追兵。老夫心底里万念俱灰！
“尽管老夫自负在商海伪装术上颇有些道行，优素福却显然已将我的心思摸了个底朝天。他全然不顾老夫先前吹嘘这批货不过是散心玩物的托词，对我当下的厄运表达了最深切的同情，恳请老夫稍安勿躁，容他在心底好生盘算一番该如何为老夫排忧解难。
“言毕，他用右手捂住脸颊，垂下头颅，手肘支在膝头，陷入了生意人特有的深沉凝思之中。待他重新抬起头来时，老夫惊愕地发现他的面容竟变得如此憔悴枯槁；老夫禁不住暗自惊叹：一位坐拥这般滔天权势的巨擘，何以竟会为一个萍水相逢的外乡过客的偶然横祸而如此劳心伤神！然而老夫曾在典籍中读到过，商界巨子往往怀揣着一颗最慈悲柔软的菩萨心肠，对于他们身上任何心血来潮的慈善壮举，世人皆断不可大惊小怪。
“因此，当听闻优素福以斩钉截铁的决断口吻宣布他已找到了唯一的生路、决意亲自掏腰包买下老夫的椰枣时，你们尽可想象老夫心中的狂喜！
“‘老夫给你的价码，’（他坦诚相告），‘断无法比得上这一两日前那般优厚；太后免费发枣的遗诏彻底冲垮了一切。但老夫愿按往常市价的足足四分之一兑付给你——这已经远高出你在别处所能搜刮到的任何开价了。老夫家财万贯。你是远道而来的外乡客，亦是咱们这座城池的贵宾。积德行善向来功不唐捐。保不齐日后某天老夫亦有仰仗贤弟相助的时刻。你我虽仅相识数个时辰，但老夫深信你我早已心心相印。况且老夫也不瞒你，老夫“或许”有法子把亏空挽回大半。老夫在远方城邑有独家代理眼线，坐拥旁人所不具备的分销门路……罢了！就这么办！老夫索性给你开出足足三分之一（1/3）的高价！

===Original===

I had traversed the waste at the cost of much labour, infinite privation, and mortal perils, precisely because this district had the reputation of being by far the best market for dates, and here was I, with an enemy left behind me, alone in the world, and my sole venture ruined.﻿ ﻿… I remembered my dreadful poverty, only so recently past, and I shuddered as I considered those unsaleable dates and my black future! Before me was a country where dates were rigorously forbidden by law; behind me a hue and cry. Despair was in my heart!
“Though I trust I have a sufficient degree of the arts essential to our profession, Yusouff must have guessed my thoughts. Ignoring my former statement that the goods I had with me were but a toy, and that I was indifferent to their fate, he expressed the deepest sympathy with my plight and begged me to bear with him while he reflected within himself how he might be of service.
“Having said this he covered his face with his right hand, bowed his head, leant his elbow upon his knee, and for some moments was plunged in what merchants use as thought. When he raised his face I was shocked to see how haggard it had become, and I marvelled that one so circumstanced should care so much for the chance misfortunes of a stranger. But I had read that these Princes of Commerce were often of tenderest heart and that one should never be surprised at any freak of generosity on their part.
“Judge therefore of my delight on hearing Yusouff say in a determined voice that he had concluded upon the only issue and that he would purchase my dates himself!
“ ‘I cannot’ (he frankly added) ‘give you as good a price even as I could have given a day or two ago; the old Queen’s idea of free dates has swamped everything. But I will pay a good quarter of the customary price﻿—which is far more than you now could obtain elsewhere. I am very wealthy. You are a stranger and, as it were, our guest in this town. A good deed is never thrown away. Perhaps some day I shall be glad of your aid also. I have seen you a few hours only, but I think we know each other’s hearts already. Moreover, I do not conceal it from you, I *may* save much of the loss. I have special correspondents in distant towns, and opportunities of sale which others do not possess.﻿ ﻿… Come! I’ll do it! I will offer you this price of one-third.

===Chinese===

老夫历尽千辛万苦、忍受无尽饥寒与生死险阻横穿不毛荒原，恰恰正是听信了此地乃是全天下椰枣销路最畅、行情最火爆的宝地；而如今老夫前有绝境后有追兵，孑然一身流落异乡，唯一的孤注一掷竟落得血本无归……我回想起不久前那段凄惨赤贫的流浪岁月，一想到眼前那些烂在手里的椰枣和一片漆黑的末路，浑身止不住打起冷战！前方是严刑峻法封杀椰枣的邻邦，身后是咬牙切齿的海捕追兵。老夫心底里万念俱灰！
“尽管老夫自负在商海伪装术上颇有些道行，优素福却显然已将我的心思摸了个底朝天。他全然不顾老夫先前吹嘘这批货不过是散心玩物的托词，对我当下的厄运表达了最深切的同情，恳请老夫稍安勿躁，容他在心底好生盘算一番该如何为老夫排忧解难。
“言毕，他用右手捂住脸颊，垂下头颅，手肘支在膝头，陷入了生意人特有的深沉凝思之中。待他重新抬起头来时，老夫惊愕地发现他的面容竟变得如此憔悴枯槁；老夫禁不住暗自惊叹：一位坐拥这般滔天权势的巨擘，何以竟会为一个萍水相逢的外乡过客的偶然横祸而如此劳心伤神！然而老夫曾在典籍中读到过，商界巨子往往怀揣着一颗最慈悲柔软的菩萨心肠，对于他们身上任何心血来潮的慈善壮举，世人皆断不可大惊小怪。
“因此，当听闻优素福以斩钉截铁的决断口吻宣布他已找到了唯一的生路、决意亲自掏腰包买下老夫的椰枣时，你们尽可想象老夫心中的狂喜！
“‘老夫给你的价码，’（他坦诚相告），‘断无法比得上这一两日前那般优厚；太后免费发枣的遗诏彻底冲垮了一切。但老夫愿按往常市价的足足四分之一兑付给你——这已经远高出你在别处所能搜刮到的任何开价了。老夫家财万贯。你是远道而来的外乡客，亦是咱们这座城池的贵宾。积德行善向来功不唐捐。保不齐日后某天老夫亦有仰仗贤弟相助的时刻。你我虽仅相识数个时辰，但老夫深信你我早已心心相印。况且老夫也不瞒你，老夫“或许”有法子把亏空挽回大半。老夫在远方城邑有独家代理眼线，坐拥旁人所不具备的分销门路……罢了！就这么办！老夫索性给你开出足足三分之一（1/3）的高价！

===Original===

It is but a poor price,’ said he, sighing yet again most heavily, ‘but it is far, far better than no price at all.’
“My relief was beyond words. I had seen myself leaving my merchandise unsold or sacrificing it at a ruinous nothing. That which Yusouff offered me was the difference between despair and a shred of hope, and though the loss was severe it left me at least with some capital for a further venture.
“Great men have a sort of simplicity in their dealings. Hardly had Yusouff discovered my gratitude and my immediate acceptance of his gift (for I could call it by no other name), than the princely fellow clapped his hands, sent for his treasurer, and had counted to me upon the spot a hundred pieces of gold. I gave him my writing of delivery, which he handed to another slave with a few words in a low voice. Then he continued to talk to me, for he was determined to detain me far into the night. Indeed it was near dawn before he whom I will now call my friend, and to whom I felt bound for life by the greatest ties of grateful affection, allowed me to pass his gates and to return to my hostelry.
“There I found that my panniers had already been removed and their contents conveyed to the purchaser’s warehouse. I admired the promptitude in business which so often accompanies a generous heart.
---
“With the early hours of the next day, before the sun had yet acquired too great power, I strolled through the bazaar, not so much cast down at the thought of my loss as cheered by the recollection that I possessed, after all, one hundred good pieces of solid gold.
“With a malicious pleasure I approached the stall of a fruit-seller. Putting down a small copper coin I begged him for a handful of dates.
“ ‘I need not full measure,’ said I, ‘only a handful to munch as I go along.’ For I knew that in the state of the market my penny might have purchased a gallon. I desired to show a neglect for small sums.
“To my surprise the fruit-seller stared at me and said:
“ ‘Dates? From what country do you come that you ask for *dates* in our town?’
“ ‘Why!’ said I, ‘is there not a glut of these? I am told the place is overflowing with them.’
“ ‘There is One-who-judges,’ said the fruit-seller resignedly. ‘But as for dates﻿—you will not find one in the whole town; our last month’s arrival was pillaged by robbers in the hills.

===Chinese===

这虽是个寒酸价码，’他说着，又是一阵沉痛叹息，‘但总归比血本无归分文不值要强上千百倍啊。’
“老夫心中的如释重负委实难以言表。我方才分明已望见自己的货物无人问津烂在手里，或是被贱卖得连渣都不剩。优素福向老夫开出的生路，将老夫从万念俱灰的绝境硬生生拽回了一线生机；尽管这一刀割得皮开肉绽，好歹留下了供老夫东山再起的盘缠本钱。
“真正的伟人在做买卖时自有一种大度干脆。优素福刚一瞧见老夫感激涕零并毫不迟疑地笑纳了他这份恩赐（老夫除了视其为恩赐断无其他称谓），这位贵胄般的豪杰当即击掌唤人，传令召来管账总管，当场向老夫如数清点了一百枚（100）纯金金币。老夫向他立下了交货字据，他随手递给另一名奴仆，低声耳语交代了几句。随后他拉着老夫海阔天空畅聊起来，执意留老夫在府中盘桓至深夜。事实上直到东方既白黎明将至，这位如今被老夫视若知己、且令老夫发自肺腑愿结草衔环结下金兰厚谊的大恩人，方才恩准老夫走出王府大门重返客栈。
“回到客栈，老夫发现自个儿驮队上的大箩筐早被搬运一空，里面的货物已尽数送入了买主的仓库。商界巨擘在雷厉风行经办买卖的同时兼具这般仁爱菩萨心肠，委实令老夫钦佩得五体投地。
---
“次日拂晓，趁着骄阳尚未肆虐，老夫漫步穿过巴扎；非但没有因割肉巨亏而垂头丧气，一想到自个儿怀里实打实揣着一百枚（100）黄澄澄的纯金金币，心头反倒涌起一阵莫名的快慰。
“老夫带着几分戏谑的快意凑到了一处水果摊前，掷下一枚小铜钱，恳请掌柜施舍一把椰枣。
“‘不必秤满分量，’我说道，‘随便抓一把容老夫沿街解馋便可。’因为老夫深悉依眼下的市面，老夫这枚铜板原本足以买下一大桶。老夫无非想摆出一副视钱财如粪土的大气。
“岂料水果掌柜瞪圆了眼珠，像看怪物一样死死盯着老夫，嚷道：
“‘椰枣？你究竟打哪个蛮荒国度冒出来的，竟敢跑到咱们城里来讨“椰枣”吃？’
“‘哎呀！’我故作诧异道，‘市面上不是椰枣成灾泛滥成灾吗？人家说全城如今漫山遍野全淌着椰枣呀！’
“‘冥冥自有公道，’水果掌柜认命般叹息道，‘可至于椰枣嘛——你翻遍全城也休想找出一颗来！咱们城里上个月新运抵的一大批货，在山里全叫土匪给洗劫一空了！

===Original===

If you will but procure me a single gallon I will readily give in return two pieces of gold, so great is the demand. Of supply there is none whatever, nor, alas! any prospect of such.’
“I was so bewildered that I hardly know what next I said, but at any rate, in reply to it, my new acquaintance told me that there were, indeed, *suspected* to be certain dates in the possession of Yusouff-the-Triumphant, ‘who’ (he remarked aside) ‘has all the luck.’ He next said it was also rumoured that Yusouff’s slaves had been seen in the last hours of the night going in procession with a great number of panniers laden on mules towards Yusouff’s warehouse, and those who brought the news swore that they could smell the smell of dates.
“ ‘But beyond that smell,’ he ended, ‘we have had nothing of dates in the place for three weeks. And if you understood our habit in the matter of food you would feel for us!’
“I have already described to you, my dear nephews, my admiration for Yusouff-the-Triumphant. Long before I had seen him his distant reputation had inflamed me. My brief acquaintance with him had exalted that feeling to what I had thought the highest pitch. But now it passed all bounds. A man so subtle in negotiations! So ready in affairs! So rapid and conclusive in a bargain! With so marvellous a command of feature and of tone! A man (in a word) so infinitely my superior in that profession of commerce to which Allah calls all great souls and in which I also was engaged! Such a man I had never thought to meet! Nay﻿—I had never thought such a one to exist upon this poor earth. I could have kissed the ground upon which he walked or have borne upon me forever, as a relic, some thread of his purse.
“ ‘Here,’ I exclaimed, ‘is the true merchant! Here is the model of all that a man of affairs should be! Oh! Mahmoud, you thought yourself something in your trade, but you have met your master, and more than your master! You have met one who is to you as the most holy of saintly men is to the basest of the Kafir. There is *none* on earth like him. Allah has raised him beyond all others.
“But it is not enough, my dear nephews,” continued the old man, whose eyes were now filled with a sort of sacred light, “it is not enough to admire those who set us great examples. We should also imitate them.

===Chinese===

只要你肯给老夫弄来区区一加仑，老夫甘愿当场奉送两枚（2）足色金币，市面上的饥渴早已到了这般地步！如今市面上根本无货可供，唉，甚至连半点进货的指望都瞧不见！’
“老夫听得目瞪口呆，恍惚间甚至不知自个儿随后搭了句什么话；不过无论老夫说了什么，新相识的掌柜顺口答复道：坊间确实‘暗中怀疑’有若干椰枣落入了胜利者优素福的私邸，‘那厮，’（掌柜忿忿地嘟囔了一句），‘天底下的鸿运全叫他一人占尽了。’他接着透露说，坊间甚至风传就在后半夜三更天，有人撞见优素福府上的奴仆大队，赶着大批骡马驮着无数大箩筐浩浩荡荡开进优素福的私家仓库，报信的人赌咒发誓说，隔着老远都能嗅到扑鼻而来的浓烈椰枣香。
“‘然而除了那股虚无缥缈的香气，’掌柜总结道，‘全城百姓已经足足三周（3）不知椰枣是何滋味了！倘若你晓得咱们本地人在吃食上的癖好，你便能体会咱们有多煎熬了！’
“我亲爱的侄儿们，老夫适才已向你们铺陈过老夫对胜利者优素福的景仰之情。早在未睹真容之前，他远扬的威名便令老夫热血沸腾。昨夜短暂的盘桓，更是将那份崇敬推上了老夫原以为的最高峰。然而此刻，老夫的敬仰彻底决堤崩溃！何等深不可测的谈判机变！何等神鬼莫测的商海手腕！何等雷厉风行、一锤定音的定案魄力！举手投足乃至神色语调的拿捏是何等炉火纯青、出神入化！一言以蔽之，在这门全赖真主感召一切伟岸灵魂并令老夫投身其中的经商神圣大业里，那人不知高出老夫几万个境界！这等旷古烁今的商界神明，老夫生平做梦都未曾料想过能亲眼得见！不——老夫原本以为这污浊凡尘断不可能诞育出这等神仙人物！老夫恨不得跪下亲吻他踏足过的泥土，抑或将他钱袋上脱落的一缕丝线充当圣髑永世佩戴在胸口！
“‘瞧瞧！’我狂呼道，‘这才是名副其实的大商贾！这才是所有商海中人当之无愧的万世楷模！啊！马哈茂德，你从前自以为在行当里算号人物，如今你可撞见真正的祖师爷了，撞见比祖师爷还要高绝万倍的真神了！他在你面前，宛如最圣洁的先贤对照最卑贱的异教徒！全天下断无第二人能望其项背，真主已将他擢升到了俯瞰众生的绝顶峰峦！’
“然而光是膜拜崇敬还远远不够，我亲爱的侄儿们，”老人的眼中此刻闪烁着某种圣洁的狂热，“光是仰慕那些为我们树立崇高榜样的先贤是远远不够的。我们还必须亦步亦趋去效仿他们！

===Original===

I determined after so rare an experience to follow as best I might in the footsteps of one who had shown himself raised high above the level of mortality.
“ ‘Him,’ said I to myself, ‘him will I copy! *He* shall be my guide! *His* manner and his tone, on that unforgettable evening, shall be my exact model! Then perhaps in time I shall do as he has done and accumulate so great a store of money as shall put me among the greatest of mankind.’
“I hastened to summon my slaves. I paid my score for the stabling, and as I looked at my small capital and surveyed my beasts I hesitated what I should do. Yusouff-the-Triumphant had, by God’s special grace overshadowing him, got hold of my substance. Nothing was left me but the camels. In such a strait I abandoned the thought of men and turned at once to heaven. I lifted up my heart to my Maker and prayed for guidance. He that has never for very long abandoned His servant answered my prayer with singular alacrity, for even as I prayed I heard two men who passed me muttering one to the other.
“The first, as they hurried along, was saying in fearful undertones:
“ ‘They have not yet a camel among them! Yet camels they must have or the terrible sentence will be pronounced!’
“ ‘Yes!’ returned his companion in a horrified whisper, ‘I fear greatly for my relatives in that town, and I am proceeding there to make certain that they shall have at least *one* camel in so terrible a time! For if a sufficiency of camels is not there by tomorrow noon I hear they are all to be impaled!’
“So speaking in subdued accents of terror, little knowing they were overheard, they walked on while I followed and noted every word.
“My mind was immediately made up. I continued, with stealthy feet, to follow these two anxious beings who were so engrossed in the coming misfortunes of their native place. At last, when we had come to an empty space where three streets met, I caught them up and faced them. Accosting them I said:
“ ‘Sirs, are you bound for such and such a place?’ (naming a town of which they could never have heard﻿—for indeed it did not exist).
“They stopped and looked at me in surprise.
“ ‘No, sir,’ they answered me together, ‘we are bound in all haste for our native place which is threatened with a great calamity.

===Chinese===

经历过这等千载难逢的神迹造化之后，我立下铁石心肠，决意竭尽所能去追随那位已然超凡入圣的巨擘的足迹。
“‘他！’老夫暗自发誓，‘老夫唯独要模仿他！他便是老夫的引路明灯！他在那难忘之夜的一颦一笑、举手投足与语气腔调，便是老夫刻骨铭心的唯一模板！终有一日，老夫亦能如法炮制，积攒下如山似海的泼天财富，跻身人间最显赫的豪门之列！’
“我火速召集手下的奴仆脚夫，结清了车马店的草料脚钱；凝视着手里仅剩的这把薄本、盘点着眼前的牲口，我暗自权衡下一步的算度。胜利者优素福承蒙真主特殊洪福垂青，夺走了老夫的全部细软，老夫手里只剩下这几峰骆驼了。在这走投无路的关头，我彻底摒弃了向凡人求告的念头，径直求助于上苍。我向造物主倾诉衷肠，谦卑地祈求神明降下明示。那位从不曾长久抛弃仆人的神明，以奇绝迅捷的速度回应了我的祈愿——因为就在我祷告之际，忽听迎面匆匆走过的两个汉子正交头接耳窃窃私语。
“其中一人神色惊惶，压低嗓门颤抖着说道：
“‘他们至今连一峰骆驼都没弄到手！可他们非弄到不可，否则那道灭顶严旨就要开斩了！’
“‘可不是嘛！’他的同伴以惊骇绝望的耳语附和道，‘我真替镇里的亲戚捏一把汗；我正十万火急赶回去，好歹得确保他们在这大难临头的节骨眼上至少能凑出一峰骆驼来！听说倘若到了明儿晌午凑不齐额定的骆驼，全镇男女老少通通都要被活活插死在木桩上！’
“他们这般用恐惧压抑的哭腔窃窃私语，浑然不知老夫在后头将每一个字全听在了耳中。
“老夫当机立断！我迈开幽灵般的轻捷步子，暗中尾随着这两位满心忧虑、全然沉浸在家乡浩劫中的倒霉蛋。最后，行至一处三条街巷交汇的空旷死角，我快步追上前去，横身拦在他们跟前。我主动搭话道：
“‘二位仁兄，敢问可是要奔赴某某宝地？’（老夫随口胡诌了一个他们闻所未闻的城名——因为世间压根没有这处地方）。
“他们驻足止步，满脸狐疑诧异地端详着我。
“‘不是的，客官，’他们异口同声答道，‘我们正十万火急赶回老家去，敝乡大难临头大祸临头了！

===Original===

Its name is Mawur, but, alas, it is far distant from us﻿—a matter of some twenty leagues﻿—the desert lies between, and we shall hardly reach it within the day that remains. For we are poor men, and only with fast *camels*’ (at this word they glanced at each other and shuddered) ‘could the journey be accomplished in the time.’
“I thanked them politely, regretted that I had disturbed them for so little, proceeded with the utmost haste to my caravan, inquired the road for Mawur (the track for which lay plain through the scrub and across the sand), and hastened with the utmost dispatch all that burning day and all the succeeding night without repose, until at dawn I passed with my exhausted train through the gates of the city. I had covered in twenty hours twice as many leagues.
“Five of my beasts I left upon the road; and some few of my slaves﻿—how many I had not yet counted﻿—had fallen out and would presumably die in the desert. But there was a good remainder.
“Unfortunately I was not alone in my venture, for I discovered that early as was the hour another man had arrived already with two camels and was standing with them under the dawn in the marketplace. Poor beasts they were, and bearing every mark of fatigue. But I was determined upon a monopoly. I had hoped from the conversation I had overheard that not a single camel would be present in the place. I would secure myself against even the slightest competition. I approached the leader of the two sorry camels and asked him there and then what he would take for his cattle. He stared at me for a moment, but to my astonishment when I offered him for a beginning the derisory price of ten pieces of gold, he accepted at once, put the coins into his pouch, smiled evilly, and moved off at a great pace.
“To my chagrin there approached within a very few moments yet another peasant, leading this time but one camel, a rather finer beast than the others. I hoped, I believed, he would be the last. I made haste to follow the same tactics with him as with the first. Like the first he took the five gold pieces without so much as bargaining, but he looked me up and down strangely before shrugging his shoulders and taking himself off hastily down a side lane.
“And then (the people beginning to drift into the street as the day rose) appeared a man leading not less than ten camels in a file.

===Chinese===

敝乡名叫马乌尔（Mawur），可唉，离这儿路途遥远得很——足足隔着二十里格（20）的漫漫黄沙——中间横亘着荒漠，只剩剩下的一天工夫，咱们插翅也难飞回去啊！咱们全是穷苦百姓，唯独骑上飞毛腿骆驼’（提及此字，二人对视一眼，浑身打了个激灵）‘方能赶在死期之前赶回！’
“老夫客客气气向他们谢过，为无端叨扰二位深表歉意；随后脚底生风疾奔回自个儿的商队，打探清楚去往马乌尔的路径（穿过戈壁与荒漠的沙丘羊肠小道清晰可见）；随后老夫押运着商队连轴狂奔，顶着白昼的酷烈烈日与漫漫长夜，中途不曾合眼歇息哪怕一刻钟，直到破晓时分，老夫带着疲惫不堪的队伍，风驰电掣般杀进了城门。在短短二十个小时（20）里，老夫疾驰了足足双倍的程途。
“沿途跑死了五头（5）牲口；几个奴仆——我手忙脚乱尚未来得及清点人数——半路掉队掉队倒毙在戈壁沙海里。所幸大队人马依然健在。
“遗憾的是，老夫并非这桩豪赌里的独行侠；因为破晓时分老夫赫然发现，已有另一个汉子捷足先登牵着两峰（2）骆驼抵境，正伫立在晨曦微光下的集市广场上。那是两头精疲力竭、满脸病容的可怜畜生。然而老夫铁了心要搞‘独家垄断’！从适才偷听的对话中，我本指望城中连半峰骆驼都休想撞见。老夫必须把哪怕最微不足道的竞争对手扼杀在摇篮里！老夫快步走向那两头病驼的主人，当场开价要将牲口全盘买断。他惊愕地瞪着老夫；教我目瞪口呆的是，当老夫试探性地抛出十枚（10）金币的嘲弄贱价时，他竟毫不迟疑一口答应下来，将金币揣进怀里，嘴角泛起一抹诡异险恶的冷笑，拔腿飞奔溜之大吉。
“叫老夫暗自肉疼的是，不出片刻，又有一名乡下汉子牵着骆驼赶来，这回只牵了一峰，成色比先前两头略显健硕。老夫暗自期盼、甚至笃定他定是最后一个漏网之鱼。我赶忙施展出一模一样的战术；同前一个汉子一样，他连讨价还价的工夫都懒得费，痛痛快快接下了五枚（5）金币，却用一种极其古怪异样的眼神将老夫从头到脚打量了一番，耸了耸肩，随后一溜烟拐进小巷溜得无影无踪。
“紧接着（随着天色大亮，市井百姓纷纷涌上街头），迎面竟又走来一个汉子，身后串联牵引着足足不下十峰（10）骆驼！

===Original===

I was seriously alarmed, but I bethought me of my reading: how all great fortunes had been acquired by speculation, how caution and other petty virtues were the bane of true trade. I boldly approached him and offered him my remaining gold for the whole bunch. Instead of meeting my offer with a higher claim, he asked to look narrowly at the pieces, and then looked as narrowly into my face. He took one of the gold pieces and bit it. He stooped and rang it upon the cobblestones. He determined apparently that it was good, and without another word took my gold, appealed to those around us as witnesses to the transaction, handed me the leading cord, and with a burst of laughter ran off at top speed.
“Here, then, was I with my thirteen new camels and what was left of my original caravan. I will not deny that I was somewhat disturbed in mind; but I could only trust in Allah. I did so with the utmost fervour, and implored Him to consider His servant, and to see to it that not another camel should reach the town before I began to sell.
“But what is man? What is he that he should order the movements of the Most High?
“I lifted up my eyes and saw approaching down the narrowness of the street a file of certainly not less than one hundred camels led by a great company of ragged men and walking with that insolent and foolish air which this beast affects and which at such a moment provoked me to rage.
“Then a slave, trembling lest he should give me offence, bade me come apart with him where steps led up the city wall. These I climbed, and from the summit I saw a sight that broke my heart.
“For there, across the plain that surrounded the city, came such a mass of camels as I hardly thought the universe contained. They came in batches of twenty, fifty, two hundred, herds and flocks of camels, driven, led, ridden, conducted in every shape from one direction and another, through the desert and cultivated land, from track and path, a very foison and cataract of camels. It was as though all the camels of Arabia, India, Bactria, and Syria had been summoned to this one place.
“And, alas, so they had! or at least as many as the king of that region could command.﻿ ﻿…
“For this was the explanation.﻿ ﻿…”
Here the old man’s eyes grew dim with tears, his voice faltered, and in spite of his present riches he broke down at the recollection of his past ill-fortune.

===Chinese===

老夫当真吓出了一身冷汗；但我旋即省起典籍中的圣训：全天下的巨额家产无一不是靠孤注一掷的投机豪赌攫取的，谨小慎微这类妇人之仁的庸俗美德向来是经商大道的绊脚石！老夫昂然阔步走上前去，掏出兜里仅存的全部金币，要将整群骆驼一网打尽。那汉子并未狮子大开口追加要价，而是要求把金币凑在眼前死死查验，随后又以同样死盯的眼神审视着老夫的面孔。他拾起一枚金币用牙咬了咬；又俯身将金币在石板路上摔得当啷作响。他显然断定真金无误，随后二话不说收下金子，大声呼唤周遭围观的市井百姓为这桩买卖作证；将牵引绳一把塞进老夫手里，随即爆发出一阵震耳欲聋的狂笑，拔腿以百米冲刺的狂奔逃之夭夭。
“这下可好，老夫手里坐拥十三峰（13）新购进的骆驼，外加老夫原本残留的驼队。老夫不能昧着良心说自个儿心里没有半点发毛；可眼下老夫唯独能寄托的只有真主。老夫以至诚至热的心志虔诚祈祷，苦苦哀求真主垂怜他的仆人，务必保佑在老夫开市发售之前，全城再也不要有半峰骆驼进关！
“然而凡夫俗子算什么东西？他凭什么胆敢对至高主宰的造化指手画脚？
“老夫抬眼一瞧，赫然望见逼仄的街巷深处，正缓缓蠕动开来一整队足足不下上一百峰（100）骆驼的庞大驼队，由大群衣衫褴褛的汉子牵引着，迈着这种畜生特有的傲慢而愚蠢的碎步踱步而来——在那个节骨眼上，这副死样直教老夫气破了肝胆！
“此时一名奴仆唯恐激怒老夫，战战兢兢拉扯着老夫的衣襟，引老夫登上通往城墙的石阶。老夫攀上墙头，极目远眺，眼前呈现的一幕活活撕碎了老夫的心肝！
“天呐！环抱城池的茫茫平原上，正漫山遍野蠕动着一片老夫做梦也未曾料想全宇宙竟能装得下的浩瀚骆驼狂潮！它们二十峰（20）一伙、五十峰（50）一群、两百峰（200）一队；成群结队的驼群，有的被驱赶，有的被牵引，有的被骑乘，从四面八方、穿越荒漠与田园、沿着大道与羊肠小径，宛如决堤的山洪飞瀑般铺天盖地汹涌扑来！那阵势宛若全阿拉伯、全印度、全大夏以及全叙利亚境内的全部骆驼，一夜之间全被征召集结到了这一座孤城之中！
“唉，事实果真如此！至少是彼邦君王所能号令的一切骆驼，尽数被赶到了此地！……
“因为事情的原委真相乃是这般……！”
说到这里，老人的双目被泪水浸得一片模糊，语声哽咽；即便坐拥眼下的万贯家财，一追忆起当年那桩惨绝人寰的横祸，他依然忍不住老泪纵横。

===Original===

“Oh, my dear nephews,” he said in broken accents, “hardly will you believe the magnitude of my misfortune! For it turned out, as I eagerly questioned the people of the place, that a war having broken out against their king on account of the Date Prohibition of which I have told you, that ruthless monarch had ordered them to collect as best they might so many thousands of camels to be present within the walls by noon of that day, or suffer massacre. If the full tale were not present every man, woman, and child would be killed. For he had been suddenly alarmed by this declaration of war and caught with an insufficient provision of sumpter beasts. His Emirs had advised him that his salvation lay in seizing without payment every beast for leagues around.
“In proportion as my soul sank so did the hearts of the townsmen rise, to see the number gradually fulfilled. By noon all was well for them﻿—but very ill for me! The officers of the king arrived, the beasts were counted and set apart, with not an ounce of copper to pay for any one of them! All seized! And my poor herd, alone and in that vast multitude, suffered the fate of all the rest, and, what was worse, every one of my slaves﻿—all were taken off to serve as drivers.
“There in a far land, alone, I stood, with not a gold piece left in my pouch and not a head of cattle to my name; once more quite destitute.
“I spent the remainder of that day debating whether to hang myself on a beam or throw myself from a minaret. The arguments in favour of either course were so evenly balanced that the sun set before I could decide between them, and even at sunset there appeared, through the Mercy of Allah, a new relief.”
“There did?” said the second of the nephews eagerly, but before his uncle could reply the intolerable noise of the muezzin was heard and the boys, rising at the signal, bowed low to their uncle and were gone.

===Chinese===

“呜呼，我亲爱的侄儿们，”他用断断续续的悲咽腔调倾诉道，“你们无论如何也难以想象老夫当年遭受的厄运有多惨烈！老夫急火攻心抓着当地百姓盘问，方才得知原来由于老夫适才向你们提及的《严禁椰枣令》，彼邦同邻国彻底爆发了大战！那位残暴不仁的国君传下雷霆血旨：勒令全城百姓不惜一切代价，必须在今日正午之前将数以千计的战驼全部征集入城，违者满门抄斩！倘若正午额度短少半峰，全城男女老幼皆要被就地屠戮殆尽！盖因那位君王被突如其来的宣战吓破了胆，偏偏军中辎重驮畜严重匮乏。麾下的埃米尔们向他进谗献策，说唯一的救驾生路，便是将方圆数十里格境内的每一头牲口分文不掏全盘强征充公！
“随着老夫的心一步步坠入无底深渊，城中百姓的心却随着额度的一点点凑齐而欢呼雀跃。到了正午时分，全城百姓皆逃过了死劫——唯独老夫迎来了灭顶之灾！君王的官军如狼似虎地杀到，当场盘点并圈禁了所有的牲口，连半文铜板都没赔付！全盘抄没充公！而老夫那群可怜巴巴的牲口，在这茫茫驼海之中，亦毫无悬念地同所有骆驼一道沦为了军资；更惨烈的是，老夫手下的所有奴仆脚夫——通通被官军强行抓去充当了军中马夫！
“老夫孑然一身伫立在遥远的异域荒原，钱袋里连一枚金币都没剩下，名下连半头牲畜的踪影都瞧不见；再一次，老夫彻底沦为了赤贫如洗的穷光蛋！
“在那一天的余下时光里，老夫苦苦纠结究竟是该寻一根房梁悬梁自尽，还是该从宣礼塔顶纵身跃下一了百了。两条死路的优劣是如此难分高下，以致日落西山老夫依然未曾决断停当；然而就在落日熔金的刹那，承蒙真主的仁慈（Mercy of Allah），一道崭新的曙光再次神迹般降临了！”
“当真降临了？”二侄急不可耐地追问；然而不等叔父答话，宣礼塔上那不容推卸的凄厉呼拜声已然刺破长空。少年们闻声而起，向叔父深深鞠了一躬，悄然退出了房间。

## IV *Al-Kantara*, or “The Bridge” / 第四章 桥
===Original===

When the hour of public executions had arrived and the boys were assembled once more at their uncle’s feet to hear the story of his fortunes, (their minds full of his last success), the old man, still occupied with that pleasing memory, began at once the continuation of his life.
“I left the valley, as I told you, my dear nephews, nourished by the memory of a whole people’s gratitude and giving thanks to God who had made me the humble instrument of so great a good. They err who think that great wealth is marked with oppression, or that the rich man has despoiled the populace. Upon the contrary, the fortunes of the wealthy are but an index of what excellent work they have done for all; and I, for my part, equally joined in my heart the memory of all the benefits I had conferred upon my kind in the matter of pipkins, and my overflowing satisfaction at the heavy bags of coin which swayed upon the backs of my camels.
“Day after day we proceeded, my caravan and I, through the high hills, pitching our camp each evening by some wooded torrent side and nourishing ourselves with the provisions with which I had amply stored my company at my departure.
“Such scenes were solemn and inclined the mind to reverence. Never had my prayers been more sincere and deep than they were during the long watches I passed in the cloudless nights of those mountains, in the solemnity of their vast woods; and the holy thoughts of grateful affluence harmonized with the ceaseless voices of the forest.
“I had during this long journey through the barrier range but little opportunity to exercise those gifts in which I may humbly say I excel. For these villages were few and poor and the opportunities for talent were rare. It was indeed my duty to keep my hand in, as the saying goes, and not to let my wealth diminish as I passed. Thus I would, for mere practice, strike some little bargain from time to time. I would purchase obsolete arms from some village less backward than the rest and sell them at some further stage onward to rude mountaineers who had not even heard of such ancient weapons. I was not above offering to carry, as my caravan passed, sundry goods from one farm to another at an agreed price, and these (after selecting what from among them seemed to me best worth keeping) I would punctually deliver to their consignees.

===Chinese===

当闹市公开行刑的时辰再次到来，男孩子们又一次围坐在叔父脚边聆听他的发家史（他们脑海中依旧充斥着他上一次的辉煌战绩）。老人依旧沉浸在那段惬意的回忆之中，旋即接着讲述他的人生际遇。
“我亲爱的侄儿们，正如我跟你们讲过的那样，我怀揣着整整一地百姓感恩戴德的温馨记忆离开了那个山谷，由衷感谢真主让我成为成就如此丰功伟业的谦卑器具。那些以为泼天富贵必定伴随着压迫剥削、或是富翁全凭搜刮民脂民膏起家的人，可就大错特错了。恰恰相反，富豪的万贯家私，不过是他们为大众做出了何等卓越贡献的精确标尺；至于我自己，一面在心中深深铭记着自己在陶锅生意上施予同胞的无尽福祉，一面看着骆驼背上沉甸甸摇晃的金钱皮袋，心中亦充满了无以复加的满足与欣慰。
“我和我的驼队日复一日地在巍峨崇山峻岭间跋涉前行。每到傍晚，我们便在林木葱茏的溪涧水畔安营扎寨，享用着我出发前为随行人员备足的丰美干粮。
“那般的山川胜景何等庄严肃穆，最能令人心生敬畏虔诚。在那些崇山峻岭万里无云的漫漫长夜里，在浩瀚莽林的沉静幽穆之中，我献上的祷告从未像那时那般真挚而深沉；感念丰裕恩典的圣洁思绪，同林海永不停歇的涛声交融在一起，何等和谐动人。
“在这段穿越屏障山脉的漫长旅途中，我几乎找不到施展自身非凡才干的机会——尽管我很谦虚地说，老夫确实天资异禀。山间的村落稀少贫瘠，任由才华驰骋的良机实在凤毛麟角。不过俗话说得好，技艺不可荒废，老夫的本分就是时刻活动手脚，绝不能让手中的财富在行进中缩水。因此，纯粹为了练手，我时不时也会做点小买卖。比如从某个不那么闭塞落后的村庄收购一批淘汰过时的旧兵器，翻过几个山头后再转手卖给连此类古老兵刃都闻所未闻的粗鲁山民。在驼队路过各处农庄时，只要价钱公道，老夫甚至也不嫌麻烦答应替他们代捎各色杂物——当然，在老夫仔细筛选并留下了其中最值钱的物件之后，其余货物都会准时无误地送达收主手中。”

===Original===

“I amused myself at my leisure, also, when I was in no haste, with occasional experiments in engineering such as suit the more educated man among his fellows. Thus I would let the water out from a dam as I passed it and then, at a considerable price, repair the ravages the escaping water might have made in the valley below. And I was even agreeable to retrace my steps and repair the damage which the flood had inevitably occasioned to the barrier itself: charging a suitable sum for both operations.
“Sometimes﻿—when the occasion offered﻿—I did business on a somewhat larger scale. I remember purchasing a whole train of wheat which was on its way to one of the larger hamlets, and when I arrived there keeping the people in some suspense (but not to the point of actual famine) until their necessity very naturally produced an excellent price for the grain. I also negotiated ransoms from time to time upon commission when I found myself in a district of brigands﻿—simple folk﻿—and I picked up some very curious carvings and pieces of metal work at a price satisfying to their rude owners yet promising an enormous profit when I should reach the plains.
“But all these were mere jests and pastimes, the occupation of enforced idleness as my long journey through the hills continued. At last I came to a place which had been described to me by a trusty servant, where, from the height of a pass I saw some thousands of feet below me the foothills descending rapidly on to an even plain which stretched, brown and sunburned, to the horizon. Not far from the base of the mountains, at the edge of this plain, a noble river wandered in many branches, separated by sandbanks; for I had been seven weeks in the hills and it was now the height of summer, the snows had long since melted away on the heights, and the stream was low.
“I pitched my last camp a mile or two from the hither bank of this great river, and sent forward certain of my servants to discover how best it might be crossed.

===Chinese===

“老夫在闲暇不慌不忙之时，也自得其乐地搞搞小工程试验，这类消遣极合乎高人一等的饱学之士的身份。比方说，老夫路过水坝时会把蓄水放出来，随后再收取一笔可观的酬劳，去修缮奔涌宣泄的洪水在下游山谷里冲出的灾患。老夫甚至乐意再折返回去，修补洪水不可避免对水坝本身造成的损毁：两笔工程各自收取一笔体面的佣金。
“有时——只要时机成熟——老夫也会做点规模稍大些的买卖。老夫记得有一次买下了正运往某座较大村落的整队小麦；待老夫赶抵那处村庄后，故意让村里的百姓在惶惶不安中煎熬了数日（当然绝不到闹饥荒的程度），直至他们迫在眉睫的饥渴极其自然地推高了粮价，卖出了极为优厚的大价钱。路经土匪强盗盘踞的村落地界时——那群山贼倒都是些淳朴人——老夫还会偶尔充当中介赚取赎金佣金；老夫还以令那些粗鄙蛮人欣喜若狂的价格，搜罗收买了一批极其稀奇罕见的雕刻与金工器物，只要老夫抵达平原，这些物什必能带来难以估量的巨额暴利。
“然而这一切终究不过是旅途中的小玩笑和消遣罢了，是穿越崇山峻岭的漫长旅途中用来打发无聊时光的营生。最终，老夫来到了一个忠仆曾向老夫描述过的关隘。从那高耸的山口极目远眺，只见数千英尺下方的山麓丘陵急剧延展，铺展成一片褐色且被烈日晒得焦灼的辽阔平原，一直绵延至天际线。就在距山脚不远的平原地带，一条壮丽的大河蜿蜒分出无数支流，被道道沙洲阻隔；因为老夫在山中足足行进了7周（7），此时正值盛夏，峰峦高处的积雪早已消融殆尽，河水水位正低。
“老夫在距这条大河南岸约莫一两英里的地方扎下最后一座营寨，随即派出几名得力仆役前去探路，查明究竟该如何渡河最为妥当。”

===Original===

They returned the next morning and told me that in several of its branches it was too deep to be forded, but at the place where the shores seemed to approach each other (where there was no interrupting islands, but one continuous sea of water four furlongs wide), a ferry had been established from a road-end and plied regularly for the passage of merchants, pilgrims and other travellers who there went over from the hills to the Kingdom of the Plain upon the further bank. I sent them back with an appointment for the ferry to be prepared to take my numerous caravan from the first hour after sunrise on the morrow. We packed all our gear, struck camp in the first dusk of dawn and duly reached the ferry head where a large flat boat manned with a dozen rowers captained by the old ferryman of the place waited us on a sort of wharf.
“The passage was tedious, and would take the whole day; for the stream was swift and no more than one camel could cross at a time.
“I was in some little hesitation how to act. If I remained upon the hither bank until all had passed over I could not be certain that my servants who had gone ahead would not play me a trick. If I crossed first I could not overlook the doings of my servants who had yet to cross; and though I had no reason to doubt their perfect honesty, neither had I any reason to doubt their vile thievish character. At last I made the following plan: I discharged all the camels of their packs, putting the packs on board in one heap, being very careful to put on board all the food as well as the coin. With this and one camel which I attended myself and hobbled, I crossed alone. I then went back again with the ferryman and his crew, still keeping my provisions and my coin, and brought over another camel and his driver, and so on until the whole of my company was transferred. Not till all the camels and their drivers were assembled, clamouring with hunger, upon the further bank, did I allow the coin and food to be landed under my very eye.
“The time which all this took made my retinue ravenously hungry, as I have said, and as the day wore on I was indeed touched by the earnest prayers they made for a little food, but I was too wise to yield, and it was not until the whole of my company was gathered together on the further bank, and I with it, that I permitted the cases to be landed and gave them all a hearty meal.

===Chinese===

“翌日清晨仆役们折返回来禀报，说那大河的数条支流皆水深难测，根本无法涉水渡过；唯独在两岸逼近对峙的狭窄处（那里没有散碎沙洲阻隔，而是一片宽达4弗隆（4）的平阔水域），有一处官道尽头的渡口，定期有渡船往返摆渡，供翻山越岭前往对岸平原王国的商贾、香客及各色过客通行。老夫立刻打发仆役回去定下渡期，命渡口自翌日日出一小时后便备好大船，接引老夫庞大的驼队渡河。我们收拾好行装铺盖，在晨光熹微的破晓时分拔营启程，准时赶到了渡口码头；只见一条巨大的平底大木船正停靠在码头边，由一位老艄公统领着12名（12）精壮水手严阵以待。
“渡河的过程繁琐冗长，非耗上一整天不可；盖因水流湍急，每次横渡只能搭载1头骆驼。
“老夫一时之间颇费踌躇，不知该如何处置最为稳妥。倘若老夫留在南岸督促，直到所有人马全部渡尽，老夫实在不敢保证先渡过去的仆役会不会在对岸耍弄花招；倘若老夫率先渡河，老夫又无法监视尚未渡河的仆仆人等；尽管老夫没有由头怀疑他们是十足的正人君子，但老夫同样没有理由怀疑他们骨子里天生卑劣的贼性。深思熟虑之后，老夫定下了如下万全之策：老夫命人将所有骆驼背上的行囊全部卸下，堆聚在船舱中央，尤其打起十二分小心，将所有干粮与金银细软悉数装船。老夫亲自押运这些财物，外加一头由老夫亲自看管并绑住蹄子的骆驼，率先独自渡河。随后老夫同艄公及水手们乘船折返，依然紧紧守着盘缠与口粮，再运过另一头骆驼及其脚夫；如此反反复复，直至整支队伍悉数渡河完毕。直到所有的骆驼与脚夫全都饥肠辘辘、吵嚷喧天聚集在对岸之后，老夫才准许在老夫的严密监视下将钱箱和粮食搬下船来。
“整套折腾耗去了大量工夫，使得老夫的随从们正如老夫所言那般，饿得前胸贴后背。随着日头偏西，他们苦苦哀求施舍一口充饥干粮的真切模样，确实令老夫为之动容；但老夫深谙世事，老谋深算，绝不轻言退让。直到全队人马安然齐聚在对岸，老夫亦立足于彼岸之后，老夫方才下令将食盒卸船，赏赐大家饱餐了一顿丰盛大餐。”

===Original===

“It was by that time near sunset. We pitched our camp and waited till the morning to find a more regular habitation, for I had noticed a very little way off from the further bank, and somewhat upstream, not a few scattered houses standing in gardens and shaded in a grove of trees.
“I had as yet no plan how I might use the sums of which I was possessed. I was rather waiting for a venture to come to me than going out myself to seek it, when a chance word from the old ferryman, as I paid him the fares (which I had already contracted for at a great reduction, seeing how numerous we were), started me upon a train of thought.
“And here, my nephews, I will beg you to observe that any hint of opportunity must be seized at once. It is thus that great things are done.
“What the ferryman said was, ‘A curse on those who come so loaded’ (for he grumbled and contended that his old crazy craft might have sprung a leak under such a pressure of traffic).
“ ‘Yet,’ said I in reply, ‘you have no lack of custom. As it is this day’s business has left many disappointed, and I see upon the further bank the fires of those who have been kept waiting the whole day. They will be a hundred or more to claim your services by morning.’
“ ‘That is true,’ he answered, ‘but luckily few come as loaded as you or with so many beasts. This is none the less a good place of traffic, for it is the only passage across the water for many miles up and down stream, and serves the main road through the kingdom.’
“I asked him why had he not thought to meet the pressure by purchasing larger boats, or more of them, and hiring more men; since it was clear there was profit in the place, and a greater demand of travellers than he could accommodate.
“He answered again in the surly tone which people use when they boast of changeless custom, that the old boat had been good enough for his father and had served him all his own life, and was good enough for him. By this reply I saw that he was without the funds for replacing his old boat by more and better craft. This my discovery was the beginning of all that followed.
“Before striking my camp the next day I first put the old ferryman in a reasonable humour by giving him good food and drink and treating him honestly in my conversation. When I saw that he was in a mood to be approached I suggested that we should enter a kind of partnership.

===Chinese===

“待忙完这一切，已是日落黄昏。我们扎下营盘，静候翌日清晨再去寻觅一处正经的落脚寓所；因老夫老早便留意到，在对岸不远处稍偏上游的地方，一片郁郁葱葱的树林掩映着不少坐落在庭院之中的零散房舍。
“彼时老夫心中尚无定数，该如何动用手中积攒的这笔巨资。老夫宁可静待良机自己找上门来，也不愿盲目出门乱撞。就在老夫给那老艄公结算摆渡钱时（因老夫一行人马众多，老夫已然将价钱狠狠砍去了一大半），老艄公无意间的一句牢骚，骤然点醒了老夫的心智。
“在此，我亲爱的侄儿们，老夫务必恳请你们谨记：商海中任何一丝机遇的微光，都必须如鹰隼扑食般当机立断！唯有如此方能成就惊天伟业。
“那老艄公嘴里嘟囔的是：‘该死的，哪个天杀的带了这么多辎重！’（他一边骂骂咧咧，一边埋怨说他那条破烂老船在如此重负往返之下，险些被折腾得散架漏水）。
“老夫闻言应道：‘可是，老人家，你的买卖可从来不缺客源啊。你看今日的客流，还有大批人被耽搁了渡不了河；老夫瞧见对岸岸边点着一簇簇篝火，全是苦等了一整天的过客。到了明日一早，排队等你的怕不是得有100多人（100）。’
“‘这话倒是不假，’艄公答道，‘不过好在很少有人像你这般带了这么多货和牲口。此地确实是条生财宝道，因为上下游数十里之内唯有此处能够渡河，这里掐着通往王国腹地的主官道呢。’
“老夫便问他，既然此地买卖如此兴隆，过往客商的需求远超他的运力，他为何从未想过购置更大、更多的船只，多雇佣些水手来承接这泼天的生意呢？
“他再次用那种守旧之人在吹嘘固步自封的陈规时惯用的生硬腔调答道，说这条老船当年他爹用得挺顺手，他自个儿使唤了一辈子也足矣，对他而言早已绰绰有余。听到这番回答，老夫心如明镜：他手里根本没有替换老船、添置更多优质船只的本钱。老夫这一洞察，便是一切后续奇迹的开端。
“翌日清晨在拔营起行之前，老夫先用一顿上好的酒饭款待了老艄公，并在交谈中以诚相待，把他的脾性顺得服服帖帖。当老夫看出他已然放下戒备、可以切入正题时，老夫向他提议：我们二人何不合伙搭档做一笔买卖。”

===Original===

“ ‘I am,’ said I, ‘quite at my leisure. I am under no need to go forward until I choose. I have thought of hiring someone of these habitations which I see in yonder grove, and of making a long sojourn here, for the perpetual spectacle of all this traffic crossing and re-crossing a great river under the mountains is a delight.’
“The old ferryman answered that he needed no partner, that he earned all that he needed by his trade and that he preferred to be alone. He also said that my foreign face was distasteful to him, and that grand people were often less trustworthy than they seemed.
“ ‘Your sentiments,’ I answered, ‘are a proof of your wisdom, and also do you honour. But has it not occurred to you that if in the place of this one old craft half a dozen good new boats, much larger and properly manned, were provided, more comers would be tempted to pass here, there would be less delay, both the volume of traffic and the pace would be increased? I cannot but think it an excellent proposition.’
“I have found, my dear nephews, that obstinate old men are easier to shepherd into financial schemes than any other sort: nor was I here disappointed.
“The old mule made the admission which all such men make after the first conventional delays. He said: ‘That is all very well, but who is to pay for them?’
“I replied quietly that I would. ‘I shall be delighted,’ said I, ‘to furnish half a dozen new boats and to pay for the men to row them until the new turnover begins. All I ask is that you shall still keep your present earnings, but share with me in equal amounts the new and extra earnings which my plan will almost certainly produce.’
“It took me some time to rub into his rusty head the terms of my very favourable proposal. He kept on mixing up the division of any future profits and the division of his present income. Never did I appreciate more than during my conversations with this stupid granfer the necessity for patience in spreading a commercial snare. I was at fearful pains to get the thing into his obtuse brain. He *could* be no poorer, for I asked nothing of his present earnings: he *might* be much richer, for he would have half any future additions. I would guarantee him the income he was already earning on condition that the much larger income to be earned by my methods should, over and above that guaranteed revenue of his, be equally divided between us.

===Chinese===

“‘老夫我，’老夫对他说道，‘眼下闲暇得很，若非老夫自愿，老夫绝不急于启程赶路。老夫琢磨着在远处那片树林里相中一处宅院租下，在此长住些时日；盖因看着山脚下这滔滔大河两岸川流不息、往返渡河的人潮盛景，实在是人生一大赏心乐事。’
“那老艄公答复说，他不需要什么合伙人，他靠这门手艺挣的钱足够糊口，而且他更喜欢独来独往。他还直言不讳地说，老夫异邦人的面孔让他看着不顺眼，而且那些衣冠楚楚的大人物往往比表面上看起来更靠不住。
“‘老人家，你的见解，’老夫答道，‘恰恰证明了你的睿智，也令人肃然起敬。然而难道你就从未思量过：倘若能用半打（6只）崭新气派、吃水更深、水手齐备的大渡船，来替换这艘破旧的老船，必能吸引更多客商取道此地渡河；如此一来不仅省却了漫长延误，往来客流量与摆渡航速亦将成倍飞涨？老夫思前想后，这委实是一桩不世出的绝妙买卖。’
“我亲爱的侄儿们，老夫历经风雨，早已看穿：比起其他任何人，那些顽固不化的老顽固反而最容易被诱入精巧的金融棋局之中；在这位老艄公身上，老夫同样未曾落空。
“这头老倔驴在经历了开头的虚与委蛇之后，终于吐露了所有此类顽固之徒终究会问的那句话。他瓮声瓮气地说：‘说得倒轻巧，可谁来掏这笔买船造船的银子？’
“老夫气定神闲地答道：老夫来掏。‘老夫不胜荣幸，’老夫说道，‘自愿出资添置半打新船，并支付水手的全部工钱，直到新航线的营运流水运转起来为止。老夫所求的全部条件，仅是老人家你依然独享现有的全部收益；至于老夫这套妙计几乎铁定会创造出来的全新额外收益，则由你老人家与老夫二人平分。’
“老夫颇费了一番口舌，才把这极为优厚的合伙章程灌进他生锈的榆木脑壳里。他脑子里始终把未来的新增分红同他眼下的固有进账混为一谈。在同这个蠢笨如牛的老汉周旋的长谈中，老夫无比深刻地领悟到：在布置商业罗网之时，耐心究竟是何等不可或缺的美德！为了让他那不开窍的脑筋彻底明白，老夫真可谓煞费苦心。他‘绝无可能’遭受任何损失，因为老夫对他眼下的收入秋毫未犯；他‘极有可能’发大财，因为未来多赚的每一文钱他都能白得一半。老夫向他担保他原本的全部进项分文不少，前提是由老夫的妙策催生出的更为庞大的增量收益，在扣除他那笔保底收入后，由老夫与他各得其半。”

===Original===

“He still seemed to think that there was some flaw or catch somewhere. He wanted the thing, simple as it was, explained to him over and over again. At last he got it clear; he got by heart and repeated the refrain: ‘Cannot be poorer, may be richer.’ Nor did it occur to him to wonder why I was so oddly generous.
“I had our contract drawn up in due form, witnessed and sealed. I then caused to be constructed by the local shipwrights four first-class flat boats, some ten *diram* long by five wide. I saw to it that they should be painted in gay colours and in general have that vulgar violence so attractive to the masses. On their completion I added them to the existing capital of the ferry line.
“At the same time I pointed out to him who was now my partner something which that same stupidity of his had made him miss, to wit, that as he had a monopoly his charges were far too low. ‘Moreover,’ said I, ‘when you consider what fine new boats I have put into service and how, as a consequence, the stream of traffic is increasing, to neglect the opportunity of profit is a great sin for which you will be answerable on the Day of Resurrection. Why, it was but yesterday that you passed over twice as many people, you assured me, as ever you did in any other one day in your life!’
“So wedded to custom was the old gentleman that he still hesitated, but remembering how right I had been in my innovation and unable to contest the evidence of his own eyes how from day to day the volume of traffic increased, he at last somewhat reluctantly consented. The fares were doubled, yet the applications of people desiring to cross the river grew no less. There arose a substantial profit, over and above the old ferryman’s original income, to be divided between us, and judged by the cost of the new boats I was making some ten percent upon my money, a very reasonable profit under the circumstances.﻿ ﻿…”
Here Mahmoud the great merchant, paused, shut his eyes for a few moments, and continued in a murmur. “A very reasonable profit. Ten percent, a very reasonable profit.” Then, suddenly opening his eyes fiercely, he fixed them upon his alarmed nephews and cried.
“Was it not strange for a man of my temperament to remain thus pottering with a few boats and leaving sacks full of coin unused? You have only heard the beginning of the scheme upon which I was engaged!

===Chinese===

“他心里似乎依然认定其中必定藏着什么圈套或猫腻。这桩事情分明再浅显不过，他却非要老夫翻来覆去解释个没完。最后，他总算彻底咂摸明白了；他把一句口诀烂熟于心、喃喃念叨：‘只赚不赔，越变越富。’他压根就没动过脑子去琢磨，为何老夫会如此不可思议地大发善心。
“老夫命人依律拟定了正式契约，由中人画押见证并加盖了印信。随后，老夫雇佣当地船坞工匠打造了4艘（4）头等平底大船，长约10迪拉姆（10），宽5迪拉姆（5）。老夫特意吩咐将船身漆上鲜艳夺目的彩画，通体散发出一种庸俗而具冲击力的奢华气派，最能迎合底层凡夫俗子的胃口。大船告竣之日，老夫便将它们悉数并入了渡口现有的资产之中。
“与此同时，老夫向如今已是老夫合伙人的艄公指出了一个因他愚钝而忽略的事实：既然他在此地坐拥独家专营权，他定下的摆渡价码委实低得可怜。‘况且，’老夫说道，‘当你看到老夫投入了这等威武的新船运营，由此招徕得往返客流滚滚翻倍，倘若白白放过这敛财暴富的良机，可是天理难容的大罪过，到了复活日你都要在真主面前吃不了兜着走！哎呀，就在昨日，你亲口向老夫保证，昨日摆渡的客流足足是你有生以来任何一天的两倍有余！’
“这位上了年纪的老爷子被祖制旧例禁锢得太深，一时之间依旧犹疑不决；但他亲眼见证了老夫革新之举的何等英明，且在与日俱增的滔滔客流这一铁证面前无从辩驳，最终颇为勉强地首肯了。摆渡票价当即翻了一番，然而求渡过河的客商却丝毫未见减少。在老艄公原有的保底收入之外，迅速涌现出一笔极为丰厚的纯利由我二人瓜分；以新船的造价成本折算，老夫这笔投资的年回报率达到了10%（10%）左右，在此类境遇之下，已然算得上一笔极为体面划算的收益了……’
讲到此处，大商人马哈茂德微微顿住，合上双目小憩片刻，口中低声喃喃自语：‘极为体面的收益。10%，极为体面的收益。’紧接着，他猛然凶悍地睁开双眼，目光如炬地逼视着惊恐万状的侄儿们，厉声喝道：
‘像老夫这般雄才大略之人，整日守着几条破船小打小闹，却将一袋袋真金白银闲置在旁，这岂非咄咄怪事？你们适才听闻的，不过是老夫所布惊天大局的序幕罢了！’”

===Original===

“I had already purchased a very nice little property with a convenient house upon it, standing some yards back from the bank of the river and perhaps one hundred yards above the ferry.
“I next purchased a field upon the further bank, exactly opposite this house and its garden. I amused myself sometimes by rowing across the river from the steps at the foot of my ground to the field which I had purchased upon the other side. I sowed that field with beans of a particular kind with which (so I assured my neighbours), I was experimenting after an agricultural fashion. They were much interested, for agriculture is highly developed in that part, with the result that the highest arts, especially those of finance, are shamefully neglected.
“I allowed a few months to pass, during which the use of the ferry under my improved methods had more than trebled. It attracted to itself, now that the passage was so much easier, forms of traffic which it had hitherto not known. I even added to the fleet one huge pontoon for the special service of an elephant which we had warning was to pass, and when this was known, those great animals, which had previously used a ford several days upstream, were attracted to the shorter mountain road by the ferry.
“When all this was so prosperously established, I informed a few of the friends I had made in the neighbourhood that I must indulge in the fancy of a rich man and amuse myself by throwing a bridge between my house and the field I had bought upon the other shore. ‘It will save me,’ said I, ‘the perpetual trouble of rowing across in my little skiff and also occupy my leisure; for I am something of an engineer.’
“In truth very little engineering was required. All I had to do was to drive strong piles at intervals into the stream, lay trestles upon them, stay them with large baulks upon either side, and so make a good working bridge. It was no more than fit for foot traffic, but for this it was very convenient.
“Having now this communication I bought more land upon the further bank and developed there a very nice little model farm. I will not deny that foot passengers would occasionally ask my leave to cross by the bridge in order to save them the tedious passage by water.

===Chinese===

“老夫早已在岸边购置了一处极为称心如意的小巧庄园，配有一栋舒适宜人的宅院，坐落在大河岸边后退数码之处，约莫位于老渡口上游100码（100）远。
“紧接着，老夫又在大河对岸、恰好正对着老夫宅邸与后花园的正对面，买下了一整片田地。老夫有时自得其乐，从自家院落脚下的石阶解开小舢板，划桨横穿大河前往老夫在对岸买下的田亩。老夫在那片地里播种了一种特殊品种的豆子；老夫向街坊邻里信誓旦旦地宣称，老夫纯粹是在以农学家的严谨姿态搞农业试验。邻里街坊对此极感兴趣，因彼处的农耕技艺高度发达，其结果便是世间最顶尖的高雅艺术——尤其是金融这门至高无上的艺术——反倒被可耻地彻底荒废了。
“老夫任由光阴流逝了数月；在此期间，在老夫先进运营理念的调教之下，老渡口的航运流水足足翻了三倍有余。如今渡河既已如此便捷舒坦，渡口自然吸引来了先前从未涉足此地的各色商贾驼马。老夫甚至向船队中增添了一艘专供大象摆渡的巨型浮船（因我们事先接到了大象过境的信报）；而当这一消息不胫而走之后，那些原先不得不绕道上游数日路程处寻找浅滩涉水的庞然大物，也纷纷被渡口这条近捷的翻山商道吸引了过来。
“待到这一切买卖皆风生水起、固若金汤之时，老夫便向在当地结识的几位挚友吹风，声称老夫打算放纵一下富人的闲情逸致，纯粹为了自娱自乐，在老夫的宅院与对岸买下的田地之间架设一座桥梁。‘如此一来，’老夫说道，‘既免教老夫整日划着小舢板往返受累，又能消磨老夫的闲暇时光；盖因老夫在工程营建上多少也算个行家。’
“实则此工程压根无需多高深的技艺。老夫所要做的一切，不过是隔着一段距离往河床里深深打下坚固的木桩，桩顶架设排木栈桥，两侧用粗大巨木斜撑加固，如此便筑成了一座极实用的过河木桥。此桥虽说仅容行人徒步通过，但就步行而言却极为宽敞便利。
“既然两岸有了这等便捷的通道，老夫索性在对岸收纳了更多田产，将其营建为一处极其标致的模范农庄。老夫并不否认，不时会有过路行人向老夫求个人情，恳请老夫恩准他们从木桥穿行，以省去乘船摆渡的繁琐冗长之苦。”

===Original===

These I always refused lest it should prejudice the interests of my friend the ferryman; I made an exception only for one or two neighbours whom I desired to favour, and occasionally for really important people with whom the ferryman would not desire to quarrel. But I have a good heart, and at last I began to wink at the use of the bridge by more than these. Children especially (for I am very fond of young people) I could not bear to condemn to the troublesome passage by ferry, and I gave orders to my people to allow their trespass.
“At last a regular path got established through my farm, and whether from slackness or generosity I know not, but I allowed the crossing of the river by my bridge to increase in volume and to become a daily practice. When it had reached a certain volume my detestation of disorder compelled me to make certain regulations. I put up a gate at either end and charged a purely nominal sum which went, as I pointed out, to the upkeep of the bridge; though, of course, it did not nearly meet that expenditure.
“To avoid the length and inconvenience of the passage by water this toll was cheerfully paid, and as the season advanced my bridge was more and more used.
“My partner, the old ferryman, saw all this with a confused eye. He had the sense to see that I would not hurt my own investment by competition, yet he could not but perceive that there was here an increasing rivalry to his own long-established route.
“At last he approached me and asked me if we could not come to some pact; I said that I saw no occasion for that. There was plenty of room for both. I was a wealthy man, and an act of generosity was a kind of luxury for me; I could hardly ask people who had now grown used to so easy a passage to go back to the monopoly of the boats with their primitive, slow and clumsy business of embarking and disembarking, and their necessary delays and crowding. I pointed out to my revered partner that the boats were still necessary for all heavy merchandise and for animals, and I also pointed out very strongly to the ferryman what he could not deny, that I would hardly do anything to prejudice him since that would be also to prejudice myself, as I was his partner. I even ridiculed him for not perceiving the force of such an argument before coming to me, and for troubling me with what was obviously nonsense.
“He still grumbled, however.

===Chinese===

“对这些请求老夫向来是严词拒绝的，免得损害了老夫那位艄公朋友的商业利益；老夫唯独对一两位老夫格外器重的近邻网开一面，偶尔也会放行几个身份显赫、连艄公自个儿也不愿开罪的达官贵人。然而老夫终究生就一副菩萨心肠，久而久之，老夫便开始对更多人借道木桥的行径睁一只眼闭一只眼了。尤其是那些天真无邪的稚童（老夫向来格外怜惜后生晚辈），老夫委实不忍心看着他们饱受舟车颠簸的摆渡之苦，便吩咐手下奴仆对他们的擅自穿行姑息放行。
“日积月累，老夫的农庄之中竟被硬生生踏出了一条常行的踩道；究竟是出于老夫的疏懒放任，还是出于老夫的宽宏仁慈，老夫亦说不清楚，反正老夫听凭借道木桥渡河的客流与日俱增，渐渐演变为司空见惯的日常之举。待到客流量积聚到一定规模之时，老夫骨子里对无序与混乱的深恶痛绝，迫使老夫不得不立下规矩章程。老夫在木桥两端各设立了一道闸门，并象征性地收取一笔微不足道的铜板；老夫向大伙讲得明明白白：这笔钱纯粹用于木桥的日常修缮与维护——尽管平心而论，这点碎银子根本填不上桥梁养护开支的牙缝。
“为了省去水路摆渡的漫长耗时与诸多不便，过往行人皆兴高采烈、毫不犹豫地缴付了这笔过桥钱；而随着季节更替，踏上老夫木桥的人潮亦愈发汹涌繁密。
“老夫的合伙人——那位老艄公——看在眼里，心里却翻江倒海、百思不得其解。他依稀能辨出老夫断然不会通过自我竞争来砸自己的招牌，然而他又不能不眼睁睁看着这道木桥正对他经营经年的老航线构成日甚一日的致命分流。
“终于，他找上门来，试探着询问老夫是否能跟他达成某种互不侵犯的和解盟约。老夫坦言：老夫瞧不出有何立约的必要。天地广阔，足容我二人各展所长。老夫身为腰缠万贯的巨室，行善积德对老夫而言本身就是一种至高无上的精神享受；老夫岂能狠下心肠，要求那些早已习惯了如此平坦捷径的乡亲百姓，重新折返回去忍受那些陈旧小木船的专横垄断，以及上下船时的愚钝迟缓、狼狈拥挤与无穷无尽的延误？老夫向老夫那位可敬的合伙人条分缕析：大船依然是不可或缺的，所有沉重的骡马大货与大牲畜非得靠他的渡船不可；老夫更严正地提醒他——这是一句他无论如何也无法辩驳的铁理：老夫身为他的合法搭档，怎么可能会去干损人不利己、砸自身买卖饭碗的蠢事呢？老夫甚至嘲弄他，怪他在找老夫理论之前为何竟未能看清这般浅显的逻辑，反倒跑来拿显而易见的无稽之谈叨扰老夫清净。
“然而，他依然嘟嘟囔囔、心怀不满。”

===Original===

He said that he was no scholar, that it sounded all right, but that he did not feel comfortable. I answered that I could not help his feelings, but it was a plain matter of common sense, and so dismissed him.
“I then announced my intention of strengthening the bridge considerably and making it sufficient to support any kind of traffic. And so I did, at a very considerable expense. When I had completed the task it was a fine structure which would take every kind of beast of burden and vehicle, and a constant stream of foot-passengers. The only exception I made was for elephants, which animal (I said) I might allow later, but not until I had had the whole thing thoroughly tested. These beasts, therefore, still had to use the ferry: but as they were few in number and difficult to handle they only increased my partner’s troubles.
“Meanwhile the fame of my bridge spread throughout all the neighbouring countries, it gathered upon itself the whole volume of commerce.
“The old ferryman came to me in a mixed mood of anger, panic and delirious complaint. He said that his revenue was falling with alarming rapidity, added (a little spitefully I thought) that my share of that revenue would be not a quarter of what it had been in the past year, and said very plainly that if I did not make some change in my regulations my own profit would disappear altogether: that nothing would be left but his original revenue and that even this was now in doubt. As I answered nothing to all this long plea but let him talk himself out he ended up by asking, with some irony, whether I was one of those rich fools who liked to throw away their money.
“Then it was that I answered him as he deserved to be answered, for I do not easily brook insult. I told him that I had mortgaged my share in the enterprise of the boats sometime before to a neighbour at a very good price before ever the bridge had appeared, that I was sufficiently pestered by this man who ascribed to me the continued decline in the revenue which I received and handed over to him, and that I would not have added to this perpetual annoyance the further complaint of my inept partner. I drove him from my presence and told him I desired never to see him again.

===Chinese===

“他辩称自己是个目不识丁的粗人，老夫的话听上去头头是道，但他心里头就是老大不自在。老夫回敬他说老夫管不着他心里自不自在，世间万事全凭常理公道断夺，随即便将他打发了出去。
“紧接着，老夫公开宣称：老夫打算下血本将木桥进行全方位加固升级，使其足以承受世间任何车辆马匹的川流往返。老夫言出必行，当真不惜工本砸下了滔天巨资。待大工告竣，一座雄浑巍峨的壮丽桥梁横空出世，足以任由世间各类驮畜车辆通行无阻，桥上行人人流更是川流不息、昼夜不绝。老夫定下的唯一豁免例外是大象；老夫宣称大象日后或许亦可登桥，但前提是必须由老夫对整座桥梁的承重极限进行彻头彻尾的严格勘验之后方可放行。因此，那些巨象依旧只能去雇佣渡船摆渡；但因大象稀少零星、又极难伺候，这一网开一面非但没能拯救老艄公，反倒给他平添了无穷无尽的折腾与麻烦。
“与此同此，老夫这座宏伟石木大桥的威名响彻了周边四邻八邦，它犹如磁石一般，将方圆百里的全部商贸巨流尽数吸附到了自己脚下。
“那老艄公再次跌跌撞撞奔到老夫跟前，神情介于暴怒、绝望与疯癫呓语之间。他哭诉说他的摆渡进项正以骇人听闻的暴跌速度断崖跳水；他还带着几分老夫听来颇显尖酸的恶意补充道：老夫从渡船生意里分得的红利，如今连去年同期的四分之一都不到；他直截了当地威胁说，倘若老夫不立刻改弦更张修改大桥章程，老夫自己的投资红利亦将荡然无存——到头来只剩下他原先的那笔保底老本，而如今甚至连那笔保底钱都岌岌可危了！由于老夫自始至终冷眼旁观、任由他把满腹怨气倒个底朝天而不置一词，他最后竟语带讥刺地质问老夫：老夫莫非是个嫌钱多没处扔、专爱挥金如土的阔佬蠢货？
“听到此处，老夫便以他应得的口吻结结实实地教训了他一番，因老夫向来绝不容忍宵小的无端侮辱。老夫冷冷告知他：早在木桥初现端倪之前，老夫便已将手中那份渡船生意的合伙干股以极为丰厚的优厚高价抵押转让给了某位近邻；那位近邻整日为着由老夫经手转交、却日益缩水的利润分成而将老夫纠缠得苦不堪言，老夫早已不胜其烦，绝无可能再容忍老夫这位无能搭档跑来无理取闹！老夫厉声将他轰出了大门，勒令他这辈子休要再出现在老夫眼前。”

===Original===

“I have no doubt that if I had been approached properly I would have made some sort of compensation to the neighbour to whom I had mortgaged at a fine figure my original share in the profits of the ferry. I had enjoyed a large sum which he could now never recover, and I might have let him have a fifth or a quarter of it back, merely as a piece of generosity. But when I discovered that he had himself resold his interest to an ignoramus who was at that moment trying to find a purchaser for his rapidly shrinking property I lost all patience with the combination of them and put every thought of the ferry out of my mind. The new purchaser foreclosed on his mortgage and got for the ferry one-third of what he had lent on it.
“It was shortly after this transaction that the old ferryman went mad. It began by his coming to my house daily and making scenes outside the doors. Then he took to breaking the windows, and at last to gathering crowds and haranguing them on his imaginary persecution at my hands. I was compelled to have him locked up in his own defence, and I am glad to say that a merciful fever soon relieved him of what had become incurable delusions. He did not recover his sanity, however, as is so often the case, even in the last few hours before death. He continued to call me the most dreadful names, and to rave, in his mania of persecution, shouting that he had been robbed and ruined. It was a pitiful ending to what had long been a useful if obscure life.
“As I could not bear to see the men whom he had employed starve I took them into my own employ for the making of a roadway to the bridge, for the further strengthening of it, the painting of it and so on, and sent all the ferryboats down the river where they would be of more use than at this part where by my enterprise and public spirit the bridge had come into existence. I purchased them as old timber from the owners and made an insignificant profit of some few thousand dinars.
“It is a pretty example of the way in which names cling to places that the point on the bank where the ferry used to ply is still called ‘The Madman’s Grave.’ For, indeed, the old fellow was buried, I heard, by his own request, close to where his boat used to ply.
“It was now high time to consider the whole question of the bridge and its finances.

===Chinese===

“老夫毫不怀疑，倘若那位接盘老夫渡船抵押干股的乡邻当初好言好语前来求告，老夫或许看在情面上多少会赏赐他几分赔偿补偿。老夫当初从他手里笑纳了一大笔白银，而如今这笔钱他已彻底无望回本；纯粹出于老夫天性里的豪爽仁慈，老夫原本甚至乐意退还他五分之一乃至四分之一的银钱。然而当老夫获悉：这厮竟转手又将那份权益兜售给了一个一窍不通的外行笨蛋，而那个倒霉蛋此刻正像热锅上的蚂蚁一般四处寻觅买家以接盘这处飞速缩水的破烂产业时，老夫对这帮鼠目寸光之徒的合谋彻底失去了所有耐心，当即把关于摆渡渡船的每一丝念头从脑海中彻底抹得干干净净。那位接盘的后生最终行使了抵押罚没权，将整个渡口船队强行拍卖，收回的银子还不及他当初借出款项的三分之一。
“就在这笔交易告罄后不久，老艄公便彻底疯了。起初是他每日天不亮就跑到老夫府邸门前撒泼打滚、大吵大闹。紧接着他发展到砸老夫宅邸的窗玻璃，到了最后竟聚众叫嚣，对着围观的人群痛陈他在老夫手下遭受的‘莫须有迫害’。为了保护他自身性命周全，老夫不得不下令将他妥善收押拘禁；而老夫十分欣慰地告诉你们：一场慈悲的高热很快便将他从这不可救药的疯狂幻觉中解脱了出来。然而正如许多疯癫之徒那般，哪怕在临终咽气的最后几个时辰里，他亦未曾恢复神智清明。他嘴里不停地用人世间最恶毒的污言秽语咒骂老夫，在迫害妄想的狂乱中嘶声尖叫，嚎叫着说自己被老夫敲骨吸髓、落得倾家荡产。一个大半生虽说卑微暗淡、却也算本分有用之人，最终竟落得如此凄惨的下场，委实令人扼腕叹息。
