“至于老夫自个儿，老夫亦绝非一毛不拔的守财奴。老夫当场认捐100枚金币（100）巨款，用于在新城核心枢纽营建一座宏伟巍峨的崭新清真寺，条件是必须另有99名（99）善长仁翁跟进捐出同等数额；老夫更出资兴建了一座面向全城市民的公共文娱大戏楼，其入场门票收缴的铜板仅勉强够支付日常清扫保洁、公税、供暖照明、老夫自个儿慷慨大笔认购的债券利息、堂倌仆役、文墨文秘杂支、美化陈设、引道修葺、固定资产年度折旧摊销，以及至多6%至8%（6、8）的微薄纯利。老夫甚至还设立了公办慈善施粥大厨房，贫苦百姓在彼处能以略高于市价的‘亲民价’买到热食；这处善堂对本城治安捕快衙门发挥了不可估量的无上威能，因衙役们在此设立了一处天字第一号的核心情报哨卡，能够时刻精准追踪老夫那些不那么走运的贫民邻里的日常一举一动。老夫还无私捐赠了一批带有厚重白镴水瓢的公共饮水马槽喷泉，水瓢皆用坚固沉重的铁链牢牢焊死在花岗岩基座上以防刁民偷盗；老夫甚至仁至义尽到了这般境地：老夫免费向全天下公开印发了新城规划图纸，上面清晰无误地标明了尚待出租放盘的地块明细及其承租条款。
“老夫立下了一条铁律：凡是在老夫土地上破土建房之人，皆须立字为据承诺在20年（20）大限届满之后，将整座房屋无偿奉送归还给老夫名下；然而考虑到大批百姓囊中羞涩无力自建家园，老夫大发慈悲设立了一支专项借贷基金，准许他们以寻常市井利率外加各类不可避免的杂费、手续费、折扣抽水等项借支现银。在方方面面，老夫皆在全力深耕并泽被老夫亲手缔造的这座崭新城邦！”

===Original===

“I had my reward in the profound respect and honour paid me by my fellow-citizens. These were convened by the council at an appropriate date to decide what recognition should be made of my services. They finally agreed, after long discussion and many very eloquent discourses, upon an illuminated parchment, which was presented to me with the most flattering cheers and songs upon the public square of the new quarter, in a tent of purple silk specially voted for the occasion and later claimed as a perquisite by my butler.
“I replied in a suitable manner to the acclamations of the crowd and the kindly flattery of the councillors; but I told them, at the end of my address, that I should feel ungrateful indeed if, upon such an occasion, a certain humble fellow-worker of mine were overlooked in the public rejoicings. Thereupon I extended my hand to the enthusiast, that young engineer of parts whom I had so fortunately met some months before and whom I had arranged should be near the steps of my dais at the required moment. I handed him up. I smiled benignantly upon him as he blushed with happy shame and pleasure. I even set him at my side.
“ ‘It is all very well, my friends,’ said I, as I concluded my little speech, ‘to speak as you do of the foresight and business acumen, organizing power, and the rest of it, which﻿—I hope justly﻿—you ascribe to me when you tell me how, as with a magic wand, I raised all this new city from the marsh which preceded it. But what would such gifts be had they not been aided and supplemented by talents no less essential, such as those which we all admire in this young friend at my right? He it is who has performed, sometimes in a very literal sense, the spadework. His has been the hard, obscure, constant labour and vigilance, without which my own more conspicuous efforts would have been in vain!’
“After a few subdued cheers from the assembly, most of whom had never heard of the young man, while the rest had forgotten him, all dispersed, and I was free to seek repose in my own new and sumptuous house.
“I am glad to say that this public mention of my worthy young colleague was not all I did for him.

===Chinese===

“老夫的一片苦心，最终在全体城邦百姓对老夫由衷的无上尊崇与顶礼膜拜中斩获了丰盛的回报！在良辰吉日，评议会郑重召集全城元老集会，研商究竟该以何等显赫规格奖掖表彰老夫立下的旷世功勋。在经历了冗长激烈的闭门推演与无数篇辞藻华丽的赞颂演说之后，元老院诸公最终全票达成决议：向老夫赐予一轴用五彩泥金精心彩绘的泥金羊皮纪念诏书；颁奖大典在新城宽阔的中央广场隆重举行，在山呼海啸般的雷鸣喝彩与赞美诗歌的伴奏下，老夫在一座特意为盛典拨款搭建的紫绸大帐之中笑纳了这一殊荣——那顶奢华大帐日后被老夫的管家顺理成章当作私家小费福利私吞充公。
“老夫面对满城如潮民众的狂热欢呼与元老议员们的谄媚逢迎，作出了极尽得体庄重的致谢答辞；然而在演讲的终局篇章，老夫语重心长地告知满城同僚：倘若在如此盛大的全民狂欢吉日，老夫身旁一位卑微勤勉的同袍战友却被盛典遗忘在角落，老夫内心委实将深感忘恩负义与无地自容！言毕，老夫主动向那位狂热信徒伸出了温暖的臂膀——正是数月前老夫有幸在荒野偶遇的那位才华横溢的年轻工程师小友；老夫事先早已吩咐小厮安排他侍立在老夫宣讲高台的石阶身旁。老夫将他一把拉上高台。看着他因狂喜、羞怯与幸福而涨得通红的面庞，老夫向他投去了菩萨般慈祥仁厚的和蔼微笑，甚至将他亲切地拉至老夫身侧并肩而立。
“‘我亲爱的同胞街坊们，’老夫在演说的尾声庄严宣告，‘诸公适才对老夫高瞻远瞩的战略眼光、商业魄力、组织才干诸如此类由衷赞美——老夫唯愿老夫当真当得起诸公口中仿佛凭一柄魔杖便从恶臭沼泽中变出一座崭新大都市的奇迹美誉。然而倘若没有另一项同样至关重要的绝顶才干相辅相成，老夫所有的天赋宏图又将何处安放？正如我们此刻在老夫身侧这位年轻挚友身上所共同钦佩的才智那般！正是他，在无数个日日夜夜里躬身力行完成了最纯粹意义上的破土拓荒！正是他那坚韧不拔、默默无闻、夙夜匪懈的苦干操劳与警醒值守，才使得老夫那些更为显赫瞩目的宏伟蓝图未曾沦为空谈泡影！’
“台下零星传来几声有气无力的附和喝彩——盖因满场万民大半从未听说过这后生的名字，而剩下的人早已将他忘得一干二净；盛典随即散场，老夫亦得以从容步入老夫修葺一新的巍峨豪宅安歇将息。
“老夫十分欣慰地告诉你们：老夫在盛典之上对这位可敬同僚的公开隆重表彰，绝非老夫施予他的全部恩泽。”

===Original===

As the agreed salary which I paid him by our contract would now soon expire, I arranged with the council that he should have a permanent post as keeper of the public squares, at a wage more than double that of the gardeners, and be granted (on condition of good behaviour) a limited pension when he should reach his seventieth year, the same to be deducted in small weekly sums from his pay: which sums, as he was not yet thirty-two would accumulate to much more than was necessary and leave over and above his retiring stipend a balance for anything the council might think useful. He was also lent, rent free, a small four-roomed house with a nice strip of front garden and a wooden shed at the side. His duties occupied him from a little before sunrise to the pleasant dusk of eve, with an hour off for meals and a fortnight’s holiday in the autumn.
“Even his little sister was not forgotten. I obtained for her, from my friends among the religious authorities (notably the mufti, who was most strenuous in her cause) the post of head cleaner at the new mosque. Her salary there was necessarily somewhat smaller than her brother’s, nor had she any holidays, while her hours were a trifle longer. But, on the other hand, she had no responsibilities.
“Shortly after all this I determined to sell my holding in this new property and to betake myself to other mercantile adventures in further lands. I had been in this place more than a year. I had made very good friends. It was the scene of a success greater than any I had yet experienced. Nevertheless I felt I could remain there no longer. The field was too small for my expanding opportunities. There was nothing left to take.
“I announced, therefore, my intentions to realize, and allowed a certain interval for a public decision upon the purchase of my land, and leases, and other interests.
“A curious discussion arose. One party, composed mainly of wealthy but intelligent young men, of university professors and of jailbirds, were insistent that the town council should buy all my land and the city possess it for the future; for it was obviously wrong (they agreed) that improvements in land and houses should go to private individuals. The other party, which was made up almost entirely of builders and auctioneers, furiously opposed this scheme which (they said) struck at the roots of all morals and family life.

===Chinese===

“鉴于依据我们当初签署的合伙文书由老夫按期向他支付的包干薪饷即将期满届满，老夫亲自出面同城邦评议会达成斡旋协议：在新城管委会为他安排了一份掌管全市公共花园广场巡查的铁饭碗差事，薪俸待遇足足是寻常花匠工人工钱的翻倍有余；并破格恩准他在洁身自好、恪尽职守的前提下，在他年满70岁（70）高龄之时按月领取一份定额退休年金——该笔年金由公库按周从他的日常薪水里扣缴微薄碎银建立专户；鉴于他彼时年方不足32岁（32），这笔长达数十年的扣缴复利滚存下来，不仅远超他安度晚年所需的实际数额，扣除其退休俸禄之后富余的庞大沉淀结余，更可任由市政评议会自由调拨用于任何公家认为有益的事业。官府还免费借给他一栋带4间房（4）、前带一小片花园、侧带一间木棚的幽雅小屋居住。他的日常差事不过是自日出前片刻忙碌至日落黄昏的美好时刻，午间有一小时饱餐歇息工夫，入秋后更有长达半个月（14）的带薪秋假。
“甚至连他那个幼妹亦未曾被遗忘。老夫凭借在城中宗教司法界泰斗（尤其是大穆夫提法官诸公，他在女娃的前程上表现得最是卖力热心）中的通天交情，在新落成的宏伟清真寺里替她谋得了一份首席清洁洒扫的清闲差事。由于性别与资历之故，她的工钱薪资理所当然比其胞兄略显微薄，亦没有半天休假，每日当差的时辰亦略显冗长。然而话又说回来，她身上压根无需承担半点差池责任。
“在办妥上述一切善后之后不久，老夫便毅然决然决定将老夫手中持有的全部新城地盘权益一次性打包变现，起驾奔赴更辽阔的异邦去开辟崭新的商海传奇！老夫在此地已客居了逾一年（1）有余的光阴。老夫结识了满城生死至交。此地乃是老夫平生所铸就的辉煌伟业之巅峰。然而老夫心中深知：老夫绝不可在此久留。这座城邦的舞台对于老夫日渐膨胀的野心与机遇而言委实太狭小了。在这片热土之上，已然再无任何值得老夫掠夺收割之物了。
“于是，老夫公开宣称了老夫意欲全盘套现离场的宏愿，并宽限了一段充裕的缓冲期，以待城邦公决究竟该由何方神圣接盘出资买断老夫名下的全部土地、租约及各项核心权益。
“一场极其离奇古怪的旷世大辩论旋即在城中爆发：其中一个阵营，通体由一批出身豪门却满脑子开明理念的热血青年、太学饱学鸿儒以及刑满释放的死囚文痞组成，他们极力敦促城邦评议会必须斥巨资将老夫手中的全部土地全盘回购，使之自此永久归属于城邦全民所有；盖因他们咬定：土地与房屋营建所带来的增值溢价，断不可任由市井私人中饱私囊！而另一个针锋相对的阵营，则几乎全盘由泥瓦建筑承包商与房产拍卖行掮客结盟组成，他们对这一倡议发起了雷霆万钧般的疯狂阻击与声讨，痛斥这种公有化构想无异于动摇了人世间一切伦理纲常与家庭秩序的立国之本！”

===Original===

*These* stoutly maintained that, in the natural scheme of Providence, all should be parcelled out among the highest bidders.
“For my part I was, like the great mass of the taxpayers, indifferent to either argument. All that interested me was the obvious fact that in the competition between these two groups on the council the value of my property necessarily rose.
“At last the first party prevailed, the city bought me out (really a most interesting social experiment!), and I received the sum of two million pieces of gold.”
“Two million pieces!” shouted the astonished little nephews in chorus.
“My children,” said the old man with a kindly smile, “to you, coming as you do from such a home as that of your father, my dear brother, the sum must seem fabulous, though to me today it sounds moderate enough. Nevertheless, you are right. From that moment I count the great change in my life and the confirmation of that Divine Mercy which had always watched me hitherto, as I now know, but which henceforward was gloriously present in every act of my life.
“Before the day when I first saw that river and that town, first met the enthusiastic young engineer, first formed my decisive plan, I had been a man subject to grave anxieties and sufferings; now precariously affluent, now starving; now again doubtfully possessed of some fleeting money. Then came the marvellous year I have just recited. Since then I have enjoyed the results of so much persistence and skill. I have gathered and enjoyed without cease the fruits of great and increasing wealth.”
“Oh, uncle, what are these?”
“They are,” said the good old merchant in grave and reverent tones, “the honour of neighbours, the devotion of friends, the admiration of all mankind, a permanent self-respect, and﻿—what is more important than them all﻿—the strong peace of the soul.”
The intolerable howl of the muezzin checked his nephews’ reply, and they, their happy eyes shining as though they themselves were the recipients of these seven figures went home in a dream of gold.

===Chinese===

“建筑商与掮客阵营斩钉截铁地坚称：依循造物主神圣的天意安排，天地间的一切物产皆理应在最高竞价者之间公开竞买拍卖。
“至于老夫自个儿，老夫同全城绝大多数懵懂无知的纳税百姓一般无二，对双方引经据典的宏大叙事压根毫无兴趣。唯一令老夫赏心悦目、心旷神怡的，乃是摆在眼前的铁一般事实：在这两大集团于评议会公堂之上的疯狂火拼竞逐之中，老夫名下地产的评估估值正以令人眼花缭乱的速度节节暴涨！
“最终，前一个阵营大获全胜，城邦官府以倾国之力全盘买断了老夫的名下资产（平心而论，这委实是一场令人叹为观止的社会学绝妙试验！）；而老夫当场落袋的买断总价，高达整整2,000,000枚金币（2000000）！”
“整整2,000,000枚金币（2000000）！”惊骇欲绝的小侄儿们异口同声地失声惊呼起来！
“我的孩子们，”老商人脸上浮现出一抹和蔼慈祥的微笑，温和地说道，“对于你们这般出身于你们生父——也就是老夫亲爱的胞弟——那等清贫寒舍的后生而言，这笔巨款听上去宛如天方夜谭；尽管在老夫今日的眼界看来，这不过是笔相当平淡的碎银子罢了。然而话又说回来，你们惊叹得完全在理。正是自那一刻起，老夫真正迎来了生命中的惊天蜕变，并彻底印证了上苍神明那自始至终庇护着老夫的‘真主的仁慈（Mercy of Allah）’——老夫如今深知，那份慈爱自始至终未曾离开过老夫半步，而自那一刻起，它更是在老夫余生的每一桩功业中如日中天、璀璨辉煌！
“在老夫初次望见那条大河与重镇、初次邂逅那位狂热的年轻工匠、初次定下老夫雷霆大计的那天之前，老夫不过是一个终日被极度惶恐与苦难折磨蹂躏的凡夫俗子：忽而勉强苟活度日，忽而沦为饥寒乞丐；忽而手里握着几文朝不保夕的飘忽铜板。然而紧接着，便迎来了老夫适才向你们娓娓道来的那段神迹般的奇迹岁月！自那一岁之后，老夫终生尽情享用着这份非凡毅力与绝世才智结出的累累硕果。老夫永不停歇地攫取并畅享着滚滚财富带来的无尽至福。”
“啊，叔父！那究竟是些什么福报呢？”
“那便是，”这位大善人以庄重而充满敬畏的虔诚语调答道，“邻里乡党的由衷尊崇、莫逆之交的忠贞奉献、天下万民的崇拜敬仰、历久弥坚的自尊自爱；以及——比世间一切荣华富贵更要紧万倍的——灵魂深处的至高宁静。”
宣礼塔上传来的凄厉刺耳的呼拜号叫声，硬生生掐断了侄儿们的答话；而这群少年双眸中闪烁着狂喜的异彩，仿佛自个儿已然亲手接过了这串令人眩晕的7位数（7）泼天横财一般，在一片金灿灿的黄金痴梦中踏上了归途。

## I *Al-Rafsat*, or “The Kick” / 第一章 飞踹
===Original===

In the days of Abd-er-Rahman, who was among the wisest and most glorious of the commanders of the faithful, there resided in the city of Baghdad an elderly merchant of such enormous wealth that his lightest expressions of opinion caused the markets of the Euphrates to fluctuate in the most alarming manner.
This merchant, whose name was Mahmoud, had a brother in the middle ranks of society, a surgeon by profession, and by name El-Hakim. To this brother he had frequently expressed a fixed determination to leave him no wealth of any kind. “It is my opinion,” he would say, “that a man’s first duty is to his own children, and though I have no children myself, I must observe the general rule.” He was fond of dilating upon this subject whenever he came across his relative, and would discover from time to time new and still better reasons for the resolution he had arrived at. His brother received with great courtesy the prospect held out to him by the wealthy merchant; but one day, finding tedious the hundredth repetition of that person’s pious but somewhat wearisome resolve, said to him:
“Mahmoud, though it would be a mean and even an impious thing to expect an inheritance from you to any of my seven sons, yet perhaps you will allow these boys to receive from your lips some hint as to the manner in which you have accumulated that great wealth which you now so deservedly enjoy.”
“By all means,” said Mahmoud, who was ever ready to describe his own talents and success. “Send the little fellows round to me tomorrow about the hour the public executions take place before the palace, for by that time I shall have breakfasted, and shall be ready to receive them.”
The surgeon, with profuse thanks, left his brother and conveyed the good news to the seven lads, who stood in order before him with the respect for parents customary in the Orient, each placed according to his size and running in gradation from eight to sixteen years of age.

===Chinese===

在阿卜杜勒·拉赫曼统治的岁月里——彼乃是普天下最英明、最荣耀的信士长官之一——巴格达（Baghdad）城中居住着一位上了年纪的商贾，其坐拥的资财是如此骇人听闻，以致他随口吐露的一句轻微评议，便足以令整个幼发拉底河流域的商贸行市产生惊心动魄的剧烈震荡。
这位商人名叫马哈茂德（Mahmoud）。他有一胞弟位列市井中产阶层，以悬壶行医为业，人称哈基姆。马哈茂德曾屡次向胞弟斩钉截铁地挑明：他平生立誓绝不给胞弟留下一丝一毫的遗产。他常挂在嘴边的话是：“依老夫之见，为人首要之责乃是顾念自个儿的骨肉亲生，虽说老夫膝下并无子嗣，但亦绝不可坏了纲常公理。”每逢同胞弟打照面，他便乐此不疲就此宏论大发议论，且时不时能为自个儿这桩铁石心肠的决断发掘出愈发冠冕堂皇的崭新托词。做医生的胞弟对于大富豪兄长描摹的这一惨淡前景，向来表现得毕恭毕敬；然而忽有一日，在第100次（100）听闻兄长这番虔诚而令人昏昏欲睡的陈词滥调之后，医生终于忍不住开口说道：
“马哈茂德，虽说指望兄长赏赐一份遗产给我的七个（7）犬子中的任何一人，委实是卑劣下作乃至大不敬之举；然而兄长能否大发慈悲，容这帮孩子亲耳从兄长金口之中聆听一番教诲，指点一下您究竟是如何积攒下如今这份受之无愧的泼天富贵的呢？”
“那自然好极了，”马哈茂德满口应承，他向来最热衷于向世人炫耀自个儿的经商奇才与辉煌功绩。“明日晌午宫门前斩首公开行刑的时辰，打发这帮小崽子到老夫府上来吧，届时老夫用过早膳，恰好得空接见他们。”
做医生的胞弟千恩万谢地告辞离去，将这一喜讯带给了七个（7）少年。按照东方孝敬尊长的古老纲常，孩子们在他面前依长幼高矮顺次垂手肃立，年龄跨度从8岁一直排到16岁（8、16）上下。

===Original===

Upon the morrow, therefore, the surgeon’s seven sons, seated gravely upon crossed legs, formed a semicircle at the feet of their revered relative, who, when he had watched them humorously and in silence for some moments, puffing at his great pipe, opened his lips and spoke as follows:
“Your father has wondered, my dear nephews, in what way the fortune I enjoy has been acquired; for in his own honourable but far from lucrative walk of life, sums which are to me but daily trifles appear like the ransoms of kings. To you, his numerous family, it seems of especial advantage that the road to riches should be discovered. Now I will confess to you, my dear lads, that I am quite ignorant of any rule or plan whereby the perishable goods of this world may be rapidly accumulated in the hands of the faithful. Nay, did any such rule exist, I am persuaded that by this time the knowledge of it would be so widely diffused as to embrace the whole human race. In which case,” he added, puffing meditatively at his pipe, “all would cancel out and no result would be achieved; since a great fortune, as I need not inform young people of your sagacity, is hardly to be acquired save at the expense of others.
“But though I cannot give you those rules for which your father was seeking when he sent you hither, I can detail you the steps by which my present affluence was achieved; and each of you, according to his intelligence, will appreciate what sort of accidents may make for the increase of fortune. When you are possessed of this knowledge it will serve you through life for recreation and amusement, though I very much doubt its making you any richer. For it is not the method nor even the opportunity of intelligent acquisition which lead to great riches, but two other things combined: one, the unceasing appetite to snatch and hold from all and at every season; the other, that profound mystery, the Mercy of God.
“For Allah, in his inscrutable choice, frowns on some and smiles on others. The first he condemns to contempt, anxiety, duns, bills, courts of law, sudden changes of residence and even dungeons; the second he gratifies with luxurious vehicles, delicious sherbet and enormous houses, such as mine. His will be done.
“A dear friend of mine, one Mashé, was a receiver of stolen goods in Bosra, until God took him, now twenty years ago.

===Chinese===

翌日，医生的七个（7）儿子盘腿肃坐，在备受尊崇的伯父脚边围成一道整齐的半圆。这位长辈一边吧嗒吧嗒抽着他那支巨大的水烟袋，一边以诙谐而沉思的目光默默端详了侄儿们良久，随后启唇开讲：
“我亲爱的侄儿们，你们的生父向来百思不得其解，老夫坐享的这份家私究竟是如何挣来的；因在他那尊贵却绝无半点油水的清贫行当里，老夫平日里随手散出的零碎碎银，在他眼里亦宛如君王的赎城巨资。对于你们这帮嗷嗷待哺的众多子嗣而言，能探寻出发财致富的通天捷径，自然被视作天大的幸事。然而老夫不妨向你们交个底，我亲爱的孩子们：关于这凡尘俗世的过眼云烟究竟依循何等章法方能疾速积聚于正信信徒之手，老夫实则一无所知！天上地下若当真存在这等捷径妙法，老夫深信此道早已家喻户晓、传遍整个人类寰宇了。而倘若当真如此，”说到此处，他若有所思地深吸了一口水烟，“万民皆谙此道，则彼此抵消归零，世间将绝无任何巨富诞生；因正如你们这般聪颖后生所深知的那样：天底下任何一笔泼天富贵，几乎无一例外皆是敲骨吸髓建立在旁人的倾家荡产之上！
“因此，尽管老夫无法传授给你们那套令尊梦寐以求的致富秘笈，老夫却能向你们细细拆解老夫是如何一步步攀上今日之富贵巅峰的；至于你们当中谁能参悟其玄机，全凭各自的天资悟性去领会何等机缘巧合能催生万贯家私。待你们通晓了这一生平履历，它自能充当你们余生茶余饭后的绝妙消遣与谈资，尽管老夫极度怀疑它究竟能否让你们兜里多添半文铜板。盖因通往泼天富贵的通天大道，既非凭靠所谓的勤勉手段，亦非凭靠敏锐过人的掠夺机敏，而是倚仗两大法宝的完美交融：其一，乃是无论严寒酷暑、面对任何人皆永不停歇生吞活剥的饕餮贪欲；其二，则是那深不可测的宇宙至高玄机——真主的仁慈（Mercy of Allah）！
“盖因全能的主宰凭其凡人难测的玄妙拣选，对一部分人怒目而视，对另一部分人则喜笑颜开。对于前者，祂罚他们饱受市井唾弃、债台高筑、传票逼索、公堂受审、颠沛流离乃至沦为阶下囚徒；而对于后者，祂则赐予他们雕梁画栋的华贵车轿、甘冽沁脾的冰镇果露甜饮，以及如老夫眼前这般富丽堂皇的巍峨宫殿。愿祂的圣意通行于天地！
“老夫曾有一位莫逆之交名叫马谢，在巴士拉做着销赃受贿的下作营生，直到20年前（20）真主取了他的性命。”

===Original===

He left two sons of equal intelligence and rapacity. The one, after numerous degradations, died of starvation in Armenia; the other, of no greater skill, is today governor of all Algeria and rings the changes at will upon the public purse. *Mektub.*”
For a moment the ancient captain of industry paused with bent head in solemn meditation upon the designs of Heaven, then raising his features protested that he had too long delayed the story of his life, with which he would at once proceed.
---
“As a boy, my dear nephews,” began the kindly uncle, while his dutiful nephews regarded him with round eyes, “I was shy, dirty, ignorant, lazy, and wilful. My parents and teachers had but to give me an order for me to conceive at once some plan of disobeying it. All forms of activity save those connected with dissipation were abhorrent to me. So far from reciting with other boys of my age in chorus and without fault the verses of the Koran, I grew up completely ignorant of that work, the most Solemn Name in which I to this day pronounce with an aspirate from an unfamiliarity with its aspect upon the written page. Yet I am glad to say that I never neglected my religious duties, that I prayed with fervour and regularity, and that I had a singular faith in the loving kindness of my God.
“I had already reached my seventeenth year when my father, who had carefully watched the trend of my nature and the use to which I had put my faculties, addressed me as follows:
“ ‘Mahmoud, I wish you no ill. I have so far fed and clothed you because the caliph (whom Allah preserve!) has caused those who neglect their younger offspring to be severely beaten upon the soles of their feet. It is now my intention to send you about your business. I propose’﻿—and here my dear father pulled out a small purse﻿—‘to give you the smallest sum compatible with my own interests, so that if any harm befall you, the vigilant officers of the crown cannot ascribe your disaster to my neglect. I request that you will walk in any direction you choose so only that it be in a straight line away from my doors. If, when this your patrimony is spent, you make away with yourself I shall hold you to blame; I shall be better pleased to hear that you have sold yourself into slavery or in some other way provided for your continued sustenance. But what I should like best would be never to hear of you again.’

===Chinese===

“他膝下留有二子，天资禀赋与贪婪成性平分秋色。其中一子历经千般屈辱折磨，最终在亚美尼亚活活饿死街头；而另一个才智平平的逆子，今日却高踞全阿尔及利亚总督显位，随意翻云覆雨侵吞国库公帑。这便是宿命‘麦克图布’啊！”
言毕，这位昔日的商业巨擘垂首肃立，在神明深不可测的旨意面前陷入了良久的深沉凝思；片刻之后，他抬起面庞，责备自个儿耽搁了生平传奇的正文，旋即接着讲道。
---
“我亲爱的侄儿们，”慈祥的叔父接着开讲，而恭顺的侄儿们则瞪圆了双眼全神贯注凝视着他，“老夫少时生就一副怯懦、邋遢、愚笨、疏懒且桀骜不驯的贱骨头。凡是爹娘与西席夫子下的号令，老夫脑海中浮现出的第一念头必定是如何变着法儿阳奉阴违违抗到底。凡是正经行当，只要不沾吃喝嫖赌荒唐浪荡的边儿，在老夫眼里皆形同水火、避之唯恐不及。别的同龄童子皆能异口同声、倒背如流吟诵《古兰经》圣训，老夫却对那部圣典一窍不通，以致典籍中最庄严肃穆的真主圣名，老夫时至今日念诵起来还带着倒吸气的怪音，全因老夫从未在羊皮卷上正眼瞧过它的模样。然而老夫十分欣慰地告诉你们：老夫平生从不曾废弛宗教天职，老夫祷告向来虔诚勤勉，且对上苍神明的仁慈厚爱怀有一种异乎寻常的坚定信念。
“待老夫年满17岁（17）那年，先父将老夫的劣根天性与老夫平日里的混账做派尽收眼底，某日终于将老夫唤至跟前，严正训话如下：
“‘马哈茂德，老夫并非与你有仇。老夫之所以管你吃饱穿暖养你到今天，全因哈里发王上（愿真主保佑他长寿！）颁下铁律：凡有弃养幼子者一律施以重杖狠狠抽打脚底心。如今老夫意欲将你扫地出门任你自谋生路。老夫打算’——说到此处，我亲爱的父亲掏出一只瘪瘪的小钱袋——‘在不损及老夫自身切身利益的前提下，打发给你一笔世间最微薄的盘缠碎银，好教你他日横尸荒野之际，朝廷耳目严苛的差官无论如何亦怪罪不到老夫头上。老夫唯独恳请你爱往哪儿滚就往哪儿滚，只要笔直朝着远离老夫门槛的方向滚就行。待到这点微薄祖产被你挥霍殆尽之时，倘若你寻死觅活自绝生路，老夫亦由得你去；不过老夫更乐意听闻你把自己卖身为奴、或以其他下作营生勉强苟活度日。然而老夫平生最梦寐以求的，乃是自此往后生生世世休要再听到你的半点音讯！’”

===Original===

With those words my father (your grandfather, dear boys), seizing me by the shoulders, turned my back to his doors and thrust me forth with a hearty kick the better to emphasize his meaning.
“Thus was I launched out in the dawn of manhood to try my adventures with the world.
“I discovered in my pouch as I set out along the streets of the city the sum of 100 dinars, with which my thoughtful parent had provided me under the legal compulsion which he so feelingly described. ‘With so large a capital,’ said I to myself, ‘I can exist for several days, indulge my favourite forms of dissipation, and when they are well spent it will be time enough to think of some experiment whereby to replace them.’ ”
Here the eldest nephew said respectfully and with an inclination of the head: “Pray, uncle, what is a dinar?”
“My dear lad,” replied the merchant with a merry laugh, “I confess that to a man of my position a reply to your question is impossible. I could only tell you that it is a coin of considerable value to the impoverished, but to men like myself a denomination so inferior as to be indistinguishable from all other coins.”
Having so expressed himself the worthy merchant resumed the thread of his tale:
“I had, I say, started forward in high spirits to the sound of the coins jingling in my pouch, when my steps happened to take to the waterside, where I found a ship about to sail for the Persian Gulf. ‘Here,’ said I to myself, ‘is an excellent opportunity for travelling and for seeing the world.’
“The heat of the day was rising. No one was about but two watermen, who lay dozing upon the bank. I nimbly stepped aboard and hid myself behind one of the bales of goods with which the deck was packed. When the sun declined and work was resumed, the sailors tramped aboard, the sail was hoisted, and we started upon our journey.
“Befriended by the darkness of night I crept out quietly from my hiding-place and found a man watching over the prow, where he was deputed to try the depth of the water from time to time with a long pole. I affected an air of authority, and told him that the captain had sent me forward to deliver his commands, which were that he should give me a flask of wine, some fruit, and a cake (for I guessed that like all sailors he had in his possession things both lawful and unlawful). These I told him I would take to my relative the captain.

===Chinese===

“说罢这番肺腑之言，先父（也就是你们的亲祖父啊，我亲爱的孩子们）一把揪住老夫的双肩，将老夫的身子扭转向门外，顺势飞起结结实实的一脚，狠狠踹在老夫的屁股上，好教老夫铭心刻骨记住他的苦心教诲！
“就这样，老夫在弱冠之年的拂晓时分，被一记飞脚踹入了茫茫红尘，去独自闯荡江湖。
“当老夫灰头土脸漫步在城中长街之时，老夫一掏腰包，惊喜地发现先父在朝廷严刑逼迫之下塞给老夫的盘缠，足足有100第纳尔（100）之巨！‘手握如此巨款，’老夫自言自语道，‘老夫尽可优哉游哉花天酒地好些天，尽情放纵老夫最嗜好的各色浪荡恶习；待这笔银子被老夫挥霍殆尽，届时再去琢磨寻觅什么偏门把亏空填补回来亦为时不晚。’
听到此处，最年长的大侄子恭恭敬敬鞠了一躬，探问答道：“敢问叔父，这‘第纳尔’究竟是何物？”
“我亲爱的孩子，”商人发出一阵爽朗的开怀大笑，答道，“老夫向你坦白：对于老夫这般身份地位之人而言，回答你这问题委实是强人所难。老夫只能告诉你：对于赤贫乞丐而言，此乃一笔沉甸甸的大钱；但对于老夫这等巨室豪门，此等面额委实低贱到了同世间一切散碎铜板毫无二致的微末地步！”
吐露完这番豪言，可敬的商人重新拾起话头：
“话说老夫听着腰包里铜钱撞击出的悦耳脆响，心旷神怡大步流星向前迈进，不知不觉便来到了河畔码头，正巧撞见一条大船正扬帆起航准备驶向波斯湾。‘妙啊！’老夫暗自思忖，‘这岂非周游列国、见识天下大千世界的绝佳天赐良机！’
“彼时骄阳似火、酷热难耐，岸边除了两名（2）正倚着河堤打盹的水手外空无一人。老夫纵身一跃轻巧跳上甲板，一头钻入了堆聚在甲板上的高耸货垛缝隙深处藏匿了起来。待到日头西斜、码头重新开工，水手们喧嚷着踏上甲板，升起巨帆，大船缓缓破浪起航。
“在夜幕苍穹的掩护下，老夫轻手轻脚从藏身之所摸了出来，在船头望见一名守夜水手正手执长竹竿时不时探测试探水深。老夫当即端出一副威严的长官派头，冷声告知他船长大人遣老夫前来传达指令：命他立刻奉上一壶私酿好酒、几枚鲜果以及一块面饼（盖因老夫老早便洞悉所有的水手箱底皆藏有见不得光的违禁之物）。老夫诓称老夫自会将这些吃食转呈给身为老夫表亲的船长大人。”

===Original===

He left me with the pole for a moment and soon returned with the provisions, with which I crept back to my hiding-place, and there heartily consumed them.
“During the whole of the next day I lay sleeping behind the bales of goods. With the fall of the second night I needed a second meal. I dared not repeat my first experiment, and lay musing till, hunger having sharpened my wits, I hit upon a plan with which surely Providence itself must have inspired a poor lonely lad thrown in his unaided weakness upon a cruel world.
“I bethought me that the watchman of either board would have some provision for the night. I remembered a sort of gangway between the high bales upon the main deck, which corridor led back far under the poop into the stern sheets. It has been so designed for the convenience of stowing and unloading, affording a passage for the workmen as they handled the cargo. I put these two things together in my mind (but to God be the glory) and formed of them a plan for immediate execution.
“I crept from my hiding-place and sauntered along the dark deck until I came upon the watchman, squatting by the rail, and contemplating the stars in the moonless sky. He had, as I had suspected, a platter the white of which I could just see glimmering against the deck beside him. I thought I also discerned a gourd of wine. I approached him as one of the crew (for they were chance strangers taken on at the wharf). We talked in low tones of the girls of Baghdad, of the police, of opportunities for theft, and of such other topics as are common to the poor, till, naturally, we came to wine. He cursed the poor quality of his own, in the gourd beside him. I, after some mystery, confided to him that I had a stock of excellent wine, and, as my friendship for him increased, I made a clean breast of it and told him it was in the stern sheets, far under the poop deck along the narrow passage between the high bales. I offered to go with him and fetch it, allowing him, in his eagerness, to go first. When he was well engaged in groping aft I turned, crept forward again silently and rapidly, picked up the loaf and cheese which I found on his platter, as also the gourd, and vanished into my hiding-hole.
“I ate my fill﻿—though somewhat too hurriedly, and remarked how long a time my shipmate was spending at searching that empty place.

===Chinese===

“那水手松开竹竿走开片刻，很快便捧着丰美的酒水面饼折返回来；老夫一把夺过吃食，悄然溜回老夫的货垛老巢，躲在暗处大快朵颐吃了个底朝天。
“在整整随后的一日里，老夫全在货垛背后呼呼大睡。当第二夜（2）的夜幕降临时，老夫那饥肠辘辘的肚皮再次抗议了起来。老夫不敢故技重施冒充船长亲眷，便在暗中苦苦琢磨；直到饥饿彻底磨砺了老夫的机敏嗅觉，老夫终于灵光一现——这定是神明亲自眷顾一个孤苦伶仃、被抛入残酷人世无依无靠的可怜后生！
“老夫暗自思忖：两舷值夜的值更水手必定随身备有夜宵干粮。老夫记起主甲板堆垒的高耸货包之间留有一条窄小的通道，这道暗廊一路向后延伸、径直穿入艉楼甲板深处。这本是为了方便码头装卸工人搬运搬抬货物而特意留出的通道。老夫在脑海中将这两桩细务融会贯通（荣耀皆归真主！），当场定下了一套立竿见影的雷霆妙计。
“老夫蹑手蹑脚钻出藏身暗洞，在黑黢黢的甲板上闲庭信步踱步，直到撞见那位蹲在舷墙旁凝视着无月夜空的守夜水手。果不其然，正如老夫暗中所料，借着微弱星光，老夫隐约瞧见他身旁的甲板上泛着一只白瓷餐盘的幽光，身侧似乎还立着一只盛酒的葫芦。老夫以甲板水手的同袍口吻凑上前去（盖因船上全是从码头临时雇来的市井浪人）。我们用极低微的耳语闲扯起巴格达的妖艳舞娘、官府捕快巡丁、趁火打劫的摸金良机、以及底层穷汉惯常热衷的各色荤素话题，聊着聊着，自然便绕到了杯中之物上来。他骂骂咧咧痛斥身旁葫芦里的劣酒难以下咽；老夫故弄玄虚半推半就之后，悄然向他倾吐心声：声称老夫箱底私藏了一坛举世无双的陈年绝品佳酿；且随着老夫与他‘兄弟情深’的迅速升温，老夫坦率向他指路：那坛佳酿就藏在艉楼深处狭窄通道尽头的货垛底下！老夫热情相邀同他一道前去取酒，并在他急不可耐的狂喜中大度容许他打头阵摸黑钻入暗廊。待他整个人弓着身子深陷于后舱货堆中摸索之际，老夫骤然转身，轻手轻脚却迅如闪电般折返前舱，一把抄走他留在甲板餐盘上的整块大面包与干酪，抄起酒葫芦，滋溜一声彻底钻回了老夫隐秘的货包暗洞！
“老夫风卷残云饱餐了一顿——尽管吃得略显仓促；并在暗处极其惬意地听着那位同袍水手在空荡荡的后舱黑灯瞎火中耗费了无穷无尽的时光瞎撞搜寻。”

===Original===

As I heard him creeping back at last cursing violently in whispers, I was aware of faint dawn in the east, and determined that my cruise must end.
“We were already in the neighbourhood of the sea, as I discovered by tasting the water over the side in the darkness and discovering it to be brackish. I bethought me that my poor comrade had now an excellent reason for ferreting me out, that the captain also would soon hear of me and that, with daylight, I should certainly be visited with a bastinado or put into chains and sold. I therefore slipped over the side (for I was an excellent swimmer) and made for the shore. There I lay on a warm beach and watched through the reeds the great sail of the ship as it slipped downstream further and further away in the growing light.
“When the sun rose the vessel was out of sight, and looking about me I discovered a little village not far from the shore inhabited by simple fishermen, but containing several houses of some pretension, the residences of wealthy merchants who came here from Bosra in their moments of leisure to relax themselves from the catch-as-catch-can of commerce in that neighbouring city.
“My first action at the opening of the new day was to fall upon my knees and add to the ritual prayer a humble outpouring of thanks for the benefits I had already received and a fervent appeal for guidance. That appeal was heard. I rose from my knees full of a newfound plan.
“To one of those wealthier houses which stood near the village I at once proceeded and sent in a message by a slave to its owner saying that my master, a wealthy dealer in carpets, solicited the custom of his lordship, and that if the great man would but accompany me to the quay I would there show him wares well worthy of his attention.
“It so happened (and here was Providence again at work) that this merchant had a passion for a particular sort of carpet which is solely made by the inhabitants of El Kzar, for they alone possess the secret, which they very zealously guard. The slave, therefore, brought me back the message that his master would not be at the pains of accompanying me unless such wares were present for his inspection. If my carpets were those of El Kzar he would willingly inspect them, but if they were of any other brand he was indifferent.
“And let this teach you, my dear nephews, how simple are the minds of the rich.

===Chinese===

“待老夫终于听见他骂骂咧咧、咬牙切齿地贴着甲板摸索折返之时，东方天际已然泛起了一抹微弱的鱼肚白；老夫当机立断：老夫的大航海生涯必须到此为止了！
“我们在黑夜中探手伸出舷墙尝了一口江水，尝出了明显的咸苦涩味，由此断定大船已然驶入了波斯湾入海口的咸淡水交界之所。老夫心知肚明：老夫那位可怜的同袍如今有了天大的理由要将老夫翻个底朝天揪出来碎尸万段，船长大人很快亦会获悉老夫的存在；一旦天光大亮，老夫必定难逃一顿皮开肉绽的严刑杖笞、乃至被当场打上镣铐卖身为奴的灭顶之灾！老夫毫不犹豫纵身翻过舷墙（老夫水性极好），悄无声息滑入水中，奋力游向水岸。老夫趴在温暖的沙滩之上，透过茂密的芦苇丛，静静目送着那艘扬着巨帆的大船在晨光熹微的潮水中顺流而下，越漂越远。
“待红日初升之时，大船早已在天际线消失得无影无踪。老夫环顾四下，发现离海岸不远坐落着一个由淳朴渔民聚居的微末渔村；然而村中竟错落矗立着数栋气派不凡的华美宅邸——那正是来自重镇巴士拉的巨富商贾们在闲暇之余，为了逃避临近大都市商海殊死博弈的残酷厮杀而专程营建的避暑别业。
“在这崭新一天的开端，老夫的第一桩要务乃是当场扑通跪倒在地，在例行的晨祷之余，以最卑微虔诚的心境向真主倾诉无尽的感恩，感念老夫迄今为止所笑纳的无量恩典，并泣血祈求神明的指引。这一祈求当场获得了灵验回应！老夫自沙滩上弹身而起，脑海中已然充盈了一套崭新的旷世妙计。
“老夫径直迈向村头一栋最显赫奢华的富商别业，打发守门的黑奴向其庄园主传话：声称老夫的东家乃是一位富甲一方的绝顶地毯巨擘，特遣老夫前来叩谒老爷，倘若长官肯移驾码头，老夫愿当场呈验一批绝不负长官尊贵法眼的绝世珍品。
“造化弄人啊（在此神明的天意再次大显神通！）：这位富商平生嗜地毯如命，尤其对产自‘克扎兰（Kzaran）’的一种绝品地毯痴迷发狂；盖因全天下唯有克扎兰城邦的工匠掌握此项独步天下的织造秘法，且向来密不外传严防死守。因此，黑奴替其主人带回的答复极其生硬：声称倘若码头上没有克扎兰地毯，老爷自个儿绝不屑于挪动半步尊躯；倘若老夫呈验的当真是正宗克扎兰神品，老爷自愿屈尊赏脸检视；但凡是世间任何其他杂牌货色，老爷连正眼都不瞧一眼！
“我亲爱的侄儿们，切记这一条铁律：这恰恰向你们昭示了富豪巨贾的脑瓜究竟有多么纯真弱智！”

===Original===

“I was willing enough that the carpets should be carpets of El Kzar, or, indeed, of any other place under heaven, for all were at my choice.
“I hastened, therefore, to send back a further message that by a curious coincidence we had upon this occasion nothing else in stock but Kzaran carpets, and begged the slave to emphasize this important point to his master.
“His reply was to twist his right hand, palm upwards, with a strange smile. I pulled out my purse, showed him the shining dinars, and asked him whether he would rather have *one* now for his fee or *five* on the completion of the transaction? With glistening eyes﻿—and even (as I thought) a pathetic gratitude﻿—the slave leapt at the latter offer.
“And let this teach you, my dear nephews, how simple are the minds of the poor.
“He hastened off to deliver my message.
“Within a few moments the master of the house appeared in great haste, and all of a fever bade me lead him to the appointed spot.”
At this moment the merchant paused and with reverie and reminiscence in his eye remained silent for at least that space of time in which a dexterous pickpocket may gingerly withdraw a purse from the sleeve of a Holy Man. The second nephew thought the opportunity arrived to suggest a doubt which had been vexing his young mind. He said with an obeisance:
“Venerable uncle, we have listened to the beginnings of your career with admiration and respect, but we are more perplexed than ever to discover how such beginnings could have led to such an end. For you appear to us as yet only to have followed that path which leads to the torturers and the bowstrings.”
“Such,” replied his uncle, with a look of singular affection, “is the general opinion entertained of all very wealthy men in the first steps of their careers; but I hope that the sequel will teach you and your clever little brothers how wrongly informed are the vulgar.”
As the merchant Mahmoud pronounced these words the call to prayer was heard from a neighbouring tower, and he hurriedly concluded:
“My dear nephews, we are called to prayer. I will cease here to speak and will continue tomorrow the story of myself and Providence.”

===Chinese===

“老夫自然求之不得地顺水推舟：地毯可以是克扎兰神品，亦完全可以是普天之下任何一个地方的货色，反正天上地下的名号全凭老夫一张嘴信口雌黄！
“老夫旋即赶忙打发黑奴折返回去报信：声称世事就是这般巧夺天工，老夫船队手头积压的全部现货，好巧不巧全盘清一色正是正宗克扎兰（Kzaran）地毯！并恳请黑奴务必向其主人力陈这一千载难逢的重磅喜讯。
“那黑奴的反应则是右手掌心向上微微一翻，嘴角勾勒出一抹狡黠贪婪的怪笑。老夫当即掏出腰包，在他眼前晃了晃金光灿灿的第纳尔，探问他究竟是乐意此刻当场落袋1枚金币打赏，还是乐意在交易大功告成之后笑纳足足5枚金币的巨额抽水？看着黑奴双眸中闪烁的绿光——以及（依老夫之见）那一脸令人动容的卑微感激——那厮毫无悬念地恶狗扑食般扑向了后一种许诺！
“我亲爱的侄儿们，切记这一条铁律：这同样向你们昭示了底层穷汉的脑瓜究竟有多么纯真弱智！
“那厮飞奔回后堂传信。
“不出数个弹指工夫，庄园主便火烧眉毛般跌跌撞撞奔了出来，满面通红急不可耐地勒令老夫立刻引路带他奔赴码头验货！”
讲到此处，商人微微顿住，双眸中闪烁着回味往昔荣光的迷离神采，陷入了沉默——其静默的工夫，恰似一个技艺精湛的小偷从一位圣僧的宽大僧袍袖口中悄无声息摸出一只钱袋所需的光阴一般。二侄子以为时机已到，便将一直盘旋在年轻心头的疑窦脱口而出；他深深鞠了一躬，探问道：
“可敬的叔父，侄儿们怀着无上的钦佩与崇敬聆听了您早年发家的壮举；然而侄儿们却愈发百思不得其解：如此这般的下作开局，究竟是如何一路通向今日这般泼天富贵的呢？盖因在侄儿们看来，您老当年踏上的那条道，分明是径直通往衙门大刑伺候与绞刑索命的死路啊！”
“世间愚昧凡夫，”叔父以极其慈祥怜爱的目光端详着他，答道，“在审视一切巨富巨贾早年迈出的第一步时，脑子里浮现出的往往皆是这等鄙陋成见；然而老夫深信，后续的故事必将向你以及你那些聪慧的小兄弟们证明：市井草民的见识究竟错得有多离谱！”
就在大商人马哈茂德吐露这番言辞之际，邻近塔楼上传来了庄严的呼拜之声；他旋即匆匆收束话头：
“我亲爱的侄儿们，神明呼唤我们去祈祷了。老夫今日且讲到此处，明日此时老夫将继续为你们拆解老夫与神明眷顾的传奇大局。”

===Original===

Upon hearing these words his seven nephews rose together, and crossed their arms; following which gesture, with three deep bows performed as they walked backwards toward the curtains of the magnificent apartment, they left their uncle’s presence.
---
“You will remember, my dear nephews,” said the merchant Mahmoud when the lads were once more assembled in a half-circle before him with crossed legs and attentive countenances, “that you left me hurrying with the collector of Kzaran carpets towards the quay where he should enjoy the sight of the merchandise. This merchant was reputed among the people of the place to be of a singularly cunning and secretive temperament, a character which (you may think it strange!) they admired as though it were the summit of human wisdom. I confess that I found him, in the matter of Kzaran carpets at least, very different from his reputation. A more garrulous old gentleman never trod this earth. He was in a perpetual stammer of excitement, and though I was careful to lead him by the most roundabout roads that he might have time to cool his ardour, the delay did but seem to increase it.
“ ‘I implore you, sir,’ he said at last, as one who could no longer restrain some violent passion, ‘I implore you, pay no attention to others in this place who may have attempted to forestall me in the matter of your very valuable cargo. Your honour is, I know, sufficient in the matter.﻿ ﻿… I am confident you will give me a free market. Also, they know nothing of Kzaran carpets in these parts: they are mere buyers and sellers﻿ ﻿… and on what a margin! Let me tell you in your ear that while many men in this place carry on the appearance of riches, most are indebted to Parsees in the capital. I alone am in an independent position and’ (here he whispered) ‘I can well reward you *privately* and *in your own pocket* for any favour you may show me.’
“Seeing him so eager, I affected a certain hesitation and embarrassment, and at last confessed that I had been approached by a local merchant whose name I was forbidden to mention and who had very kindly sent me as a present by a slave the sum of 1,000 dinars. To this he had attached no conditions, but he had also, quite independently, sent word that he had himself orders for carpets which he was bound to fulfil.

===Chinese===

闻听此言，七个（7）侄儿齐刷刷弹身而起，双臂交叉抱胸，向着通往这座豪阔殿宇门帘的方向一边恭顺倒退、一边深深行了三次大礼，悄然退出了叔父的内堂。
---
“我亲爱的侄儿们，想必你们还记得，”当少年们再次盘腿围坐成一道半圆、满脸企盼端详着长辈之时，商人马哈茂德开口讲道，“昨天你们听老夫讲到正同那位痴迷克扎兰（Kzaran）地毯的收藏家兼富商火急火燎奔向码头去‘大开眼界’。这位掌柜在当地坊间素以城府极深、诡诈莫测著称；世俗凡夫俗子（你们或许觉得不可思议！）竟将这种阴鸷小人视作人间至高智慧的化身而推崇备至。然而老夫向你们坦白：单就克扎兰地毯一节而言，老夫发现这厮的行径同坊间传闻委实判若两人。天底下绝无第二个老家伙能比他更为聒噪饶舌、沉不住气！他激动得结结巴巴、语无伦次；尽管老夫打起十二分小心，故意领着他兜兜转转专挑羊肠弯路绕大圈子，好教他那滚烫的邪火有工夫冷缩降温，然而这一番延宕折腾反倒像火上浇油般彻底引爆了他的狂热！
“‘先生，老夫求求您啦！’他终于像一头再也按捺不住狂暴情欲的野兽般哀嚎起来，‘老夫求求您，千万莫要理会本城那些企图捷足先登截胡您这批绝世重器的宵小之徒！老夫深知您的品格贵重，金字招牌一言九鼎！……老夫坚信您必定会给老夫一个公平竞买的独家先机！况且此地的凡夫俗子对克扎兰地毯一窍不通：他们不过是一群唯利是图的倒买倒卖之徒……且算盘打得何等刻薄！老夫不妨附耳告知您一个绝密内幕：本城许多人表面上看似腰缠万贯，实则底子里早已被帝都首善之区的拜火教高利贷钱庄吸干了骨髓！唯独老夫底子清白、坐拥万贯真金白银；而且，’说到此处他压低嗓门化为鬼祟耳语，‘只要您能在买卖上向老夫略微倾斜关照，老夫必在暗中私下里、直接将沉甸甸的重谢塞进您老个人的私囊腰包之中！’
“瞧见他已然丧魂落魄至此，老夫故意流露出一副左右为难、局促不安的神态，终于吞吞吐吐向他招供：声称本地另一位老夫立誓不可泄露尊姓大名的神秘商贾早已私下找上了老夫，且极其阔绰地遣家奴奉上了一笔整整1,000第纳尔（1000）的见面薄礼！老夫声称那位贵人并未附加任何严苛条件，却极其客气地捎话过来：声称他自个儿亦承揽了一批不得不履约交割的地毯订单。”

===Original===

His profit (he had said), if I would give him a first choice, would be so considerable that he would be very ready to offer me a handsome commission on the completion of the bargain; quite apart from the 1,000 dinars which were but a little present from one man to another. ‘This thousand dinars,’ I added, ‘now in my possession, I have accepted. A present is a graceful act and can be taken with a clean conscience. But the *commission* is another matter. I must consider beyond everything the interests of my master. I shall not mention the offer made to me (for with all his confidence in me he is himself a business man and might misunderstand my position), but I shall think it my duty to give him no advice save to sell to the highest bidder.’
“ ‘It is I!’ shouted the aged connoisseur eagerly, ‘it is I who will prove the highest bidder! Nay, my dear fellow, since such bargains are often concluded in private, would it not be better to tell your master forthwith that no possible competitor can stand against me in this place? Let him first discover the sum offered by my rival and I give you leave to suggest a sum larger by one-tenth, which shall decide his judgment. Meanwhile,’ he added, ‘two thousand dinars are but a small present for one in your position, and I shall willingly﻿—joyfully﻿—propose to give you that sum, not for a moment on account of the service which I am certain you design to do me, but purely as an expression of my esteem.’
“His excitement had now so risen that I fear his judgment was lost. Already he saw before him in his mind’s eye a pile of the noblest Kzaran carpets, all ready for the caravan. Already he saw a rival calmly acquiring them on the distant wharf, the witnesses placing their seals, the words of completion.
“He trembled as again he urged on me the little gift, the personal gift, the trifling gift of 2,000 dinars.
“ ‘Sir,’ said I in reply, a little stiffly, ‘I am not accustomed to take secret commissions under any disguise. My duty is clear: if I cannot receive a firm offer superior to that already made me, and that backed with proof that you are indeed, as you say, the most solid man in the place﻿—then I must close with my first client. If indeed I were certain of an immediate payment in a larger sum I would accept your proposal. But how can I know anything of this place?

===Chinese===

“‘那位掌柜坦言，’老夫对他说道，‘倘若老夫能容他拔得头筹优先挑选，其倒手转卖的纯利将极其骇人，因而他极其乐意在交割大功告成之后向老夫奉上一笔极为肥美的私家佣金；这笔重酬完全不包括那笔纯属朋友间随喜赠予的1,000第纳尔（1000）见面礼！这1,000第纳尔（1000）现钱，’老夫补充道，‘如今已然稳稳落袋于老夫私囊之中。朋友间赠予礼物乃是雅事，笑纳起来自是问心无愧。然而那笔私下许诺的佣金抽水，可就是另一码事了！老夫身为忠仆，首要考量者必须是老夫东家的至高利益！老夫绝不会向东家透露这笔私相授受（因东家虽说对老夫信任有加，但他自个儿终究是个精明算计的生意人，极易对老夫的苦心产生误解）；然而老夫认定老夫的本分，乃是绝不向东家进半句谗言，唯劝东家认准一条铁律：价高者得！’
“‘老夫就是那个出价最高之人！’那位上了年纪的收藏家急得扯着嗓子失声尖叫，‘普天之下绝无人能比老夫出价更高！哎呀，我亲爱的老弟，既然此类大宗私约向来皆是私下密室敲定，您何不当机立断告知您的东家：在本城地面上绝无任何买家能同老夫一较高下！您不妨先去探明老夫那位死对头究竟开出了何等价码，老夫全权授意您直接在彼人出价的基础上原地加码整整十分之一！如此一来必能彻底敲定乾坤！与此同时，’他急赤白脸地补充道，‘区区2,000第纳尔（2000）对于您这般体面体恤的贵人而言不过是九牛一毛，老夫心甘情愿、欣喜若狂奉送给您这笔碎银子！这绝非为了酬谢您替老夫鞍前马后——老夫坚信您必定愿意成全老夫——而纯粹是聊表老夫对您个人人品才干的至高敬意！’
“他内心的邪火此刻已然狂烧到了病入膏肓的境地，老夫唯恐他脑子里的理智已然荡然无存。在他那充血的幻想眼帘之中，他仿佛早已亲眼望见了一大摞绝世克扎兰（Kzaran）神品地毯整整齐齐码放在大车之上随时准备起运；他仿佛望见他的死对头在远方的码头上气定神闲抢先截胡画押盖印、落袋为安。
“他浑身止不住瑟瑟发抖，再次死乞白赖向老夫兜售那份微薄的私人心意——那份区区2,000第纳尔（2000）的微薄打赏。
“‘长官，’老夫板起面孔，神色略显生硬地回敬道，‘老夫平生绝无在任何名目幌子之下私吞暗佣回扣的下作习惯！老夫的立场清清白白：倘若长官无法当面开出一份彻底碾压对手的坚实出价、且拿出铁证证明长官当真如自诩那般乃是本城底蕴最深厚的巨室——那老夫便只能径直回绝长官，同老夫的第一位主顾签约交割了！倘若长官当真能当场奉上一笔更为庞大的现银巨款，老夫或许尚可考虑成全长官。然而老夫初来乍到，对本城的虚实水深又从何知晓呢？”

===Original===

The thousand dinars of which I spoke are coined and in a wallet; I have them safe. With all the respect due to your age, I have no information upon your credit in this town. And I confess,’ I added in another tone, ‘that I *am* acquainted with your rival’s position, which is perhaps more solid than you think. I confess I think it would be simpler and to the better interest of our house if I were to go straight to him now and have done with it.’
“As I spoke thus the old man lost all reason. It was piteous to see one of his age and venerable hairs dancing and spluttering with excitement. He shook his fists in the air, he called to Heaven in shrill tones, he betrayed all the frenzy of the collector. He contrasted the mercantile motives of the unknown competitor with his own passion for pure art. He called Heaven to witness to the reality of his wealth, and at last in a sort of fury tore from his garments the jewels which ornamented them, thrust into my hand all the cash upon his person (it was in a leather bag, and amounted altogether to no more than 500 dinars), added to this a brooch of gold, which he dragged from his scarf, and said that if this instalment were not a sufficient proof of his good faith and credit he knew not how to move me.
“I shrugged my shoulders and suggested that instead of making so violent a protestation and at such risk to his fortune he should go back soberly to his house and return with an instrument of credit and two witnesses (as the law demands), while I awaited him patiently at that spot. I, at least, was in no haste and would honourably abide his return. He was off at a speed which I should never have thought possible at his age.
“I waited until he had turned the corner of a distant hedge of prickly pears, and not until he was quite out of sight did I gather the jewels, the coins, and the precious ornaments which in his haste he had thrown at my feet, and very rapidly betake myself in the opposite direction.
“Never was the Mercy of Allah more evidently extended. The plain was naked outside the town, the river perhaps a mile distant; my plight, as it might appear, desperate. I pinned the gold brooch to my cloak, I distributed the jewels openly upon various parts of my person, and I proceeded at a smart pace over the open plain towards the river.

===Chinese===

“‘老夫适才提及的那1,000第纳尔（1000）乃是硬邦邦铸造成型的金币现银，安安稳稳装在钱袋里；老夫手中攥着真金白银！恕老夫直言，念及长官的高龄，老夫对长官在本城的空头信用实在无从查考。况且老夫不得不坦白，’老夫换了一副极其冷酷的腔调继续施压，‘老夫对长官那位死对头的深厚底蕴早已了如指掌，人家的财力恐怕远比长官想象的还要扎实坚固万倍！老夫私心以为，最省心、且最合乎老夫东家至高利益的抉择，莫过于老夫此刻径直折返回去同那位掌柜完成签约落袋为安！’
“听闻老夫这番绝情之辞，那老汉彻底丧失了最后一丝理智！看着一个白发苍苍、本该德高望重的老者在老夫面前像个泼妇般手舞足蹈、唾沫横飞狂叫撒泼，情形委实令人扼腕叹息。他双拳在半空中疯狂挥舞，尖叫着对天赌咒发誓，将其身为病态收藏癖患者的歇斯底里宣泄得淋漓尽致。他将不知名竞争对手满身铜臭的市井动机，同自个儿对纯粹艺术的崇高痴恋进行了声泪俱下的对比控诉。他呼唤苍天为他的泼天身家作证；到了最后，他在一阵彻底疯魔的狂暴痉挛中，硬生生从身上的长袍上扯下了一切点缀其间的珍宝玉石，将随身携带的全部现银钱囊（装在一只鹿皮钱袋里，满打满算不过500第纳尔（500）上下）一股脑硬塞入老夫掌心，顺势从领口扯下一枚赤金打造的华美金胸针，嘶声嚎叫着说倘若这笔定金尚不足以昭示他的赤诚与信誉，他实在不知该如何撼动老夫这副铁石心肠了！
“老夫耸了耸肩，向他提议：与其这般失魂落魄大动肝火、冒着损毁自身健康的巨大凶险在此折腾，莫如安步当车折返回府邸，取来合法的正式票据文书以及律法硬性规定的两名（2）画押见证人，而老夫自当在原地耐心恭候大驾。老夫反正是个闲散之人，绝不急于一时，定会在原地信守诺言静候长官折返。那老家伙闻言拔腿就跑，其飞奔的速度在老夫看来根本不像一个年过花甲的老翁所能达到的极限！
“老夫一直耐心静候，直到他仓皇的身影彻底消失在远处一道仙人掌树篱的拐角尽头；老夫直到确认四下再无半个人影，方才慢条斯理弯下腰来，将他适才在疯癫之中抛洒在老夫脚边的珍宝、金币与无价配饰悉数捡拾齐备，随后以风驰电掣般的绝顶脚力径直朝着截然相反的方向疾驰狂奔！
“承蒙真主的仁慈（Mercy of Allah）！老夫平生从未见过神明之光竟显现得如此耀眼夺目！城外是一片空旷平坦的荒原，奔涌的大河距老夫约莫有一英里之遥；在世人眼里，老夫当下的处境委实命悬一线、凶险到了极致。然而老夫神色自若，将那枚纯金胸针从容别在长袍翻领之上，将各色宝玉明目张胆挂在周身各处，随后迈着健步如飞的轻快步伐，横穿空旷的原野径直奔向大河河滩。”

===Original===

It was with the greatest joy that I found upon its bank two fishermen about to set sail and proceed downstream to sea. Their presence inspired me with a plan for escape.
“I chatted negligently with them (still keeping one eye upon the distant house of my aged but excitable friend). At last with a light laugh I offered one of them a piece of gold, saying that I should be pleased to try the novel experience of a little cruise. The fisherman, who was quite unacquainted with so much wealth, and seemed somewhat awestruck, gave me some grand title or other, and promised me very good sport with the fish and a novel entertainment. But even as he and his companion pushed out from shore I turned in my seat on the deck and perceived in the plain a rising dust which betrayed the approach of the merchant with his witnesses and a company of his slaves.
“Suddenly changing my expression from one of pleased though wearied expectancy to one of acute alarm, I shouted to my new companions: ‘Push away for your lives, and stretch your sail to its utmost! These are the commissioners sent by the caliph to reassess and tax all fishing-boats upon a new valuation! Already had they seized three upon the beach when I left and found you here!’
“At these words the worthy fellows were inspired by a fear even greater than my own. They manfully pushed into the swiftest part of the current, and, though a smart breeze was blowing, hoisted every inch of the sail, so that the boat ran with her gunwale upon the very edge of the water and was indeed dangerously pressed. But I had the satisfaction of seeing the merchant and his retinue vainly descending the riverbank, at perhaps one-half our speed, calling down curses upon us, threatening with their fists, shouting their public titles of authority, their menaces of the law, and in every way confirming my excellent pair of fishermen in the story I had told them.
“It was a pleasant thing to loll on deck under the heat of the day, toying with the valuable ornaments I had so recently acquired and lazily watching my companions as they sweated at the halyards, or alternatively glancing along towards the shore at the little group of disappointed people which fell so rapidly behind us as we bowled down the tide. Soon their features were no longer plain, then their figures could scarcely be distinguished.

===Chinese===

“在河岸边，老夫以无以复加的狂喜骤然撞见了两位正欲扬帆起航、顺流出海打渔的当地渔夫！他们的存在犹如神迹降临，瞬间在老夫心头点燃了金蝉脱壳的逃生妙策！
“老夫漫不经心地凑上前去同他们搭讪闲扯（一只眼珠子始终死死斜睨着那位年迈而癫狂的老友府邸的方向）。最后，老夫发出一阵轻快的朗笑，随手向其中一人抛出了一枚耀眼的金币，声称老夫颇有雅兴体验一番出海巡游的乘风破浪之趣。那渔民平生何曾见过如此泼天的真金白银，当场吓得目瞪口呆肃然起敬，嘴里不住给老夫扣上各类显赫尊贵的官衔头衔，拍着胸脯保证定要让老夫捕得满舱鲜鱼、尽享不世之乐。然而就在他和同伴合力将渔船推离河滩浅水的一刹那，老夫在甲板上霍然回首，只见远处原野之上黄沙漫天、烟尘滚滚——那正是那老掌柜纠集了见证人以及整整一队精壮家奴正气势汹汹杀奔而来！
“老夫脸上的神情瞬间由百无聊赖的惬意企盼，骤然剧变为了惊骇欲绝的滔天恐慌！老夫扯着嗓子冲着身旁新结识的渔夫同伴失声尖叫：‘快划！逃命啊！把所有的风帆全部拉满！追上来的是哈里发王上派来清丈重税的大内钦差特使！他们要对全天下的所有渔船按全新估值抄没征税！老夫适才在海滩上眼睁睁瞧见他们强行扣押了三艘渔船，老夫这才拼死逃到你们这里报信啊！’
“听闻老夫这番晴天霹雳般的‘噩耗’，这两个老实巴交的渔汉心头的恐惧瞬间比老夫还要浓烈万倍！他们使出了吃奶的力气奋力将渔船撑入水流最湍急的江心主航道；尽管此刻江面上正刮着凌厉的逆风劲风，他们却发了疯似地将每一寸帆布拉到了极致！整艘渔船倾斜得船舷几乎贴着滚滚波涛狂飙突进，险象环生到了极致！然而老夫却极其惬意地目睹着那位老富商带着他的恶奴家丁徒劳地沿着河岸一路狂奔追赶，其追击的速度满打满算不过只有我们船速的一半；他们嘴里喷吐着滔天诅咒，在岸上疯狂挥舞着拳头，扯着嗓子叫嚣着自个儿的官衔官威与律法严惩——凡此种种垂死挣扎，恰恰在每一个维度上百分之百印证了老夫适才向这两位质朴渔夫编造的绝妙谎言！
“在骄阳似火的晌午时分，老夫极其惬意地仰面横卧在甲板之上，手里漫不经心把玩着适才白捡的珍奇异宝，一边懒洋洋瞧着老夫的两个同伴在升降索旁累得汗流浃背气喘吁吁，一边冷眼瞥向渐渐远去的岸边那一小撮急得跳脚的跳梁小丑。很快，他们的面容便在水汽中模糊不清，紧接着连那一道道晃动的身影亦彻底隐没在了天际线尽头。”

===Original===

The last impression conveyed to me was of some little very distant thing, stamping with impotent rage and shaking wild arms against the sky. I could not but deplore so grievous a lapse in dignity in one so venerable.
“When we were well away from the neighbourhood of the city I asked the fishermen whither they were bound; to which they answered that their business was only to cruise about outside and fish during the night, returning at dawn with their catch. ‘Would it not be better,’ I suggested, ‘seeing that these rapacious fellows will hang about for a day or so, to carry me to some town of your acquaintance along the coast where the reigning powers do not suffer from the tyranny of Baghdad? For my part I am free to travel where I will, and the prospect of a change pleases me. I shall be happy,’ said I, ‘to reward the sacrifice of your catch with fifty dinars.’
“At the prospect of much further wealth the fishermen were at once convinced: they sang in the lightness of their hearts, and for three days and three nights we sped down the Gulf, passing bleak mountains and deserted rocky promontories, until upon the fourth day we came to a town the like of which I had never seen.
“ ‘Shall we land here?’ said I.
“ ‘No,’ said the fishermen, ‘for it is in a manner within the caliph’s dominions, and perhaps that accursed tax of which you spoke will be levied here also.’
“ ‘You know better than I,’ replied I thoughtfully, standing for a moment in affected perplexity. ‘Let me, however, land in your little boat. I have a passion for new places. I will come out to you again after the hour of the midday prayers, while you stand in the offing.’
“To this arrangement they readily consented. I rowed to the land, and when I had reached the shore I was pleased to see my fearful hosts quite three miles out upon the hot and shimmering sea. Gazing at them, I hope with charity, and certainly with interest, I pushed the little boat adrift (for I had no reason to return to those poor people) and made my way inland. I disposed of my jewellery at prices neither low nor high with local merchants. I preserved the old fellow’s golden brooch, which I imagined (for I am a trifle weak and superstitious) might bring me good fortune, and when all my transactions were accomplished I counted my total capital, and found myself in possession of no less than 1,500 dinars.

===Chinese===

“在老夫眼帘中收拢的最后一幅凄凉残影，乃是一个极其遥远模糊的小黑点，在岸边绝望而无能地跺着脚，冲着苍穹疯狂挥舞着干瘪的双臂。对于一个这般年逾古稀的长者竟如此斯文扫地、丧失起码的尊严风度，老夫委实忍不住在心底由衷替他扼腕叹息。
“待渔船彻底驶离了重镇地界的大海深处，老夫探问渔夫他们意欲驶向何方；渔汉们答复说他们平生的营生不过是在近海游弋夜捕，待天明拂晓时分再载着鱼获折返回港。‘二位好汉，’老夫当即进言建议，‘既然那帮贪得无厌的鹰犬税吏必将在港口蹲守数日不散，二位何不索性将老夫顺道捎带送往沿海某处二位熟悉的异国通商口岸？在彼处当地的诸侯藩王向来不吃巴格达（Baghdad）朝廷暴政那一套！至于老夫自个儿，老夫向来随遇而安天高任鸟飞，换个新环境正合老夫心意。老夫极其乐意，’老夫说道，‘以整整50第纳尔（50）巨款，作为补偿二位今夜弃网断渔的无上厚礼！’
“面对如此横财诱惑，渔夫们当场便被彻底说服了：他们心花怒放引吭高歌，我们的大船载着欢声笑语在波斯湾的碧波上破浪疾驰了整整三天三夜（3），掠过荒凉险峻的孤峰与寸草不生的高耸海岬；直到第四天（4）清晨，一座老夫平生从未见识过的壮丽异域重镇豁然耸立在眼前。
“‘我们可否在此靠岸？’老夫问道。
“‘万万不可，’渔夫们惊恐地回绝道，‘因彼处在名分上多少仍属哈里发管辖的疆域，天知道长官提及的那道该死的渔船重税是否亦已蔓延至此地。’
“‘二位所言极是，’老夫故作高深莫测地沉吟片刻，装出一副左右为难的神态，‘既然如此，且容老夫划着二位船上的小舢板独自登岸。老夫平生嗜好探访异域风土人情。待到午祷时分老夫自会划船折返回来同二位汇合，届时二位只需将大船停泊在离岸外海静候即可。’
“对于这套安排他们毫不犹豫欣然应允。老夫奋力摇橹划向水岸；待老夫双脚踏上坚实的海滩陆地，老夫欣喜地望见老夫那两位胆战心惊的东道主已然将大船退到了足足3英里（3）开外水汽蒸腾的滚烫海面之上！老夫怀着慈悲之心——当然更伴随着浓厚的兴味——深情眺望了他们良久，顺手一脚将那条小舢板推入滚滚波涛任其随波逐流（因老夫压根毫无理由再折返回那帮可怜虫身边），随即大步流星向内陆重镇挺进。老夫在当地巴扎以不高不低的公道市价，将随身搜罗的珠玉细软全盘出手换成了现银；唯独那枚老家伙的金胸针被老夫精心保留了下来，老夫在心底隐隐觉得（因老夫骨子里多少有点迷信软肋）此物必能给老夫带来滔天鸿运。待所有的交割清算悉数告罄，老夫闭门盘点了随身总资产：老夫惊喜地发现老夫名下坐拥的现银，足足高达整整1,500第纳尔（1500）之巨！”

===Original===

The cold of the evening had come by the time my accounts were settled and the strings of my pouch were drawn. I set myself under an arbour where a delicious fountain played in the light of the setting sun, which shone over the waters of the sea, and drinking some local beverage the name of which I knew not, but the taste and effect of which were equally pleasing, I reflected upon my increase of fortune.
“ ‘You left home, Mahmoud,’ said I to myself, ‘with one hundred dinars, of which your excellent and careful father deprived himself rather than see you face the world unarmed, or himself receive the bastinado. You have been gone from home a week; you are perhaps some 800 miles from your native city; your capital has been multiplied fifteen-fold, and so far you may look with an eager courage towards the further adventures of your life, for very clearly the Mercy of Allah is upon you.’ ”
At this moment a nasal hooting from the neighbourhood turret warned the company to turn their thoughts to heaven. The boys, who had sat fascinated by their uncle’s recital, knew that the end of their entertainment had come. The third son of the surgeon was therefore impatient to exclaim (as he hurriedly did): “But, dear uncle, though we see that a certain chance favoured you, and not only your native talents, yet we do not perceive how all this led to any main road to fortune.”
“My boy,” said the merchant Mahmoud, pensively stroking his beard and gazing vacuously over the heads of the youngsters. “I do not pretend to unfold you any such plan. Have I not told you that did such a plan exist all would be in possession of it? I am but retailing you in my humble fashion the steps by which one merchant in this city has been raised by the infinite goodness of the Merciful (His name be adored!) from poverty to riches.﻿ ﻿… But the call for prayer has already been heard and we must part. Upon this same day of next week, shortly after the last of the public executions has been bungled, you shall again come and hear me recite the next chapter of my varied career.”

===Chinese===

“待老夫清结了所有账目并结结实实扎紧了钱袋绳口之时，暮色苍茫中的阵阵清凉已然扑面而来。老夫在海边一处幽雅的凉亭藤架下落座，凉亭前一泓甘泉在落日熔金的余晖中欢快喷涌，夕阳将整片浩瀚海面染得一片赤金；老夫端起一杯老夫虽不知其名号、但滋味与效用皆极其美妙宜人的当地佳酿，细细回味着老夫财富的惊天飞跃。
“‘马哈茂德啊马哈茂德，’老夫自言自语道，‘你当年离开家门之时，怀揣的不过是那区区100第纳尔（100）盘缠——那是你那位可敬而精明的先父宁可咬牙割爱、亦不愿坐视你赤手空拳闯荡江湖或自个儿惨遭官府杖笞的买命钱！如今你离家满打满算不过短短一周（7）光阴；你身处之地距你的桑梓故土足足有800英里（800）之遥；你的初始本钱已然不可思议地暴涨了整整15倍（15）！走到今日这步田地，你尽可怀揣着无畏的胆魄去拥抱余生更为波澜壮阔的惊天大冒险了，因摆在眼前的铁证昭示得再明白不过：真主的仁慈（Mercy of Allah）自始至终正沉甸甸笼罩在你的头顶之上！’
就在彼时彼刻，邻近塔楼上传来了一阵刺耳的鼻音长啸呼拜，提醒满城信众将凡俗心智转向至高无上的天庭。被叔父生平传奇深深震撼迷醉的少年们，心知今日的筵席已然走到了尾声。做医生的三侄子因而急不可耐地抢先高呼（他确实仓皇脱口而出）：“可是，亲爱的叔父，虽说我们看出了某种虚无缥缈的横运眷顾了您，且您身上确实展现出了天生的才智，然而我们却依然参悟不透：这一切究竟是如何踏上一条通往泼天富贵的康庄大道的呢？”
“我的孩子，”大商人马哈茂德若有所思地捋着修长的胡须，双眸空洞茫然地凝视着少年们头顶上方的虚空，温和地说道，“老夫从未吹嘘过老夫能向你们传授任何此类通盘秘笈。老夫难道未曾告诫过你们：若当真存在这等捷径秘笈，天下凡人岂非早已人手一份了么？老夫不过是以老夫卑微粗浅的言辞，向你们如实拆解本城的一个市井小商人，究竟是如何凭借普慈特慈真主（愿祂的圣名受万世赞颂！）的无量恩典，一步步从赤贫如洗跃升至万贯富豪的真实台阶罢了！……然而唤礼之声已然响彻云霄，我们必须就此作别。在下周的同一天，待到市集最后一名死囚的斩首公开行刑再次被笨拙的刽子手搞砸之后，你们且再到老夫府上来，亲耳聆听老夫跌宕起伏生平传奇的下一卷华章吧。”

## III *Al-Tawajin*, or “The Pipkins” / 第三章 陶锅
===Original===

On the appointed day of the next week, when, with the hour of public executions, the noonday amusements of the city come to an end, and the citizens betake themselves to the early afternoon’s repose, the seven boys were once more seated in the presence of their uncle, whom they discovered in a radiant humour.
He welcomed them so warmly that they imagined for a moment he might be upon the point of offering them sherbet, sweetmeats, or even money; they were undeceived, however, when the excellent but extremely wealthy old man, drawing his purse lovingly through his fingers, ordered to have poured out for each of them by a slave a further draught of delicious cold water, put himself at his ease for a long story, and resumed his tale:
“You will remember, my delightful nephews,” he said, “how I found myself in the hermit’s hut without a friend in the world, and with a capital of no more than twelve thousand dinars which I had carried thither in a sack upon donkey-back. Indeed, it was entirely due to the Mercy of Allah that my small capital was even as large as it was: for had the merchant in the bazaar discovered the pearls to be false he would not only have offered me far less but might possibly, after having disposed of the pearls, have given me over to the police. As it was, Heaven had been kind to me, though not bountiful, and I still had to bethink me what to do next if I desired to increase my little treasure.
“Taking leave, therefore, of the good hermit, I pressed into his hand a small brass coin the superscription of which was unknown to me and which I therefore feared I might have some difficulty in passing. I assured my kind host that it was a coin of the second Caliph Omar and of value very far superior to any modern gold piece of a similar size. As the hermit, like many other saintly men, was ignorant of letters, his gratitude knew no bounds. He dismissed me with a blessing so long and complicated that I cannot but ascribe to it some part of the good fortune which next befell me.

===Chinese===

在次周约定的吉日，当闹市公开行刑的时辰敲响、全城白昼的游乐盛宴画上句号、满城百姓纷纷退入内室享受午后小憩之际，七个（7）少年再次围坐在叔父膝下；他们惊喜地发现叔父今日神清气爽、喜气洋洋。
他对侄儿们的迎候是如此热情洋溢，以致少年们在一瞬间甚至痴心妄想，以为叔父或许当真要慷慨赏赐他们冰镇果露、精致蜜饯点心、甚至是白花花的现银；然而当这位可敬却腰缠万贯的老人，一边深情地用修长的指尖摩挲着鼓囊囊的皮钱袋，一边吩咐黑奴给每个孩子再次倒满一大碗甘冽沁脾的冰凉冷水，随后自个儿斜倚在软榻上准备娓娓道来时，孩子们心中的幻梦顿时破灭了。老人接着讲述他的发家传奇：
“我可爱聪颖的侄儿们，”他开口说道，“想必你们还记得，老夫孤身一人流落在苦修隐士的荒郊土棚之中，举目无亲，身旁唯有一头小毛驴背上驮着的麻袋里装着的那区区12,000第纳尔（12000）盘缠。平心而论，老夫这笔微薄的启动本钱之所以能有如此数目，全盘皆是仰仗真主的仁慈（Mercy of Allah）！因倘若当年巴扎市集的珠宝掌柜当场识破那串珍珠乃是伪劣假货，他非但会把价码压得极低，甚至很有可能在将珍珠据为己有之后反咬一口将老夫扭送官府法办！无论如何，上苍待老夫虽说谈不上泼天恩赐，倒也算得上慈悲庇佑；而老夫若想让手中的微薄积蓄继续滚雪球般壮大，便不得不苦思冥想下一步该迈向何方。
“于是老夫同那位善良的苦修神僧依依惜别；临别之际，老夫将一枚老夫连铭文图样都辨认不清、因而唯恐在市集上难以脱手兑换的微薄小黄铜铜板硬塞入他枯槁的手心。老夫信誓旦旦向老夫善良的东道主保证：此乃第二任哈里发欧麦尔时期的绝世古币，其真实市价远超当今天下任何一枚同等分量的赤金金币！正如世间诸多圣僧隐士那般，这位苦修士亦是个目不识丁的文盲，因而感动得热泪盈眶不知该如何报答。他为老夫献上了一段冗长繁复得令人咋舌的无上祈福祷词，以致老夫不得不将随后降临于老夫头顶的滔天横运，相当一部分归功于这道神圣的祝福！”

===Original===

“For you must know that when, after laying in stores at a neighbouring village, I had driven my donkey forward for nearly a week over barren and uninhabited mountains, and when I had nearly exhausted my provision of dried cakes and wine (a beverage which our religion allows us to consume when no one is by), I was delighted to come upon a fertile valley entirely closed in by high, precipitous cliffs save at one issue, where a rough track led from this enchanted region to the outer world. In this valley I discovered, to my astonishment, manners to be so primitive or intelligence so low that the whole art of money-dealing was ignored by the inhabitants and by the very governors themselves.
“The king (who, I am glad to say, was of the faithful) had, indeed, promulgated laws against certain forms of fraud which he imagined to be denounced in the Koran; but these were of so infantile a character that a man of judgment could very easily avoid them in any plans he might frame for the people’s betterment and his own. The population consisted entirely of soldiers and rustics, among all of whom not one could be discovered capable of calculating with justice a compound interest for ten years.
“Under these circumstances my only difficulty lay in choosing what form my first enterprise should take. After a little thought I decided that what we call in Baghdad an amalgamation of competing interests would be no bad beginning.
“I began with due caution by investing a couple of thousand dinars in the merchandise of a potter who had recently died and whose widow needed ready cash to satisfy the sacred demands of the dead. She spent the money in the ornamentation of his tomb, with which unproductive expenditure the foolish woman was in no small degree concerned.”
Here the eldest of the nephews interrupted Mahmoud to ask, most respectfully, why with a capital of twelve thousand dinars he had used but two, and why he had begun his experiment upon the petty business of a poor widow.
“My son,” said his uncle affectionately, “you do well to ask these questions. They show a reasoned interest in the great art of getting. Well then, as to the smallness of my beginning, it was, I hope, due to humility. For ostentation is hateful. But a good deed is never thrown away﻿—and how useful I found this reserve of ten thousand dinars (which I had in my meekness kept aside) you shall soon learn.

===Chinese===

“你们可得知道：老夫在邻近村落采办了干粮草料之后，赶着老夫的小毛驴在寸草不生的荒凉群山间跋涉了近乎一周（7）光阴；当随身携带的干面饼与葡萄佳酿（在我们这门正信宗教里，只要四下无人窥见，信徒是被恩准尽情痛饮此等甘霖的）快要见底之时，老夫欣喜若狂地撞见了一处四面皆被巍峨险峻的绝壁悬崖死死封锁的肥美世外桃源——唯有一条崎岖陡峭的羊肠绝径，将这处人间仙境同外界大千世界勾连在一起。在这座幽闭的峡谷之中，老夫极其惊愕地洞察到：此地民风淳朴到了蛮荒的境地，或者说市井智力低下到了令人发指的程度，以致整个金钱借贷与金融运作的高深艺术，在当地平民乃至官府牧守长老的心目中，竟全然是一片闻所未闻的盲区空白！
“当地的国王陛下（老夫十分欣慰地告诉你们，彼亦是一位虔诚的正信君王）确实曾颁布过严刑峻法，严厉打击某些他认定在《古兰经》中遭受诅咒的诈伪营生；然而那些禁令幼稚可笑到了极点，任何一个稍有眼界的精明之士，在谋划造福百姓兼肥自个儿腰包的宏伟蓝图时，皆能易如反掌将它们玩弄于股掌之间。全境人口清一色由大头兵与泥腿子农夫组成，在满城黎民之中，你竟挑不出一个能够公允精准计算出十年（10）复利本息的明白人！
“在此等天赐良机之下，老夫唯一的踌躇仅在于究竟该以何种营生作为老夫开天辟地的第一步棋。经过一番深思熟虑，老夫断定：我们在巴格达（Baghdad）商界称之为‘恶性竞争实体的并购重组’，委实不失为一个绝妙的开端。
“老夫打起十二分审慎，首先拿出了区区两千第纳尔（2000），全盘吃下了一位刚刚撒手人寰的老陶匠留下的满窑存货与家当——老陶匠的未亡人孤孀急需现银以操办亡夫神圣的丧葬典仪。那个愚蠢的妇道人家将这笔银子全砸在给亡夫修葺华而不实的豪华陵墓之上，整日为这笔毫无生产力的挥霍开销操碎了心。”
讲到此处，最年长的大侄子极其恭敬地打断了马哈茂德的话头，探问道：叔父当年手握整整12,000第纳尔（12000）巨资，为何仅仅动用了区区两千块（2000），又为何偏偏相中了一个孤苦寡妇的微末作坊来开启宏图大业呢？
“我的孩子，”叔父极其慈爱地答道，“你问得极妙！这昭示出你对敛财生财的无上大道怀有一份理性的研求之志。至于老夫当年开局之所以如此收敛微薄，老夫自忖是源于谦逊的美德；盖因招摇过市向来令人生厌。然而善行绝不会白费——至于老夫在心性克制之下悄然雪藏的那整整10,000第纳尔（10000）后手储备金日后发挥了何等惊天动地的神威，你稍后便能领会！”

===Original===

“As to why I began operations in the kiln of this poor widow, it was because I have ever loved the little ones of this world and aided them to my best endeavour. This charitable action also turned out to be wise, as such actions often do; for I could thus proceed at first unnoticed and begin my new adventures without exciting any embarrassing attention.
“I continued to live in the same small hut I had hired on my arrival, under the floor of which I kept my modest capital; and I put it about, as modesty demanded, that I was almost destitute.
“As it was indifferent to me for the moment whether I obtained a return upon this paltry investment or no, I was able to sell my wares at very much the same sum as they had cost me, and as I had bought the whole stock cheap, that sum was less than the cost of manufacture. There was a considerable store of pipkins in the old sheds, and while I sold them off at charitable rates (very disconcerting to other merchants), I had time to consider my next step.
“Upon this next step I soon determined. When, with due delay, my original stock of pipkins had been sold, I purchased a small consignment of clay, I relit the fires in the kiln, I hired a couple of starving potters, and I began to manufacture.
“The fame of my very cheap pipkins had spread, as was but natural, and secured me an increasing number of customers for my newly made wares. But I thought it wrong to debauch the peasants by selling them their pots under cost price any longer. I was constrained by the plainest rule of duty to raise my prices to the cost of manufacture﻿—though no more, keeping justice as my guiding star. For, depend upon it, my dear nephews, in business as in every other walk of life an exact rectitude alone can lead us to the most dazzling rewards.
“This price of mine was still lower than that of all the other pipkin-makers, who had been accustomed from immemorial time to the base idea of profit, and were in a perpetual surmise what secret powers I had to permit me such quotations. But I made no mystery of the affair. I allowed all my friends to visit my simple factory and I explained to their satisfaction how organization and a close attention to costings were sufficient to account for my prosperity.
“Still, as my sales continued to grow, new doubts arose, and with them, I am glad to say, new respect for my skill in affairs.

===Chinese===

“至于老夫为何偏偏相中这位贫苦寡妇的陶窑作为破局之所，全因老夫平生最怜恤这尘世间的苦难卑微之辈，且向来乐意竭尽所能扶危济困。这项积德行善之举事后亦证明英明至极，正如世间诸多善举往往皆有善报那般：老夫借此得以在神不知鬼不觉中悄然落子，开启老夫崭新的商海冒险，而丝毫不曾引来任何令人局促不安的市井侧目。
“老夫依旧寄居在老夫进城时租下的那间逼仄土坯茅屋之中，老夫那笔微薄的启动老本就埋在屋内的泥地泥砖之下；老夫对外大肆放出风声，正如谦逊美德所要求的那样，宣称老夫是个赤贫如洗的穷汉。
“由于彼时彼刻老夫压根不在乎能否从这笔微不足道的投资里榨取什么即期回报，老夫索性以近乎进货成本的白菜价，大肆抛售老夫名下的这批陶器瓦罐；且因老夫当初吃进全盘存货时本就狠狠压低了行市，这笔售价甚至远低于正规的工场制造工本！破旧的库棚里堆积着大批陶锅，在老夫以近乎慈善救济的骨折价将它们大甩卖（这把当地的其他陶器掌柜打得晕头转向措手不及）的同时，老夫亦赢得了充裕的光阴去谋划老夫的下一步杀招。
“老夫很快便敲定了下一步。待到老夫手中的原始陶锅存货在一番蓄意的拖延放量中终于售罄之时，老夫出资采办了一小批陶土原料，重新点燃了窑炉的熊熊烈火，以极廉价的工钱雇佣了两位快要饿死的落魄陶匠，老夫正式破土开工大搞生产制造了！
“老夫出产的陶锅物美价廉的名号不胫而走，这是人之常情，极为自然地为老夫新出窑的器皿引来了源源不断激增的汹涌主顾。然而老夫认定：继续以低于工本的亏本价向农夫兜售陶罐，无异于纵容并腐蚀了淳朴乡民的品性！在最纯粹的天职戒律约束之下，老夫顺应道义将售价精准调高到了工场制造成本线——分文不多加，全凭公平正义作为老夫灵魂的指路明灯！因切记这一点，我亲爱的侄儿们：在商海之中正如在人世间的任何其他行当一般，唯有绝对无暇的正气清白，方能将我们引向最璀璨耀眼的无上赏报！
“即便老夫调价之后，老夫开出的价码依然远低于全城所有其他陶锅作坊掌柜；那帮人自古以来便沉溺于市井赚取暴利的下作习气不可自拔，整日为老夫究竟施展了何等妖术秘法方能开出如此低廉的报价而陷入了无穷无尽的无端猜忌。然而老夫对此绝无半点藏着掖着：老夫大方容许所有的挚友同僚随意参观老夫质朴无华的作坊工场，老夫向他们条分缕析极尽透彻地阐明：严密科学的生产组织与对生产成本细致入微的严苛管控，便足以全盘解释老夫欣欣向荣的发家之谜！
“然而随着老夫的销路如日中天节节暴涨，坊间不可避免滋生出崭新的疑虑，而伴随着这股疑虑同时涌现的，老夫十分欣慰地告诉你们，乃是同侪对老夫通天商业手腕由衷升腾而起的崇高敬畏！”

===Original===

“The simple folk wondered by what art I had contrived so difficult a financial operation, but as it was traditional among them that one who sold goods cheap was a benefactor to the community, my action was lauded, my fame spread, and the number of my customers continually increased.
“You will not be slow to perceive, my dear boys, that my competitors in the bazaar, being compelled, to compete with my ruinous prices, were all embarrassed, and that the less attentive or privileged soon began to fall into financial difficulties, the first of course being those who were the most renowned among these simpletons for their cunning, their silence, their lying, and their commercial skill in general. These, as they were perpetually trying new combinations to discover or to defeat my supposed schemes, were an easy prey. Even the straightforward fellows who knew of no art more subtle than the charging of ten percent above cost price, and who did not play into my hands by any wearisome financial strategy, began to be roped into my net as the area of my operations spread. For when I had acquired, at a calculated loss, a good half of the pottery business in this sequestered paradise, I could, by what is known as the fluctuation of the market (but I will not confuse you with technical terms), put my remaining competitors into alternate fevers of panic and expectation very destructive to a sound business judgment.
“Upon one day I would declare that a large consignment of pottery being about to reach me, I could sell pipkins at half the usual price. Pipkins fell heavily, and I bought through my agent every pipkin I could lay hold of. The supposed consignment, I would then put about, had been broken to atoms by an avalanche which had overwhelmed the caravan at the very boundaries of the state. Price leapt upward, and as I was the author of the rumour I was also the first to take advantage of the rise in price. But the very moment, my dear nephews, that my sluggish competitors attempted to follow suit the market would, oddly enough, fluctuate again in a *downward* direction.
“Upon a certain morning when one Abdullah (who was my boon companion and the next merchant in importance to myself) decided to mark his best pipkins at ten dinars the dozen I happened most prudently to have offered my own at eight and a half dinars to my favourite customers.

===Chinese===

“这群淳朴老实的土包子百思不得其解，老夫究竟施展了何等出神入化的神技，方能运筹出如此精妙绝伦的高难度资本运作；然而在他们的古老传统习俗里，凡是能廉价贱卖百工器物之人皆被奉为利国利民的世间大善人，因而老夫的善举被满城传颂，老夫的威名如日中天，登门光顾老夫字号的主顾人流亦呈现出井喷之势。
“你们想必不难参悟，我亲爱的孩子们：老夫在巴扎市集上的竞争同行们，被逼无奈只得跟进老夫这等伤敌一千自损八百的毁灭性低价，个个皆陷入了捉襟见肘的财务困境；而那些平日里疏于打理、或是缺乏特权后台的小作坊，很快便迎来了资金链断裂的灭顶绝境。首当其冲沦为牺牲品的，恰恰正是这帮蠢伯之中素以诡诈刁钻、城府深沉、满嘴谎言、以及所谓‘商海老油条’著称的行家里手！这帮家伙整日挖空心思算计谋划，妄图窥探并攻破老夫那套子虚乌有的‘商业阴谋’，因而最容易沦为老夫盘中的下酒菜。甚至连那些平生只知在制造成本之上老老实实加价10%（10%）行市溢价、从不屑于同老夫玩弄任何令人昏昏欲睡的金融诡计的本分老实掌柜，随着老夫商业版图的狂飙突进，亦不可避免地被一张大网一网打尽！盖因在老夫以精准计算的账面亏损全盘吞下了这处世外桃源整整半壁江山的陶器产业之后，老夫便能凭借商界所谓的‘行市剧烈波动’（老夫就不拿晦涩的行话来叨扰你们清修了），令老夫剩下的所有竞争同行在惊恐万状的恐慌与虚妄亢奋的期盼之间如坐过山车般来回剧烈颠簸——这对于任何理智健康的商业研判而言，皆是致命的毁灭性打击！
“某一天，老夫会对外公开宣告：由于一大批大宗陶器货队即将抵港，老夫愿以寻常半价倾销陶锅！陶锅行市瞬间断崖暴跌，而老夫则暗中通过隐秘经纪人，将市面上所有能搜刮到的陶锅按白菜价全盘暗中扫货吃进！紧随其后，老夫立刻四下放风宣称：那支假想中的庞大运输驼队，在边境关隘不幸遭遇了一场惊天雪崩，整批瓷器全被砸成了齑粉！陶锅价格瞬间应声暴涨跃上九重天；而由于老夫自始至终皆是这场谣言的始作俑者，老夫顺理成章亦成了第一个在高位套现狂揽暴利之人！然而我亲爱的侄儿们，就在老夫那些反应迟钝的竞争同行们终于咂摸过味来妄图跟风跟进之时，离奇古怪的是，整个大盘行市却冷不丁再次朝着‘暴跌’的深渊一路狂泻！
“某天清晨，一位名叫阿卜杜拉（Abdullah）的掌柜（彼乃是老夫酒席上的莫逆挚友，亦是城中仅次于老夫的二号陶业巨头）横下一条心，决定将他作坊里最顶级的上等陶锅挂牌定为每打10第纳尔（10）；而老夫恰好在此之前极其‘未雨绸缪’地向老夫最核心的老主顾们，开出了每打仅8.5第纳尔的极度诱人底价！”

===Original===

“And all this while I lived upon my hidden hoard.
“Poor Abdullah came to me in a sweat, very early the next morning, and after some meaningless compliments and many pauses, asked me to go into partnership. ‘For’ (said he) ‘though he admitted he had not my capacities, yet he had a long experience in the trade, a large connection and many influential friends in the allied lines of pipkin brokerage, pipkin insurance, pipkin discount, pipkin remainders, and﻿—a most important branch﻿—the buying and selling of imaginary pipkins.’
“He could﻿—he anxiously assured me﻿—be of great service as an ally, but he was free to confess that if he continued as he was he would be ruined; for, to tell the truth, he had already come to the end of his resources and had not a dinar in the house.
“I heard him out with a grave and sympathetic countenance, heaving deep sighs when he touched upon his fears, nodding and smiling when he spoke of his advantages, patting him affectionately when he professed his devotion to myself, and assuming a look of anguish when he spoke of his approaching ruin.
“But when he had concluded﻿—almost in tears﻿—I told him in tones somewhat slower and graver than my ordinary, that I had one fixed principle in life, bequeathed me by my dear father, now in Paradise, *never to enter into partnership*; no, not with my nearest and dearest, but ever to remain alone in my transactions. I frankly admitted that this made me a poor man and would keep me poor. It would be greatly to my advantage, in the despicable goods of this world, to have at my disposal Abdullah’s marvellous experience, his great array of family and business connections (to which my wretched birth could make no claim), and above all his genius for following the market. But the goods of this world were perishable﻿—especially earthenware﻿—and the sacred pledge given to my sainted parent counted more with me than all the baked mud in the world.
“As I thus spoke Abdullah’s breast heaved with tempestuous sobs, provoked by the affecting example of my filial piety, but also, I fear, by the black prospect of his own future.
“I could not bear to witness his distress. I hastened to relieve it. Though my vow (I said) forbade me solemnly to enter into partnership, yet I could be of service to him in another manner.

===Chinese===

“而在此期间，老夫全凭地砖底下深埋的那笔巨额私藏老本支撑周转！
“可怜的阿卜杜拉在翌日黎明破晓时分便满头大汗、气喘吁吁地跌撞闯入老夫房中。在一通毫无营养的谄媚客套与无数次尴尬语塞之后，他苦苦哀求老夫同他结为合伙同盟。‘盖因’（他自陈道）‘尽管他自愧弗如老夫的通天神技，但他平生在这一行当浸淫多年，人脉广博，且在陶锅经纪、陶锅保险、陶锅票据贴现、陶锅尾货清仓、以及——最为至关重要的核心支柱——虚拟陶锅现货期货倒买倒卖等各色关联产业之中，坐拥大批手眼通天的生死至交！’
“他万分焦灼地向老夫拍着胸脯保证：一旦结盟他必能成为老夫的左膀右臂；然而他亦不得不坦白招供：倘若局势继续这般恶化下去，他非得落得倾家荡产不可；因老实说，他手头的银根早已彻底断绝，家中甚至翻不出半文第纳尔现银了！
“老夫以极尽庄严肃穆与悲天悯人的神态耐着性子听完了他的倾诉：每逢他触及内心的恐惧，老夫便报以深沉沉痛的叹息；每逢他炫耀自身的长处，老夫便含笑赞许频频点头；每逢他赌咒发誓对老夫的赤胆忠心，老夫便慈爱地轻抚他的肩头；而每逢他哀叹大难临头的破产末路，老夫脸上更是恰到好处地流露出如丧考妣般的无尽痛苦！
“然而待他倾诉完毕——眼眶里早已噙满了绝望的泪水——老夫以一种远比往常更为迟缓、更为威严的腔调告知他：老夫平生有一条铭心刻骨、坚不可摧的行事铁律，乃是先父临终升入天国前夕给老夫留下的神圣遗训——‘平生绝不与任何人合伙立约！’；绝不，哪怕是面对老夫最挚爱的手足至亲，老夫在商海搏击中亦必须自始至终孤身一人独步天下！老夫推心置腹地承认：正是这条铁律导致老夫平生沦为一介清贫穷汉、且注定将终生安贫乐道。平心而论，就这凡尘俗世的鄙陋铜臭而言，倘若能坐享阿卜杜拉出神入化的实务阅历、其庞大显赫的家族政商门路（老夫这等微末寒门对此做梦亦不敢高攀）、尤其是其追涨杀跌的绝世行市嗅觉，对老夫自是天大的造化！然而红尘俗世的浮华终究是过眼云烟——尤其是这泥胎陶土瓦罐更是脆弱易碎——先父在九泉之下的神圣托付，在老夫心目中的千钧分量，胜过这全天下所有的泥胚熟土加在一起！
“随着老夫这番感人肺腑的陈词落地，阿卜杜拉胸膛剧烈起伏，爆发出一阵山崩地裂般的号啕大哭——这固然是被老夫感天动地的纯孝情操所深深震撼，但老夫唯恐，更多的乃是被他自个儿眼前那片漆黑一团的绝望前景吓破了胆！
“老夫实在不忍心目睹挚友遭受如此诛心折磨，老夫当即赶忙施以援手力挽狂澜。老夫告知他：尽管神圣誓言严禁老夫同他合伙立约，然而老夫却完全能以另一种更为高尚的形式救他于水火！”

===Original===

I would lend him money at a low rate of interest to the value of half his stock upon the security of the whole. Times would change. The present ruinous price of pipkins (by which I myself suffered severely) could not long endure. He would lift his head again and could repay me at his leisure.
“He thanked me profusely, kissed my hand again and again, and gave me an appointment next day to view his merchandise and draw up the contract.
“I visited him at the hour agreed. The public notaries drew up an inventory of his whole stock, including his house and furniture, his prayer beads (which I was interested in, for they were of a costly Persian make), his dead wife’s jewels, all his clothes, his bed, and his pet cat﻿—an animal of no recorded pedigree but reputed to be of the pure Kashmir breed. I carefully noted all flaws, however slight, in each pipkin of his warehouse and set all such damaged goods aside as a makeweight. The sound pipkins I made no bones of but accepted frankly at their market value, and when the whole was added up the valuation came to no less than 20,000 dinars. Yet so hidebound in routine were the inhabitants of the place that Abdullah﻿—if you will believe me!﻿—had actually set his business stock down in his old books at fourfold that amount!
“As I had had to carry on, I had not now left by me my full hoard of 10,000 dinars. I had but 8,000 left. Yet I was in no difficulty. Half 20,000 is 10,000﻿—but there would be deductions!
“The costs of all this inventory and mortgage were, of course, set down against my valued friend Abdullah, but since he had not the ready cash wherewith to pay the notaries, their clerks, the demurrage fees, the stamps, the royal licence, the enregistration, the triplicates, the broker’s commission, the﻿ ﻿…”
“Pray, uncle,” cried the youngest of the nephews, “what are all these?”
“You must not interrupt me, my boy,” answered the great merchant, a little testily, “they are the necessary accompaniments of such transactions.﻿ ﻿… Well, as I was saying, the broker’s commission, the porter’s wages, the gratuities to the notaries’ servants, the cleaning up of the warehouse after all was over, and a hundred other petty items, I generously allowed them to be deducted from the loan; for our Prophet has said, ‘Blessed is he that shall grant delay to his debtor.’

===Chinese===

“老夫愿以极其优惠低廉的利息，以他名下全盘资产与存货作为质押担保，向他借出一笔金额相当于他全部存货市值一半的借款！时局终将好转，陶锅眼下这般自相残杀的毁灭性低价（平心而论，老夫自个儿亦为此遭受着剜心般的惨烈亏损）绝无可能长久维系下去。他定能重振旗鼓昂首挺胸，日后手头宽裕了再从容还清老夫本息即可。
“他闻言对我千恩万谢、感恩戴德，按住老夫的手亲了又亲，当即与老夫定下翌日清晨在作坊碰头查验资产、正式拟定抵押契约的大计。
“老夫依约准时登门。官办衙门的公证人当场铺开文卷，对他名下的全部家当展开了巨细靡遗的清点盘点：不仅包括作坊的全部存货与机器，甚至连他的宅邸、全部红木桌椅陈设、他日常念诵的祈祷念珠（老夫对此极感兴趣，因那是由波斯精工打造的无价珍品）、他亡妻留下的全部珠玉首饰、他身上的长袍衣物、他睡卧的床榻、甚至连他的爱猫——一头虽无名贵族谱佐证、但坊间盛传乃是正宗克什米尔名贵血统的灵猫——亦被悉数造册登记在案！老夫以极其严谨挑剔的目光，查验出他库房里每一只陶锅上存在的哪怕最微不足道的瑕疵裂纹，并将所有此类残次品打入另册充作折价添头；至于那些完好无损的上等正品陶锅，老夫则豪爽大度毫不含糊，全盘按公道市价予以确认估值；待全盘汇总折算完毕，全盘资产的评估总价高达整整20,000第纳尔（20000）之巨！然而当地百姓墨守成规到了何等可悲的境地：若非亲眼所见你们定不敢相信——阿卜杜拉那厮早年在自家陈年老账册上，竟大言不惭将这批存货资产虚标成了整整翻了四倍（4）的天文数字！
“由于老夫近期作坊日常营运开销颇大，老夫地砖底下深埋的那10,000第纳尔（10000）家底并未全额闲置，老夫手头满打满算只剩下8,000第纳尔（8000）现银可用。然而这对老夫而言压根不算难事：两万（20000）的一半虽说是整整一万（10000）——然而商海借贷之中，向来少不了各类名正言顺的‘合理扣缴’啊！
“通盘这般繁复的资产清查与抵押造册开销，理所当然理应由老夫可敬的挚友阿卜杜拉一人承担；然而鉴于他手头连半文垫付公证人、书办文牍、滞纳金、印花税、皇家特许特批费、官府备案登记费、一式三份抄造费、中介经纪人抽水佣金等等杂费的现银都掏不出……”
“敢问叔父，”最小的侄儿失声喊道，“这都是些什么玩意啊？”
“休要打岔，小崽子，”大商人略带几分愠怒斥责道，“此乃世间一切正规商业交割不可或缺的合法伴生物！……话接前文，经纪人佣金、脚夫搬运费、打赏给公证人随从奴仆的茶水小费、事毕之后作坊通盘大扫除保洁费、外加整整一百样（100）零碎杂项开支，老夫皆极其慷慨大度恩准从借款总额中直接扣除抵充；盖因先知圣训有云：‘宽限其债务之人，福莫大焉！’”

===Original===

That very evening, with every phrase of goodwill and expressed hopes for his speedy recovery of fortune, I counted out to my dear friend Abdullah the full balance of 16,325 dinars and one half dinar, and left him overjoyed at the possession of so much immediate wealth.
“But, alas! no man can forecast the morrow, and all things were written at the beginning to be as they shall be. So far from pipkins rising, the price fell slowly and regularly for three months, during which time I was careful to restrict my own production somewhat, though my poor dear friend, in his necessity, produced more feverishly than ever, and thereby did but lower still further the now really infamous price of pipkins.
“At last he came to a dead stop, and could produce no more. I gladly allowed the first, the second and even the third arrears of interest to be added to the principal at a most moderate compound rate, but there was some fatality upon him, and I was inexpressibly shocked to hear one morning that Abdullah had drowned himself over night in a beautiful little lake which his long dead wife had designed for him in his once charming pleasure grounds.”
“Oh! Poor man!” cried all the nephews in chorus.
“Poor man! Poor man indeed!” echoed their benevolent uncle, “I was a stranger in that country. He was the closest tie I had to it, and, indeed, in my loneliness, the nearest companion I had in the whole world.” And here the good old man paused to breathe a prayer for the departed companion of his long-past youth. He then sighed deeply and continued:
“I used what had now become my considerable influence with the government to provide him a costly funeral at the public expense﻿—for he had left no effects, nor even children to follow him. I walked behind the coffin as chief mourner, and though I attempted to control my grief, all the vast crowd assembled were moved by my manly sorrow, and several spoke to me upon it at the conclusion of the sad rites.
“I allowed the decent interval of three days to elapse and then did what I had no choice but to do. I took over Abdullah’s factory on foreclosure and added it to my own.
“In this way the valuable kilns and stores of clay and wheels and vehicles, etc., all became my property. I had them valued, and was pleasurably surprised to discover that they were worth at least 25,000 dinars.

===Chinese===

“就在同一天傍晚，伴随着老夫口中吐露出的每一句溢美祝福与祝愿他早日重振家业的由衷期盼，老夫当面结结实实向老夫亲爱的挚友阿卜杜拉数出了扣除上述所有规费之后的全部净现银——总计整整16,325.5第纳尔（16325）！随后飘然离去，任由他沉浸在骤然坐拥如此巨额即期现银的狂喜之中。
“然而造化弄人啊！凡人岂能勘破天机预卜明日之祸福，造物主早在创世之初便已注定了天地万物的终局归宿！陶锅行市非但未能如他所愿绝地反弹，反倒在整整三个月（3）的光阴里呈现出极其稳定且不可阻挡的阴跌暴跌之势！在此期间，老夫极其审慎地逐步压缩了老夫自家的产量；而老夫可怜的挚友，在迫在眉睫的债务逼索之下，却如发了疯一般不惜工本昼夜疯狂增产，其结果不过是火上浇油，将本就惨不忍睹的陶锅市价进一步砸入了暗无天日的万劫不复深渊！
“最终，他彻底弹尽粮绝，再也烧不出半只陶罐了。老夫极其宽厚仁慈地恩准他将第一期、第二期乃至第三期拖欠的利息，以极其低廉公道的复合复利直接滚入借款本金之中；然而冥冥之中似乎有一柄死神的达摩克利斯之剑悬在他的头顶——某天清晨，老夫听闻了一桩令老夫惊骇欲绝、痛心疾首的晴天霹雳：可怜的阿卜杜拉，竟在昨夜跳入了他已故亡妻早年在他那座昔日如诗如画的花园别业中为他亲手设计的一泓碧波秀丽的小湖之中，自尽溺亡了！”
“啊！可怜的人啊！”小侄儿们异口同声失声痛哭惊呼起来！
“可怜的人！委实是可怜透顶的人啊！”慈悲祥和的叔父同声附和长叹，“老夫当年客居异乡，人生地不熟。他乃是老夫同彼邦最亲密的精神纽带，实则在老夫形单影只的漂泊生涯中，他乃是老夫在这大千世界里最推心置腹的至交同袍啊！”说到此处，可敬的老人闭目默哀，为他那逝去已久的青春同伴献上了一段深情的祈祷。随后他长长叹了一口气，接着讲道：
“老夫动用了老夫彼时已然举足轻重的通天政商威望，在官府公帑开销下为他操办了一场极尽哀荣的国葬大典——因他身后既无半点值钱余产，膝下亦无半个子嗣送终。老夫以首席治丧孝子的身份手扶灵柩缓缓前行；尽管老夫使出浑身解数极力克制内心的悲恸，满场聚集的浩瀚万民依然无不为老夫铁骨男儿的真切悲恸所深深动容，在凄凉葬礼收尾之际，不少显贵纷纷上前安慰老夫节哀顺变。
“老夫耐着性子等待了整整三天（3）体面的哀悼缓冲期，随后毅然决然做出了老夫别无抉择的唯一决断：老夫依法行使了抵押罚没权，全盘接管了阿卜杜拉名下的全部作坊工场，并将其全盘并入了老夫的名下。
“就这样，价值连城的陶窑、堆积如山的陶土原料储备、辘轳水车、马车车辆等等一切家私，尽数顺理成章化为了老夫的合法私产！老夫命人重新盘点评估，极其惬意地获悉：这批资产的实际保守估值，至少高达整整25,000第纳尔（25000）之巨！”

===Original===

“A full two years had now passed since my first coming to this happy and secluded valley where Allah had poured out upon me His blessings in so marvellous a fashion. I was lonely, as you may imagine, but I manfully faced my duty. I continued to supervise and extend my manufactory of pipkins which now provided these articles for more than half the households of the state. I therefore could and did put the price of these useful articles upon a basis which, if it was somewhat higher than that to which people had grown accustomed during my earlier manipulations, had the priceless advantage of security, so that the house wife could always know exactly what she had to disburse﻿—and I what I should receive. As I manufactured upon so large a scale my overhead charges.﻿ ﻿…”
“What are overhead﻿ ﻿…” began the eldest nephew, when his uncle, visibly perturbed, shouted “Silence!﻿ ﻿… You have made me forget what I was going to say!”
There was an awkward pause, during which the old man restored his ruffled temper and proceeded:
“I was able to buy clay more cheaply and better than the private pipkin-makers (for so they were now called, with well merited contempt) who still vainly attempted to compete with me, and my business automatically grew as the poor remnant of theirs declined.
“Not only did I continually increase in wealth by these somewhat obvious methods, but also in the power of controlling property; for when some fresh fool among my fellow pipkin-makers found himself in difficulties, it was my practice to seek him secretly, to condole with him upon what I had heard was his approaching misfortune, and to save him from ruin by taking over the whole of his stock. Nay! I would do more. I would rescue him from the sad necessity of attempting some new unknown trade by taking him into my own employment at a generous salary (but upon a monthly agreement); with a pretty concession to sentiment I would even leave him to manage his own dear old booth in the bazaar to which so many years had now accustomed him. I look back with pleasure upon the tears of gratitude which stood in the eyes of those to whom I extended such favours.
“So things went on for one more year, and another, and another, till the fifth year of my sojourn among these simple people was completed.

===Chinese===

“自老夫初次踏入这片真主以神迹般手法向老夫倾洒无量恩典的秀美世外桃源算起，光阴已然悄然流逝了整整两个春秋（2）。如你们所能想象的那般，老夫孤身一人客居异乡难免形单影只，然而老夫以昂扬的男子气概直面天职！老夫继续全力督造并拓张老夫名下的陶锅工场帝国，此时老夫的作坊已然垄断并供应着全境超过半数黎民百姓的日常炊具所需！因此，老夫完全有资格、且当真将这些日用民生必需品的行市定价，锚定在了一个绝对稳定的全新基准之上——尽管这个价码相比于老夫早年翻云覆雨操盘时的骨折价略显昂贵，却具备了无可估量的‘确定性’与安全感：全境的主妇自此对每日柴米油盐所需的开销明明白白，而老夫对每日落袋的银两进项亦心知肚明！由于老夫的生产规模是如此庞大恢弘，老夫的综合固定管理杂支摊销……”
“敢问叔父，什么是‘管理杂支摊销’……”大侄子刚欲开口探问，却见叔父脸色骤变、极其败兴地暴怒叱喝道：“闭嘴！……你把老夫刚要脱口而出的经商大道全给搅黄了！”
现场陷入了一阵尴尬紧绷的死寂；片刻之后，老先生平复了心头的愠火，继续讲道：
“老夫能够以远比那些‘私人小作坊主’（彼处百姓如今皆以这般受之无愧的鄙夷口吻称呼他们）更为低廉的大宗折扣采办成色更优的陶土原料，那些企图蚍蜉撼树同老夫恶性竞争的跳梁小丑日益凋敝，而老夫的商业帝国则如滚雪球般自动狂飙突进。
“老夫不仅凭借这些显而易见的正道阳谋使得财富与日俱增，更在统御全境私有财产的话语权上登峰造极！每逢老夫的同行陶匠之中又冒出某个身陷财务绝境的新倒霉蛋，老夫的惯例向来是微服夜访其门楣，对他所面临的横祸厄运表达最真挚的慰问与同情，并以原价全盘收购其全部滞销存货的菩萨心肠将他从破产深渊中拯救出来！不仅如此！老夫更会仁至义尽做绝好事：老夫绝不忍心看着他被迫改行涉足陌生的生计，而是当场将他收编入老夫麾下工场，支领一份优厚体面的高薪（当然皆是按月续签的雇佣合同）；出于对人间温情的一抹浪漫体恤，老夫甚至恩准他继续坐镇打理他多年来早已习惯的那间巴扎市集旧铺面！每当老夫回想起当年那些笑纳老夫恩典之人眼眶中滚落的滚烫感激泪水，老夫心中便油然升起无尽的惬意与欣慰。
“局势便这般波澜不惊地度过了一年，又是一年，又是一年，直到老夫在这帮淳朴黎民之中客居的第五个年头（5）终于圆满收官。”

===Original===

“I was in complete control of the pipkin trade, making all the pipkins that the nation needed, and free from any rival. The house which I had built for myself was the finest in the place, but covered, I humbly add, with many a sacred text.
