‘Remember, pray, that you have taken an oath in the name of the Scroll, and trivial as this may seem to a man of your depraved character, others take the matter more seriously.’
“I stood silent under the rebuke and waited his further words.
“ ‘Well, well,’ said he suddenly, ‘where were you half an hour after sunrise on the fourth day of the Fast of Ramadan in the three hundred and seventh year from the Flight of the Prophet?’
“As I stood aghast at the question, he touched one hand significantly with the finger of the other and said, ‘I do not wish to press you.’
“ ‘I have not the least idea,’ said I.
“The pleader glanced significantly at the judge and then continued:
“ ‘You haven’t the least idea? Can you tell me approximately where you were?’
“ ‘No, I cannot,’ said I. ‘It is very long ago, and I was but a young and innocent child.’
“Here the judge interrupted me sharply: ‘We are not concerned with your young and innocent childhood. Answer the question, if you please, and make no speeches.’
“The pleader consulted his notes, looked up to me again and said: ‘You have told us the charter was signed in your presence and delivered over to you.’
“ ‘Yes,’ said I.
“ ‘Don’t answer,’ interrupted His Holiness, sharply, ‘until you are asked a question.’
“ ‘No,’ said I.
“ ‘Take great care, witness,’ said His Holiness in a menacing voice. ‘Take very great care!’
“ ‘Thank You, your Holiness,’ said the pleader. ‘And now, sir,’ said he, addressing me in a very firm tone indeed, as though he had caught red-handed some thief creeping in to his household, ‘will you please tell us where is that charter now? Can you produce it?’
“ ‘His Holiness is sitting on it,’ said I, simply.
“Here the judge almost rose from his throne, so strongly was he moved.
“ ‘Were you not a layman and naturally ignorant of the forms of the court, I should condemn you to some very severe penalty,’ said he. ‘I make allowance for your lack of custom; but I warn you, you may go too far.’
“ ‘I will pass over the last remark, witness,’ continued the pleader in dignified tones. ‘I think you were not quite yourself when you made it. Will you be good enough to answer my question? Where is the charter now?’
“ ‘Well,’ said I, all bewildered, ‘I handed it up for His Holiness to see what my case was, and to the best of my belief he﻿—’
“ ‘Silence,’ thundered the judge.

===Chinese===

“‘切莫忘记，你可是当着神圣经卷立下过毒誓的！对你这种品行败坏的无赖而言这或许不过是儿戏，但旁人对待誓言可远比你虔诚庄重得多！’
“面对这通当头棒喝，我唯有默不作声，任凭他继续发难。
“‘那么，’他冷不防逼问道，‘在先知迁徙纪元第三百零七（307）年斋月第四（4）天的日出后半个时辰，你人究竟在哪里？’
“我被这突如其来的刁钻问题问得目瞪口呆，他却意味深长地用一根手指点了点另一只手掌，慢条斯理地说道：‘老夫并不想苦苦相逼。’
“‘老夫压根一点印象也没有，’我说。
“大律师意味深长地朝法官递了个眼色，随即继续发难：
“‘一点印象也没有？那你能不能大体交代一下你当时人在何处？’
“‘不能，老夫交代不出，’我说。‘那毕竟是陈年旧事了，当时老夫不过是个年幼无知的孩童。’
“这时法官厉声打断了我：‘法庭并不关心你年幼无知的童年！劳驾你回答问题，休要在公堂上高谈阔论！’
“大律师低头核对了案卷笔记，再次抬头盯着我道：‘你适才口口声声说，特许状乃是当着你的面签署画押并移交到你手里的？’
“‘是的，’我说。
“‘在没有向你提问之前，休要抢着作答！’法官老爷厉声喝止。
“‘是，’我说。
“‘当心点，证人！’法官老爷语带威胁地警告道。‘你给老夫格外当心点！’
“‘多谢法官老爷明断，’大律师说道。‘那么现在，原告老爷，’他转过身来，用审问捉贼捉赃的窃贼般的严厉语调逼问道，‘劳驾你告诉大伙儿：那份特许状如今身在何处？你能当庭出示吗？’
“‘法官老爷正坐在屁股底下呢，’我直截了当地据实相告。
“听到这话，法官气得几乎从宝座上一跃而起，激动得浑身发抖。
“‘若不是看你是个不懂公堂规矩的白衣布衣门外汉，老夫非将你处以极刑严惩不可！’法官怒不可遏道。‘老夫念你不知者不怪，姑且宽恕你的莽撞无礼；但老夫郑重警告你，休要得寸进尺！’
“‘老夫且放过你刚才那句大逆不道的话，证人，’大律师以威严庄重的语调接茬道。‘老夫权当你说胡话时神志不清。劳驾你正面回答老夫的问题：特许状如今身在何处？’
“‘可是，’我满头雾水地辩白道，‘老夫刚才明明亲手呈递给了法官老爷御览，好让他明察老夫的诉求，就老夫所知，他分明——’
“‘闭嘴！’法官咆哮如雷。”

===Original===

‘Brother Selim, I am afraid we shall get no further on this tack. The witness evidently does not or will not understand your drift. Allow me to ask him a question.’ He then turned with a sort of false kindness upon his face and said to me in measured tones: ‘What we want to know is, where is the charter upon which you claim?’
“ ‘I put it into court﻿ ﻿…’ I began.
“At this the judge gave a little gesture of despair and sighed. Then he spoke.
“ ‘It is a principle of the law of this country,’ said His Holiness, leaning back in apparent weariness as though he were instructing a child, ‘I should have thought a principle known even to the meanest of His Majesty’s subjects, that a document must be proved. Have you proved the charter of which you speak?’
“ ‘I don’t know what you mean, Your Holiness,’ said I, in very genuine fear.
“The judge leaned forward towards me and said in measured tones: ‘Remember that I am treating you leniently. I am doing what I can for you, I understand the difficulty of your position. Take care! Take very great care! Brother Selim, have you any other questions to ask?’
“ ‘I have one or two of some consequence, if I may be allowed, Your Holiness?’
“ ‘Certainly, brother Selim. Pray continue. We are all ears.’
“The pleader cleared his throat, again consulted his notes, looked up to me and said: ‘What were your earnings in games of hazard during the year concluding with the opening of the last Feast of Ramadan?’
“I answered that I had no exact calculation, but that I had small stomach for such pastimes, and might have won or lost anything between one hundred and two hundred dinars.
“ ‘Take care, take very great care!’ said His Holiness, addressing me again.
“ ‘Between one or two hundred dinars,’ said the pleader, in a musing sort of voice, and I noticed that the judge was taking a note of my reply. ‘Now be good enough, you base fellow, to answer me this﻿—and remember you are upon your oath﻿—have you been in the habit of cheating at cards, loading dice, stacking packs, palming coins, and in other ways overreaching those who joined you in what they thought to be an innocent amusement?’
“I was about to reply when he again thundered at me: ‘Remember you are upon your oath,’ and His Holiness was moved to add:
“ ‘Take care, witness, take very great care!’
“ ‘No,’ said I.

===Chinese===

“‘塞利姆贤弟，照这个路数审下去，老夫恐怕咱们审不出什么眉目来。证人显见是不明白、抑或压根不愿明白你的用意。容老夫亲自问他一句。’法官脸上随即挂上一副虚伪的温和神色，用拿腔拿调的语气质问我道：‘咱们想查明的无非是：你赖以起诉的那份特许状究竟在何处？’
“‘老夫已然呈堂交公了……’我刚要辩解。
“法官见状露出一副无可奈何的绝望神情，长叹了一口气。随后他开口道：
“‘咱们本邦律法有一条基本铁律，’法官老爷靠回宝座，显出一副宛如在教导稚童般的倦怠神态，‘老夫本以为即便国王陛下治下最下贱的草民也该通晓此理：那便是一切公文文书皆须举证证明真伪。你口口声声提及的特许状，你可曾在堂上完成过司法举证程序？’
“‘老夫实在听不懂老爷的意思，’我发自肺腑地惶恐道。
“法官身子前倾凑近我，字斟句酌地告诫道：‘记住，老夫眼下对你已是法外开恩、极尽宽大！老夫正竭尽所能替你周全，老夫体谅你处境艰难。你可得当心！给我千万当心点！塞利姆贤弟，你可还有其他紧要问题要讯问？’
“‘若蒙法官老爷恩准，老夫尚有一、两（1、2）个关乎宏旨的问题。’
“‘请便，塞利姆贤弟。只管问下去，咱们大伙儿洗耳恭听。’
“大律师清了清嗓子，再次低头审视案卷笔记，抬头逼视着我道：‘在截至上一届开斋节（Feast of Ramadan）开幕为止的那一（1）年里，你在各种博彩赌戏中的收益究竟几何？’
“我答辩说自个儿并无确切账目，因我一向对这等赌博营生毫无兴致，输赢大体在一百（100）到两百（200）第纳尔之间浮动。
“‘当心！千万当心点！’法官老爷再次厉声警告我。
“‘在一百（100）到两百（200）第纳尔之间，’大律师若有所思地自言自语，我注意到法官正在飞快地把我的回答记录在案。‘那么你这个卑鄙小人，劳驾你老实交代——切记你可是立过誓的——你平日里可有在牌桌上出老千、灌铅骰子、码牌换牌、偷藏金币，以此等下三滥勾当坑骗那些以为只是消遣玩乐的同伴赌友？’
“我正欲分辩，他再次如晴天霹雳般冲我吼道：‘切记你立下了毒誓！’而法官老爷也忍不住火上浇油道：
“‘当心点，证人，给我格外当心点！’
“‘没有，’我说。”

===Original===

“At this moment I was astonished to see everybody, including the pleader, sit down suddenly, cross-legged, upon the floor, while I stood there upon my little block of wood, most terribly conspicuous. It was due to a gesture from the judge.
“ ‘So far,’ said he, in a solemn and majestic manner, ‘I have allowed things to take their course, because, as I have said, every latitude must be allowed to one who is foolish enough to plead his own case. But the dignity of His Majesty’s court forbids me to be silent upon hearing this last reply to a question of the most profound and searching kind, requiring an adequate reply. The witness has insolently answered “No.” ’ He then turned to me and said, with a severity that thrilled me to the marrow: ‘This is a Civil Court; but remember, sir,’ and here he raised his voice in a very terrible manner, ‘I can impound documents and present all that you have said to the Lord Prosecutor.’
“ ‘Yes, Your Holiness,’ said I, now thoroughly at sea.
“ ‘Proceed,’ said the judge, simply, to the pleader.
“ ‘I have only one more question to ask,’ said the pleader.
“ ‘Proceed, proceed, brother Selim,’ said the judge with geniality.
“ ‘Do you or do you not suffer from the itch?’
“ ‘My lord,’ said I, ‘am I really to reply to﻿—’
“His Holiness interrupted me with a violence which I little expected from one in so exalted a position. ‘Answer the question!’ he shouted, ‘answer the question at once!’
“ ‘Well,’ said I, ‘to tell the truth, I have some little affection of the sole of my left foot, but I conceive that with careful attention and proper medical advice﻿—’
“ ‘That will do,’ said the pleader, putting up his hand. ‘We have heard all we need to hear,’ and he sat down again upon his mat.
“ ‘Any rebulgence?’ said His Holiness, looking round with a pleasant smile upon all assembled.
“I had no idea what was meant, but my friend the scrivener passed me up a little note, saying, ‘Do you carefully reexamine yourself so as to undo the effect of this terrible cross-examination.’
“It was all Greek to me, but grasping at a straw I addressed His Holiness, and said:
“ ‘Oh, Voice of God and Justice upon earth! I would like to ask myself certain questions.’
“ ‘By all means,’ said he graciously. ‘Let me inform you what is the custom of the court. You have first to stand and ask yourself the question; you shall then stand up again and reply to it.’

===Chinese===

“就在这一刹那，让我惊得目瞪口呆的是，全场所有人——包括那位大律师——在法官的一个手势之下，竟齐刷刷全都在地毯上盘腿坐下了；唯独老夫孤零零踩在那截小木块上，显得格外扎眼凄凉。
“‘到目前为止，’法官以极其肃穆威严的腔调宣告，‘老夫之所以听之任之，正如老夫先前所言，全因对那些愚蠢到胆敢亲自出庭自辩之辈，法庭理应给予一切最大限度的宽容体谅。然而听到证人对适才那等直指灵魂深处、理应作出详尽诚恳答复的严峻质询，竟然给出了如此大逆不道、轻浮狂妄的一字答复——“没有”，国王陛下法庭的至高尊严令老夫决不能再姑息缄默！’随后他转过头来逼视着我，用一种令我彻骨生寒的狠厉语调喝道：‘这里固然是民事法庭；但你给我听好了，汉子！’说到这里，他的嗓门陡然拔高，骇人听闻至极，‘老夫完全有权当庭扣押公文卷宗，将你在堂上的一言一行全数移送给帝国检控总长大人发落！’
“‘遵命，法官老爷，’我说，此时我早已被绕得云里雾里、不知所措。
“‘继续审，’法官轻描淡写地吩咐大律师道。
“‘老夫唯独剩下一（1）个问题要讯问了，’大律师说。
“‘请问，请问，塞利姆贤弟只管问，’法官和颜悦色地应道。
“‘你身上究竟生没生疥疮瘙痒顽疾？’
“‘老爷，’我说，‘难道老夫当真得回答这等……’
“法官老爷突然爆发出一阵暴怒咆哮，那等狂暴凶悍完全不符合他这等显赫尊贵的身份地位。‘回答问题！’他歇斯底里地吼道，‘立刻回答问题！’
“‘好吧，’我说，‘实不相瞒，老夫左脚脚底板上确实生了点轻微的顽癣小毛病，但我琢磨着只要悉心照料调理，并遵从大夫的医嘱……’
“‘够了，’大律师一抬手打断了我。‘大伙儿想听的都已经听得明明白白了！’言毕，他心满意足地重又坐回草席之上。
“‘可有任何反向追问辩明（rebulgence）？’法官老爷环顾全场，脸上重新堆起和蔼可亲的笑容询问道。
“我压根不知这句黑话是何用意，但我身旁的那位书记官朋友悄悄递给我一张纸条，上头写着：‘你赶紧自个儿反诘盘问自个儿一番，好抵消适才那通可怕盘诘的恶劣影响！’
“这对我而言简直如同天书，但溺水之人哪怕抓到一根稻草也得拼死一搏，我便向法官老爷启奏道：
“‘苍天金口、人间青天大老爷！老夫想反向盘问自个儿几个问题。’
“‘准奏！’他恩威并施地准许道。‘老夫且给你指点指点法庭规矩：你必须先站在此处向自个儿发问；随后你再站立起来回答自个儿的提问。’”

===Original===

“Kneeling, I struck the pavement of the court three times with my forehead as is the custom, and rising again turned towards the empty space upon the little block of wood and said:
“ ‘Now, witness, remember you are upon your oath; did you or did you not receive the charter from the king and his council in the terms you have mentioned?’
“I then leapt upon the little block of wood and turning to the place I had just occupied, I said:
“ ‘I did.’
“I then jumped down again (luckily I was still a young man and the exercise did not affect me or cause me loss of breath) and I asked:
“ ‘What did you do with that document?’
“I leapt upon the little block of wood again and turned to the place I had just occupied and answered:
“ ‘I brought it into court.’
“Once more did I take up my place on the floor, standing beneath the little block of wood.
“ ‘Having brought it into court, what did you do with it?’
“I returned to my little block of wood, faced the place I had just occupied and said:
“ ‘I handed it up to His Holiness.’
“The judge then spoke. ‘I have had enough of this and I refuse to waste the time of the court longer. It is in my power to condemn you to the king’s dungeons forever, and I may say that never in my long experience of our august courts have I come across anything to parallel your repeated insolence. I have already told you that you have not proved your document and therefore for the purposes of this court it does not exist. Stand down.’
“The words ‘stand down’ signify in the technical language of this great people ‘sit down,’ and can only be disobeyed under the most fearful penalties. I at once obeyed and resumed my place cross-legged upon the mat.
“The judge was now free to give his decision, but first he turned to the pleader who had opposed me and said in the most genial tones:
“ ‘Brother Selim, you have, I take it, proved your document, especially the word “and”?’
“ ‘Oh, yes, my lord,’ answered he, in a satisfied manner. ‘I have further interpleaded for secondary and excised the four principal terminants, all of which are duly stamped, passed, filed, recorded, exuded, denoted, permuted, polluted and redeemed.’ To each of these words the judge nodded with greater and greater content, and then asked:
“ ‘Do you call any further witnesses, brother Selim?’

===Chinese===

“我依循成规双膝跪地，脑门狠狠磕在公堂石板地上连叩了三（3）记响头，随后站起身来，面向那截空荡荡的小木块，煞有介事地发问道：
“‘证人听真，切记你可是立过誓的！你究竟有没有从国王及其枢密院手中，依照你适才提及的条款受领过那份特许状？’
“问罢，我纵身跳上那截小木块，转过身来面对我刚刚站立的空地，答道：
“‘确实受领过！’
“随后我又纵身跳回地面（幸好老夫当年年富力强，这番上蹿下跳并未让我气喘吁吁），再次发问道：
“‘那你把那份文书如何处置了？’
“我又一次跳上小木块，面向原先的位置，朗声答道：
“‘老夫把它带到了公堂之上！’
“我又一次跳回地面，侍立在小木块跟前：
“‘既然带到了公堂之上，你又拿它做了何用？’
“我重新跳上小木块，面对原先的立足点答道：
“‘老夫亲手呈递给了法官老爷！’
“这时法官老爷终于按捺不住，开口喝道：‘老夫受够了这出闹剧，法庭决不再为你白白虚掷光阴！老夫手中执掌着将你终身打入天牢的大权，在老夫执掌威严法庭的漫长岁月里，老夫敢说从未见过有谁如你这般三番五次挑衅蔑视公堂！老夫早已告诫过你，你根本未能完成对公文的法定举证程序，因此在法庭看来，那份文书根本自始不存在！退席退下（Stand down）！’
“在这支伟大民族的法学黑话行话里，‘退席’的意思便是‘坐下’，胆敢抗旨不遵便要遭受极刑重惩。我立刻俯首遵命，乖乖回到草席上盘腿坐好。
“如今法官老爷终于得以从容宣读裁决，但他首先转头望向那位同我针锋相对的大律师，用极尽和蔼可亲的语调询问道：
“‘塞利姆贤弟，依老夫看，你应当已然完成了对文书的法定举证，尤其是那个“以及（and）”字吧？’
“‘启奏老爷，那是自然！’他得意洋洋地答道。‘老夫更进一步就次要事实提出了独立辩护，并依法剔除了四（4）项主要终止要件，所有文书均已妥善加盖印花、通过审核、归档入库、载入法典、分泌析出、明确标示、排列置换、污染受染并依法赎回！’每听到大律师嘴里吐出一个法学怪词，法官老爷便心满意足地点一下头，眼中的赞许愈发浓烈，随后问道：
“‘塞利姆贤弟，你可还要传唤任何证人出庭？’”

===Original===

“ ‘I call none,’ replied the eminent man, ‘for if I do the vile plaintiff would have an opportunity of cross-examining them and that would give away my whole case.’
“ ‘I think you have done wisely,’ said His Holiness, by way of an obiter dictum. ‘Things shall therefore turn upon the charter alone.’ With these words I perceived by the rustle all about me that the last phase of the trial had come and that my fate was sealed. I thought I had observed in the manner of its conduct I will not say a bias, but a sort of atmosphere unfavourable to my claims; for though it was impossible to conceive that any personal or other feeling could affect His Holiness’s mind, yet I dreaded his decision. None the less I awaited that decision with some interest, for, after all, nothing is certain until it is concluded.
“The judge put down his crown, assumed a headgear which resembled that of the Special pleaders but gilded, and with the interior of the long furry ears carefully painted in silver by way of contrast; for such the Custom of the Law demands when a decision is about to be delivered.
“He spoke:
“ ‘From the evidence that has been laid before me it is clear that there exists, or has existed, will exist, or may exist, or can exist or at some other time existed, demurrer notwithstanding, some charter wherein the word **and** is the point at issue. That form was admitted by the defence, I think?’ All the pleaders rose and bowed and then again were seated upon their carpets. ‘But I gather’ (and here he looked sternly at me) ‘that there was no acceptance by the plaintiff. We have, I take it, an operative clause wherein the operative word is **and**. “**And** so long as the salt tax endures.” Many points brought forward in defence of the Crown I am compelled to overlook. It is the glory of our courts of justice that they exercise an absolutely evenhanded dealing between man and man, and that His Majesty himself is bound by their decisions.’ (Here there was a murmur of applause which was instantly suppressed.) ‘I make therefore so bold as to say that the Counsel engaged by His Majesty on this occasion have said many things with which I do not agree and others which I shall not take into account.

===Chinese===

“‘一个也不传唤，’那位名震天下的大律师答道，‘因老夫若传唤证人，那个卑鄙可鄙的原告便有了交叉质询的机会，那岂不是要泄露老夫全盘诉讼策略的底牌嘛！’
“‘老夫以为你所决断极明，’法官老爷借题发挥附带评述道。‘既然如此，是非曲直便全凭特许状本身定夺了。’听到此言，我从身遭人群的一阵窸窣骚动中察觉出，庭审已然步入最后关头，老夫的命运已然大局已定。在此案审理过程中，老夫不敢断言公堂有何偏袒不公，但隐隐察觉出一种于老夫诉求极其不利的冷酷氛围；虽说绝难想象会有任何私心杂念能够动摇法官老爷通神的心智，但我心底依旧对即将到来的判决惴惴不安。尽管如此，我仍怀着几分探究的好奇等待着判决下达，毕竟尘埃未定之前，乾坤犹未可知。
“只见法官老爷摘下宝冠，换上了一顶同大律师相仿却通体镏金的皮帽，两只毛茸茸长耳朵的内衬特意涂成了银色以资对照；每逢宣判神圣裁决之际，法界律法惯例历来必须穿戴此等冠冕。
“他朗声宣判道：
“‘依呈堂供证研判，显而易见，不顾任何抗辩阻却，世上确实存在、或曾经存在、或将要存在、或可能存在、或能够存在、或在其他某个历史时期曾经存在过某份特许状，其中连词“以及（and）”乃是争讼焦点之所在。依老夫所见，被告方对此格式要件并无异议吧？’台下全体大律师齐刷刷起身鞠躬，随后重又坐回草席之上。‘但老夫研判，’（说到此处他严厉地瞪了我一眼）‘原告对此却并未完全受领。依老夫之见，本案涉及一条生效核心条款，其中生效核心词汇便是“以及（and）”。所谓“以及只要盐税（salt tax）绵延不绝”。对于国王陛下辩护团所提述的诸多抗辩理由，老夫不得不暂且搁置不表。咱们法庭的无上荣光，正在于对人与人之间执掌绝对不偏不倚的公道天平，即便国王陛下本人亦受本庭判决之无上羁束。’（堂下顿时响起一阵被迅速压制的赞叹低语。）‘因此老夫胆敢断言：国王陛下此番聘请的法律顾问团，在堂上所发表的诸多高论中，有许多乃是老夫所不敢苟同的，另有诸多高论老夫亦不予采纳。

===Original===

It is equally clear that the case presented by the plaintiff is, as he put it, no case at all, and that were I to rely, as I shall not, upon the strict forms of law, he is already out of court.’ At this all looked severely on me and I felt my stature singularly diminished, and crouched lower upon my mat. His Holiness continued:
“ ‘I shall treat this matter as though I had heard no pleadings upon either side, for this I take to be the true attitude of a judge concerned with justice alone. We have, then, this operative “**and**﻿ ﻿…” this decisive word “**and**.” ’ Here His Holiness leaned back on his throne, cast his eyes upwards towards the rich arabesques of the ceiling, sighed and continued:
“ ‘The word **and** is among the most significant of our ancient, glorious language. It has been used upon innumerable occasions. Our industrial classes, our nobility and even our middle classes, as well as the poor in their humble station, are compelled to its continual expression. It is, if I may so express myself, part of the heritage of our race. He would indeed be poor in spirit, and weak in his allegiance to the imperial traditions of this island,’ continued His Holiness, warming to his subject, ‘did he not appreciate the majesty, the significance, the grandeur upon occasions, the full effect and indeed the awful weight of this little word,’ and here he dropped his voice, ‘ “**and**.” “**And** so long as the salt tax﻿ ﻿…” etc. That is the point. **And**﻿ ﻿… I trust I have made myself clear.’
“All heads nodded in unison, while a song in adoration of His Holiness was sung by an acolyte who entered at this stage of the proceedings (as is customary in courts of law), and a hidden chorus, distant, but just heard, added a short canticle of praise. His Holiness waited for the conclusion of these ceremonies, which are invariably interpolated during any important judgment, and then continued:
“ ‘**And** so long,’ ‘What is the significance of that word “**and**”? I take it that it is affirmative, negative, copulative and restrictive; but that is﻿—not all. I think it is also constructive, instructive, and destructive. It is only by using it in all these ways that we can fully appreciate its preponderant significance in the issue before us.’

===Chinese===

同样昭然若揭的是，原告所呈递的诉由，正如他自个儿陈述的那样，根本算不上任何诉由；老夫若依循严格的程序法典——老夫当然不屑于如此机械——原告早就该被净身轰出法庭了！’听到这话，全场所有人全都用无比严苛的冷酷眼神剐着我，我只觉得自己登时矮了半截，越发畏缩地蜷缩在自个儿的草席里。法官老爷继续宣判道：
“‘老夫且将此案视同从未听过原被告任何一方的陈词辩护，因老夫笃信唯有如此超脱，方为一个唯以天理正义为念的青天法官之本色！那么，呈现在咱们面前的，便是这个生效核心词“以及（and）”……这个一锤定音的关键之词“以及（and）”。’说到这里，法官老爷仰靠在宝座靠背上，双目深邃地仰望着天花板上繁复华美的阿拉伯卷草纹饰，长叹一声，娓娓道来：
“‘“以及（and）”这个字眼，乃是咱们古老而光辉的国语之中最重若千钧的词汇之一！在千千万万的场合中，它被千百次反复运用。咱们的工商阶层、贵族豪门，乃至中产阶级，乃至那些身处卑微底层的黎民寒士，在日常口角之间无不时刻受其统御驱遣。倘若容老夫直抒胸臆，这实乃咱们伟大民族血脉遗产不可分割之瑰宝！倘若有人不能领会这个小字所蕴含的庄严神圣、微言大义、宏伟壮阔、充沛法力，以及那令人肃然起敬的千钧分量，’法官老爷愈讲愈亢奋，神采飞扬，‘那么这个人无论在精神上何等贫瘠，对咱们海岛帝胄传统的忠诚必定何等脆弱不堪！’讲到此处，他压低了嗓门，‘“以及（and）”。“以及只要盐税（salt tax）……”云云。这才是破案的关键枢纽！以及……老夫深信自个儿已然剖析得透彻入骨了吧！’
“全场所有人整齐划一地点头称是；与此同时，一名少年侍祭依循法庭典礼旧制步入堂前，高声唱起赞颂法官老爷圣明的赞美诗，远处屏风后隐隐传来一阵若有若无的和声合唱，献上一阕短小圣歌。法官老爷肃立静候这些凡遇重大宣判必不可少的插曲礼仪告一段落，随后继续庄严宣判：
“‘“以及只要（And so long）”，“以及（and）”这个字眼究竟承载着何等法理意涵？依老夫卓见，它兼具肯定、否定、并列连缀与限制排他之四重属性；然而——这还远远不止！老夫笃定它同时兼具建设性、启迪性与毁灭性！唯有从所有这些维度全盘参透，咱们方能彻底领悟其在摆在咱们面前的是非纠纷中所占据的压倒性决定意义！’”

===Original===

Once more all heads nodded and even I was constrained to follow the custom, although, in my ignorance, I could make neither head nor tail of the learned argument. The lawyers present bore a look of such intense absorption that one would have thought their lives depended on what was to come.
“ ‘The plaintiff in reciting the clause,’ said His Holiness quite suddenly, ‘emphasized a very singular phrase “forever and forever,” and also those other words, “so long as the state endures,” but I noticed a curious hesitation upon his part when he came to this word **and**.’
“Here someone in the back of the court yawned in so audible a manner that there was a sudden interruption signalling an ejection, and I learned later that the unfortunate man had perished as he deserved.
“ ‘I appreciate fully,’ continued the judge, ‘that my decision will, subject to appeal, determine in great part the future of this ancient state. Since we are destined soon to acquire and administer the whole world it may be said that my humble remarks upon this occasion will deflect the history of the human race itself. No one can be insensitive to such a responsibility. My duty is clear. The word “**and**,” standing as it does between the first and second parts of the phrase on which is based plaintiff’s claim under the charter, clearly determines that claim. But to determine is to terminate. The plaintiff has therefore no rights here, in the sense of the word “rights” as used by the august body of our Statute and Common Law. He is at the mercy of the Crown, and his claim is disallowed. He may think himself lucky that I have not taken advantage of my full powers and had him whipped at the cart’s tail or thrown down a well. Let record be made and all adjudged as decided.’ With this His Holiness majestically rose, granted a benediction to the kneeling multitude, and was about to leave the court when the pleader Selim interrupted with the words:
“ ‘What about costs, your Godship?’
“ ‘With the Judgment,’ said the judge wearily over his shoulder; and I noticed to my dismay ten pens busily scribbling and wondered what was coming next.

===Chinese===

满堂脑袋再次整齐划一地频频点头，甚至连老夫也受迫于规矩不得不跟着连连颔首，虽说凭老夫粗浅的见识，对这通高深莫测的宏论根本听得如堕五里雾中、完全摸不着头脑。在场的众位律师个个神情凝重专注到了极点，活脱脱宛如自个儿的身家性命全系于法官接下来的金口玉言一般。
“‘原告在诵读条款之际，’法官老爷冷不防话锋一转，‘特意加重了那句极其古怪的措辞“永永远远”，以及另几个字眼“只要国祚绵延”；但老夫敏锐地捕捉到，每当他念到这个“以及（and）”字时，脸上总掠过一丝耐人寻味的犹疑与心虚！’
“讲到这里，公堂后排某位看客打了个响亮至极的大哈欠，堂上巡弋差役登时哗然喝止，当场将其架出公堂驱逐示众；老夫事后才打听到，那个倒霉蛋后来罪有应得地命丧黄泉了。
“‘老夫深知，’法官继续宣判道，‘除却尚待上诉之外，老夫今日所作之裁决，在很大程度上将彻底决断这个古老帝国未来的国运兴衰。鉴于咱们这支帝胄天命所归，不日便将兼并并统治整座大千世界，因而可以说，老夫在此庄严场合所发表的区区微末管见，注定将扭转全人类历史的发展轨迹！面对这等千钧重任，任谁也无法无动于衷。老夫的职责昭若日月：原告依特许状起诉所依据的文句分为前半截与后半截，而这“以及（and）”字恰恰横亘于两截之间，它明明白白地终结断定（determines）了该项诉求。然而在法理上，终结断定即意味着彻底终止消灭（terminate）！因此，依咱们威严神圣的成文法令与普通法（Common Law）对于“权利”一词的崇高法理定义，原告在本堂根本不享有任何合法权利！原告之死活全凭王恩定夺，其诉求依法全数予以驳回不予受理！原告应当深感庆幸：念在老夫法外施仁，未曾动用全权将他绑在囚车车尾抽打示众、或将他直接扔进枯井溺毙！退堂备案，钦此画押！’言罢，法官老爷威风凛凛地霍然站起身来，向满堂跪拜的信众赐下祝福，正欲拂袖离堂而去，冷不防大律师塞利姆尖声打断道：
“‘天神老爷，那诉讼公堂规费由谁承揽？’
“‘悉数由判决承担！’法官头也不回、极其倦怠地抛下一句；老夫惊恐万状地瞧见，堂上竟有十（10）支鹅毛笔正在羊皮纸上沙沙飞速疾书，我暗自心惊肉跳，不知接下来又有什么飞来横祸降临。”

===Original===

“Indeed I was weary of the whole affair and desired nothing better than to hide my humble head in my poor lodging and on the morrow with what was left of my poor little hoard﻿—at the most 400 dinars﻿—to leave for the mainland, and there with this tiny capital attempt to reconstruct my fortunes.
“It was not to be as I imagined. Even as I approached the door of the court I was approached by the pleader Selim, who repeated that phrase hitherto meaningless, ‘What about costs?’ and found I might not depart from the building till I had provided 350 dinars, leaving me with exactly fifty to face the world! Luckily I had my pouch upon me. I hurriedly counted out this last poor remainder of my wealth; then, fearing to return to my lodging (the rent of which I could no longer discharge) I paced hopelessly along the quays through the evening and on through the darkness, until, about midnight, I espied a master mariner about to board his vessel.
“ ‘For what sum,’ I asked him, ‘will you take me on deck to the mainland?’
“ ‘It is a hundred dinars,’ he said roughly.
“ ‘I have it not,’ I answered. My stomach was already clamouring for food. ‘I have but fifty dinars, and some part of that I must reserve for my nourishment lest I perish before reaching land.’
“ ‘Well,’ said he a little less roughly but with no humanity in his tone, ‘you may crawl up forward among the cordage if you like and give me forty-five of your coins; the five you shall keep to feed yourself with when you land.’
“I thanked him humbly for his unexpected kindness. I tried to find some warmth in the chilly night, huddled amid coils of rope on the little deck forward. At dawn the last of the crew came aboard, two great sails were hoisted and we passed out upon the sea.
“Before the sun was high we had dropped over the horizon, and left behind us the palaces of the land where I had thought to find security and repose. There I was, I who had so lately had the world at my disposal, a beggar, hopeless for the coming days, and wondering where on my landing I should find food to keep me living for a week together.”
As Mahmoud was concluding there rose a loud wail, piercing and prolonged which startled him and all the file of boys aligned cross-legged before him upon the floor. It proceeded from the youngest of the nephews.
“What is it, my little fellow?” said his uncle in real alarm.
“Ah! Ah!”

===Chinese===

“老夫对这整场闹剧早已心力交瘁，满心只想钻回自个儿那间破败寓所里把脑袋缩起来，待到翌日凭着残存的一点点微薄私蓄——满打满算顶多剩下四百（400）第纳尔——搭船逃往对岸的大陆，靠着这丁点微末本钱设法东山再起。
“然而造化弄人，事情压根不像老夫盘算得这般轻巧。老夫刚行至公堂门槛，那位大律师塞利姆便快步追了上来，嘴里念念有词地重复着此前对我毫无意义的那句口头禅：‘那诉讼规费由谁承揽？’结果老夫当场被扣留下来，被告诫倘若拿不出三百五十（350）第纳尔，老夫休想踏出这栋大楼半步；这样一来，抛下给我的盘缠恰恰只剩下五十（50）个第纳尔去闯荡江湖！万幸钱袋带在身上。我慌里慌张地数清了这笔最后的可怜余资交差；随后，因害怕返回寓所（我囊中羞涩再也交不起房租了），我失魂落魄地在码头上游荡了整整一个黄昏与黑夜，直到将近子夜时分，我瞧见一位船长正准备登船起航。
“‘要多少银两，’我打听道，‘你才肯让我在甲板上搭乘去往大陆？’
“‘一百（100）第纳尔，’他粗声粗气地吼道。
“‘我拿不出这笔钱，’我答道。此时我的肚皮早已饿得咕咕直叫。‘我手里总共只有五十（50）第纳尔，还得扣除一部分充作干粮口粮，免得还没靠岸就活活饿死在海上。’
“‘行吧，’他的语气稍微缓和了一丝，但依旧冰冷毫无半点人情味，‘你要乐意，就爬到前甲板缆绳堆里蜷着去，把你手里的四十五（45）枚银币给我；剩下的五（5）个子儿留给你自个儿，等你上岸后买口饭吃。’
“对他这番出人意料的善心，我感激涕零、千恩万谢。在这寒意彻骨的夜色中，我缩在前甲板盘绕成堆的粗大缆绳缝隙里瑟瑟发抖，苦苦寻觅一丝暖意。黎明时分，最后一名水手登船归位，两（2）面巨大风帆迎风拉起，海船破浪驶入茫茫大海。
“旭日尚未高升，海岛便已隐没在地平线尽头；那片原本让我以为能觅得安宁与栖身之所的王公殿宇，尽数被我们抛在身后。曾几何时，整座天下都曾任凭我随意驱遣呼应；而此刻，我竟沦为了一个一文不名、对未来万念俱灰的乞丐，满脑子唯独在惶恐发愁：待上了岸，我究竟该去哪儿讨得一口吃食，好苟延残喘支撑过接下来的一周光景。”
讲到此处，马哈茂德的话音戛然而止，公堂前突然爆发出了一声刺耳尖锐且绵延不绝的凄厉哀嚎，吓得老富翁浑身一颤，也惊动了面前席地而坐的一排侄儿少年。那哀嚎声正是从最小的侄儿嘴里传出来的。
“怎么了，小家伙？”叔父着实吃了一惊，关切地问道。
“啊！啊！”

===Original===

sobbed out the poor infant. “Lost! lost! All lost! All that lovely money lost! I cannot bear it, uncle. I cannot bear it!” and he burst into floods of tears.
“For heaven’s sake,” said the old man, rolling upon his seat in his concern for the child, “do not take on so! There is no cause for such a bother. You make too much of it. It is but part of a tale. Do you not see how I have been restored to great fortune? Are you not in this palace of mine with all my slaves around you? and splendid hangings upon the wall? Come, look about you and do not mix up these words of the past with real things that you can touch and see today.”
The little boy tried to stifle his sobs, but they returned with increased violence.
“Oh, uncle, to think that you, who had been so rich, should become so poor; to think that those who gain great wealth cannot keep it forever! Consider all your wealth! Oh, it is terrible, the death and destruction of it!” Throwing himself down upon the marble floor he buried his face in his crossed arms and kicked either foot alternately in the air, in the violent paroxysm of his grief.
His uncle was so moved that he knelt beside the child to soothe him.
“Pray, pray, restrain yourself,” he said. “You will do yourself some hurt. I admire you, my dear boy, I perceive your singular gifts. More than all your elder brothers do you seem to know that which the young should seek in life. You understand indeed what others do not always, all that money means. But it is terrible to see one of your age suffering such agonies from a mere recital of its loss. Indeed, I am moved to console you,” here the old man was tempted to put his hand into his pouch and offer some small coin in consolation to the child, but he recollected himself and continued, “to console you with a pleasant draft of cold water, which I am afraid I forgot to tender to you and your brothers when I last had the pleasure of your attendance.”
The little fellow sat up, still sobbing, but attempting to dry his eyes and moaning from time to time “All that money! All that lovely money!”

===Chinese===

可怜的孩子抽噎着喊道：“全没了！全没了！全都打了水漂！那么多白花花的银子啊！我受不了，叔父，我受不了啊！”说着他便泪如雨下。
“看在苍天的份上，”老人心疼得在软榻上坐立难安，关切地宽慰道，“莫要这般伤心动气！犯不上这般痛心疾首。你太当真了！这不过是陈年旧事的一段故事罢了。难道你没瞧见叔父如今早已重振家业、坐拥万贯家财了吗？你眼下不正是置身于老夫的金碧宫殿之中、被无数奴仆环侍簇拥吗？墙壁上不还悬挂着华丽璀璨的锦缎织毯吗？快，四下里瞧瞧，切莫把昔日的戏言同你今日亲眼所见、亲手所触的真金白银混为一谈啊。”
小男孩竭力想要止住抽泣，但悲痛之情却愈演愈烈、汹涌反扑。
“呜呜，叔父，一想到您曾经富可敌国，后来竟变得那般一贫如洗；一想到那些赚得万贯家财的大人物，竟然没法把金山银山永生永世守住！想想您那些数不尽的钱财啊！天啊，那么多的金银财宝全军覆没、化为乌有，简直太骇人听闻了！”他猛然一头栽倒在冰凉的大理石地板上，将小脸深深埋在交叠的双臂之中，在极度悲恸的抽搐狂乱中，两只脚丫在半空中轮番乱蹬胡踢。
叔父见状大为感动，连忙屈膝跪在孩子身旁悉心安抚劝解。
“行行好，行行好，快克制一下情绪，”他柔声抚慰道，“仔细伤着了身子骨。老夫着实钦佩你，我亲爱的孩子，老夫看出了你身上天纵的非凡慧根！比起你那些年长的兄长们，你似乎比谁都更清楚后生晚辈在尘世间究竟应当追求何物。你确确实实体悟到了旁人往往参悟不透的真谛——那便是金钱的全部神圣意涵！然而瞧见如你这般幼小的孩童，单单听闻金钱亏蚀的叙述便遭受如此撕心裂肺的剧痛，老夫实在于心不忍。老夫着实忍不住想要好好犒劳宽慰你一番，”讲到此处，老人下意识地想把手伸进腰间的钱袋里，摸出一枚碎银赏给孩子以资宽慰，但他猛然回过神来打住了念头，改口接茬道，“……想要用一碗甘甜清凉的冷水来宽慰宽慰你；老夫只怕上回有幸蒙你们兄弟光顾大驾时，老夫一时疏忽忘了给你们端上茶水解渴了。”
小家伙这才挣扎着坐起身来，尽管依旧抽抽搭搭，却开始用袖口擦拭泪眼，嘴里仍时不时抽噎哀叹道：“那么多银子啊！那么多白花花的银子啊！”

===Original===

Pure, cold, crystal water was brought round and gratefully sipped by the boys, who when they had thus refreshed themselves at their uncle’s expense, thanked him warmly and disappeared with reverence from his presence just at the moment when the voice of the muezzin was heard from a neighbouring minaret boring the faithful to tears with its repeated call to prayer.

===Chinese===

一碗碗纯净清冽、晶莹剔透的凉水随即被端了上来，少年们满怀感激地小口啜饮着；在用叔父慷慨奉上的这番破费解渴润喉之后，孩子们热情洋溢地向叔父道谢致敬，随后毕恭毕敬地告退离去。恰在此刻，邻近宣礼塔上传来了宣礼员那令人百无聊赖、催人泪下的唤礼祈祷声，周而复始地折磨着城中虔诚忠信的善男信女们。

===Original===

To

**Oriana Huxley Haynes**

===Chinese===

献给

奥丽安娜·赫胥黎·海恩斯（Oriana Huxley Haynes）

## VII *Ghanamat*, or “The Sheep” / 第七章 绵羊
===Original===

As Mahmoud’s nephews filed in just after the hour of public executions to hear the continuation of their uncle’s absorbing tale, they wore an expression different from that which he had observed on their faces during so many days. The thought of this great man subjected to misfortune like any other, and passing through the trials of actual poverty, had shocked their young and sensitive souls. They had been trained indeed, even in their short experience, to the idea that they and their poor father must suffer contempt; but that the head of the family should ever have passed through such things shook their faith in the world.
The aged merchant, a little concerned with their appearance, warned them that what he had to tell them, not only upon that day but later too, would be concerned with no happy relation. “These were, my children,” said he, “the days of my dereliction. They served to humble me, and often when I have occasion to turn a poor man out of his house or to prosecute some starving widow for debt, or to see to the imprisonment of one who has failed to keep some contract I may have imposed upon him, I sigh and chasten myself with the thought that I myself might (but for the infinite goodness of my God) have been in his or her position. Though, frankly, I cannot say, considering the ineptitude of such people, that I can ever imagine them in mine.” Having so prefaced what he had to tell, the merchant proceeded:
“I sat down, then, in the bows of the boat on that miserable night watching for land. I tried to make some plan, as is the habit of men of my temper, but none would form itself in my fatigued and unhappy brain. One asset I had and one only (over and above the few coins which could hardly last me for more than a day) and that was the dress I wore; for I still carried the fine clothes of my former rank. I had worn them on the occasion of the trial; indeed, I now had no others.
“This accoutrement and a certain proud manner of bearing which I had acquired during the past years of my affluence saved me from insult; though I am not sure that if I had been asked to carry some package for the wealthier of the passengers I should not have accepted the opportunity of reward. I spent about half my poor handful of cash on a meal; with the remainder I purchased provision for the evening.

===Chinese===

行刑斩首的时刻刚过，马哈茂德的侄儿们便鱼贯而入，前来聆听叔父引人入胜的后文。这回，他们脸上的神情迥异于多日以来的常态。一想到这位伟岸无比的长辈竟也如凡夫俗子般横遭不幸，尝尽了赤贫煎熬的滋味，少年们稚嫩而敏感的心灵受到了深深的震动。虽说在他们尚浅的阅历中早已习惯了自个儿和穷酸父亲遭受白眼鄙夷，但一想到堂堂一家之主也曾历经这般劫难，他们对这个世道的敬畏信念不禁摇摇欲坠。
老商人打量着他们的神情，心中略感关切，告诫他们不仅当天、甚至日后要讲的诸多往事，都绝非什么令人开怀的乐事。“孩子们啊，”他说，“那些岁月乃是老夫流落穷途、落魄无依的日子。正因有了那些磨难，才教会了老夫谦卑顺服；往后每逢老夫不得不将穷汉逐出屋宅、或逼债状告嗷嗷待哺的孤儿寡母、或将未能履约的负债人投入大牢之时，老夫总会抚膺长叹，暗自自省：若非真主浩荡洪恩，老夫本人何尝不也可能沦落到他们这般凄惨田地？不过凭良心说，鉴于此类平庸之辈生就的愚钝无能，老夫委实无法想象他们有朝一日能坐上老夫今日的位置。”这番开场白交代已毕，商人便接着叙述道：
“在那凄风苦雨的绝望之夜，老夫坐在船头，极目搜寻着陆地的踪影。正如老夫这类脾性之人向来的习气，老夫竭力想要盘算出一套谋划，可是在疲惫不堪、愁云惨雾的脑海里，却怎么也拼凑不出个头绪来。老夫手里唯独剩下一样本钱（除却几枚勉强只够支撑一天的零碎铜板之外），那便是身上穿的这套行头；因老夫身上依然穿着昔日显贵官位时的锦衣华服。当初对簿公堂时老夫便是这副穿戴；说实话，老夫手头也委实没有别的衣裳了。
“正是这身华贵行头，加上老夫昔日腰缠万贯时所养成的几分睥睨自傲的气度，免去了老夫在船上受人欺凌辱骂；不过倘若当时有富商豪客肯赏两个铜板雇我扛行李，老夫也难保自个儿不会为了赏钱而折腰效劳。上岸之后，老夫花掉手中那把可怜铜钱的大半饱餐了一顿；用剩下的余钱买了些干粮，留作晚饭充饥。”

===Original===

“The town in which I found myself was﻿—happily﻿—too busy and populous a mart to pay attention to a chance wanderer. Lacking all direction, trusting as I had trusted long ago to fortune or rather to Providence, I betook myself after the worst heat of the day had passed, to a chance track which led first along the river side, above the harbour, and afterwards climbed through the gardens of the city to the hills beyond.
“The countryside was here of a nature more fertile than the countries through which I had hitherto passed. The dense trees of the woods made a grateful shade for me in that silent afternoon, and when I had passed beyond these on my upward journey I came to a great rolling land of sparse grass, feed for cattle.
“It was hired out, it would seem, to graziers; for I saw some little way off standing in the attitude of a shepherd and holding his bent staff beside his vigorous old frame, a very remarkable figure. I approached him without any set idea of what my adventure might lead to. I only knew that things could not be worse. Perhaps I had somewhere in my mind a guess, half-formed, that I could be of some service at some small wage. At any rate I accosted him. I have seldom seen an expression more haughty and vigorous and marvelled that it should be that of a hired man. He was perhaps sixty years of age with a strong bearing, eyes luminous and almost fierce, and a face in outline that of a hawk, or better, that of an eagle. And as he stood there he watched, grazing before him, a great flock of sheep, well fed, and fat; of a high breed, excellent to behold. There were at least a thousand of these and it would seem that the religion of this part (for they also were true believers) did not forbid the use of bells. For I heard a multitudinous tinkling come up from the flock as it moved. Very far away the plateau made an edge against the sky, and between that horizon and the summit to which I had reached, folds, with water pools concealed in them, diversified the great sweep. But there were no trees. All was bare and majestic under the sky as the light melted towards evening.
“The shepherd returned my salutation, accepted my offer to share the very scanty food which I had purchased and so sat down before me on the ground to eat.
“As we ate we grew acquainted. I told him frankly enough of my misfortune, though not in detail.

===Chinese===

“万幸的是，老夫所踏足的那座市镇商贾云集、人烟稠密，根本没人会去留意一个偶遇的异乡流浪客。老夫毫无明确方向，宛如很久以前那般，全心寄托于运气，更确切地说是寄托于天命前程。待到午后最酷烈的暑气消退，老夫便踏上了一条蜿蜒小径；那小径起初沿着港口上方的河畔延伸，随后穿过城郭边缘的果园菜圃，一路攀缘伸向城外的丘陵荒原。
“比起老夫此前途经的大陆各处，这片乡野显得更为沃腴丰饶。在静谧幽深的午后，林间繁茂蓊郁的古树洒下了令人心旷神怡的清凉浓荫；待老夫穿过林木继续拾级而上，眼前豁然开朗，呈现出一片广袤起伏、长满稀疏牧草的起伏丘陵高原，正是一处放牧牛羊的绝佳牧场。
“看光景，这片高原多半是租给各大牧主的；因老夫远远瞧见前方伫立着一个极不寻常的身影，那人手拄弯头牧羊杖，矍铄苍老的身躯呈现出一副典型的牧羊人姿态。老夫并未盘算什么明确图谋便朝他迎面走去。老夫心里唯独清楚一点：眼下的境况断不可能比现在更糟了。老夫心底或许隐隐掠过一丝未成形的念头：或许能给他打打下手，讨口残羹冷炙或微薄工钱度日。不管怎样，老夫上前同他搭了话。老夫生平罕见有谁的眼神能如他这般傲岸凌厉、老当益壮，老夫着实惊诧于一个雇工奴仆身上竟会有这等气派！他年纪约莫六十（60）岁上下，身形硬朗，目光炯炯有神、近乎凶悍，面庞轮廓宛如苍鹰，或者说恰似一只苍劲的雄鹰。他就那般伫立在高原上，凝视着眼前正悠闲啃食牧草的一大群羊羔——只只膘肥体壮、毛色油亮，显然是极名贵上乘的品种，令人赏心悦目。整群绵羊少说也有一千（1,000）只上下；看来当地的宗教规矩（因他们亦是真主的虔诚信徒）并不禁绝在牲口身上佩戴铃铛。随着羊群走动移步，漫山遍野飘荡起一片清脆细碎的悦耳叮咚之声。在极目远方，高原的边缘如刀削般切齐苍穹；而在那天际线与老夫立足的山峦峰巅之间，数不清的山峦褶皱中隐匿着处处水塘清泉，点缀着这片苍茫辽阔的大地。然而漫山荒原并无半棵树木。夕阳西下，天光在暮色中逐渐消融，四野显得荒凉而无比雄浑壮阔。
“牧羊人回应了老夫的问候致意，欣然接受了老夫同他分食手中那点可怜口粮的提议，便在老夫对面的草地上席地而坐，一道吃了起来。
“在一口口咀嚼咽食之中，我俩逐渐熟络交谈起来。老夫相当坦诚地向他吐露了自个儿的不幸横祸，虽说并未和盘托出全部细枝末节。”

===Original===

‘I was,’ said I, ‘only the day before yesterday a rich man. Today I am what you see, and my last piece of silver is gone.’ He looked at me gravely and said that there was One Who gave and Who took away. His Name be exalted. ‘These sheep, for instance,’ said he, ‘are the property of a man contemptible in every way, foolish, irascible, a bad master and (one would have thought) an unwise merchant. Yet he prospers while I, the shepherd, remain upon a hire too small to permit me to save. And so it has been for years!’
“ ‘I have not,’ added he a little bitterly, ‘the faculties for that sort of life which my master pursues. At any rate I am quite certain of this: that by any common judgment of men he is the inferior and I the lord. Yet here I am!﻿ ﻿… The world deals harshly by poor men.’ He looked at me to see if the words sank in.
“As he thus spoke (his sadness seemed to relieve my own with a sense of our common dependence) the sun now near the horizon warned us of prayer and I was glad indeed to see that this new chance companion was as much alive as I to the duties we owe our Maker. He fell upon his knees and bowed to the evening prayer as I did beside him, and for some moments, as we recited the sacred formula, all worldly thoughts passed from my mind and I think from his also. We rose at the same moment from this exercise, each filled, I felt, with brotherhood. I was the first to break the consecrated silence.
“I did so by asking him whether he had never thought, in the course of his long years as a shepherd, how money might be made by the stealing of his master’s sheep, or by some trick with them? Whether he had never had the opportunity to blackmail his master, or in some other way to increase his fortune? For it seemed intolerable to me that a man such as he described his employer to be, should be wealthy while he were poor. He shrugged his shoulders as though in despair and answered simply:
“ ‘In the distant past I often attempted such things; but invariably have I failed. Indeed, the master graziers of this part know me well for one who has attempted at their expense every kind of bold chance, and I would never have employment from them were it not for my skill in lambing and in every other part of the trade. As it is they watch me rigorously. Their spies are everywhere. I could not, I fear, make one dinar by any one of the methods you suggest.

===Chinese===

“‘实不相瞒，’我说，‘就在前天老夫还是个坐拥万贯家财的富翁。如今却沦落为你眼前见到的这副模样，连最后一文银钱也花得精光了。’他面色庄重地凝视着我，说道：主宰一切的唯有那一位，赏赐的是祂，收回的亦是祂。愿祂的神名永享赞颂！‘譬如这群羊吧，’他说，‘乃是属于一个在各个方面皆令人不齿的鄙劣之徒，那家伙既愚蠢透顶又暴躁易怒，待仆役苛刻之极，按理说（任谁看来）都是个蠢不可及的买卖人。然而那等货色却日进斗金、发迹享福，而老夫这个放羊倌，所得工钱微薄得根本攒不下半文养老钱。岁岁年年，皆是如此！’
“‘老夫身上，’他又略带苦涩地补充道，‘委实缺乏我那个东家所擅长的那套市侩本领。无论如何，老夫唯独敢咬定一点：若按凡人的公道是非公论，他在各个方面皆属下三滥，老夫才配称得上一方之主！可老夫如今却偏偏沦落在此！……这个世道对待穷苦之人何其刻薄寡恩！’他眯起双眼打量着我，想看看这番话是否戳中了我的心坎。
“听着他这通倾诉（他眉宇间的忧伤仿佛以同病相怜的沦落感抚慰了我的凄苦），渐渐沉入地平线的落日提醒我们到了礼拜的时辰；眼见这位偶遇的新同伴同我一样，对敬奉造物主的神圣天职恪守不怠，老夫心中甚是欣慰。他屈膝跪倒在地，朝向落日祷告晚礼拜，老夫亦并肩跪倒在他身旁；在念诵神圣经文的几个片刻间，一切尘世纷扰皆从老夫脑海中一扫而空，老夫相信他亦复如是。礼拜完毕，我俩同时起立，心中皆充盈着兄弟般的融融暖意。老夫率先打破了这片神圣的静默。
“老夫开腔试探着问他：在他漫长放牧的悠悠岁月里，难道他就从未琢磨过：如何靠盗卖东家的肥羊、或是暗中玩弄些见不得人的掉包手脚来大捞一笔？难道他就从未逮住过机会勒索东家一番，或是借由其他门道来发家致富？因在老夫看来，一个如他口中所述的那般鄙陋蠢材腰缠万贯，而他这等傲骨之人却一贫如洗，简直是天理难容。他无可奈何地耸了耸肩，干脆利落地答道：
“‘在久远的昔日，老夫何尝没有多次尝试过此类勾当；但无一例外每次都折戟沉沙！实不相瞒，这一带的各大牧场主早就将老夫摸得一清二楚，深知老夫曾屡屡打过他们财产的如意算盘；若非凭着老夫替母羊接羔接生以及料理牲口行当里无可替代的绝活手艺，他们是断断不肯赏老夫一口饭吃的。饶是如此，他们平日里依旧对我严防死守，耳目安插得到处皆是。老夫只怕，靠你方才提及的任何下三滥手段，老夫连一（1）个第纳尔也捞不着。”

===Original===

I have in my time tried them all. I have forged receipts; I have sold sheep which afterwards I entered as dead from accident; I have falsified the returns of the lambing; I have sometimes raised a sum of money upon the flock under pretence that it was my own. My only reward has been fine and imprisonment and cruel torture. But the truth is that I have not the faculties of the merchant. They are, I take it, granted to some and withheld from others. For my part I have despaired of their exercise and shall never turn to them again.’
“His words filled me at once with pity and with hope, and (since ingenuity is never long absent from men of my temper) a scheme suddenly appeared.
“ ‘Why should not we,’ I said, after I had gathered wood and lit a fire to meet the approaching darkness, ‘enter into partnership? I think I may say without boasting that I possess in a singular degree those faculties which you say you lack. God made me in every part for a merchant. I can conceal, distort, forestall, outdo, bully, terrify and even boldly snatch, far better than any other man I have come across. Only once in my life have I fallen into the weakness of trusting others and as you see I have paid bitterly for that weakness.’ So I spoke, not noticing that I was yet again committing the same error in suggesting partnership to a mere stranger. But in truth Allah had blinded me; purposing to make me taste misfortune to the full that I might the better adore His later beneficence.
“ ‘All these talents I have in abundance and more also,’ I continued, ‘for God has been very good to me. You, on the other hand, have what I lack; that is, a knowledge of the towns round about and of their markets; of the value of sheep; of the system which has been organized for the catching of ingenious men; and of how that system may be avoided. Between us, then, we have all the things needed for our success. Come, let us determine with the very next breaking of the light to try our fortunes together.’
“After I had thus spoken the shepherd looked at me long and anxiously over the fire, the reflection of which shone in his piercing eyes. I wondered whether he were wavering and to what conclusion he would come. At last he spoke, slowly enough.
“ ‘I am not willing,’ said he, ‘I am not willing﻿ ﻿… but I will take the risk. The worst that can come to me I have already suffered.

===Chinese===

“‘在老夫这辈子手底下，此类勾当全都挨个儿试过一遍。老夫伪造过收据账目；老夫暗中变卖过羊只，回头在账簿上谎报意外暴毙；老夫篡改过产羔繁殖清册；老夫甚至曾假冒牲口归自个儿所有而拿去抵押借款。然而老夫所换来的唯一赏赐，无非是沉重的罚金、暗无天日的大牢以及惨无人道的严刑拷打！追根究底，说到底乃是老夫根本没有经商做买卖的狡黠天赋。在老夫看来，这等禀赋全凭天意，真主赐予了某些人，便断绝了旁人。就老夫而言，老夫早已对此心灰意冷，这辈子绝不敢再起半点非分之想了。’
“听完他这番肺腑之言，老夫心头顿时涌起一股交织着怜悯与希望的热流；（鉴于如老夫这般秉性之人脑筋向来转得飞快），一条妙计陡然在脑海中浮现。
“‘既然如此，’待老夫拾拢柴火升起篝火抵御侵袭而来的苍茫夜色后，老夫开口说道，‘咱们为何不联手入伙做一桩买卖呢？老夫自问毫不夸口地说，在经商天赋这桩事上，你所欠缺的一切狡黠才智，老夫皆以超凡绝伦的程度兼而有之！真主将老夫浑身上下的每一根骨头都造就成了天生的商业奇才！老夫善于隐匿真伪、颠倒黑白、抢占先机、以智压人、威逼恐吓乃至明火执仗强取豪夺，手段远迈老夫生平所遇见的任何凡夫俗子。老夫这辈子唯独跌倒过一次软肋——那便是轻信了旁人的鬼话，正如你所见，老夫为了这层软肋付出了惨痛至极的代价！’老夫这般大言不惭地夸夸其谈，浑然不觉自个儿竟然重蹈覆辙，向一个素昧平生的陌生汉子提议结盟入伙。实则真主蒙蔽了老夫的心智；真主定意要让老夫将世间的苦头尝尽受绝，好让老夫日后能更加虔诚敬畏祂浩瀚深沉的神圣洪恩！
“‘所有这些才干本领老夫皆绰绰有余，且远不止于此，’老夫继续煽动道，‘因造物主待老夫委实恩重如山！而反过来，你身上恰恰坐拥老夫所欠缺的长处：那便是你熟稔周遭城镇市集的门道与行情；精通羊群的真实身价；洞悉那些专为缉拿机巧之徒而设下的天罗地网，并且深知该如何避开风头暗度陈仓。如此一来，你我二人联手，做成大买卖所需的一切要件便全数齐备了！来吧，趁着明朝第一缕曙光破晓，咱们便联手闯荡一番，去市集上撞撞大运！’
“听完老夫这通游说，牧羊人隔着熊熊跳动的篝火，久久凝视着老夫，跳跃的火光在他炯炯如炬的双眼里闪烁明灭。老夫暗自揣度他心意是否已然动摇，究竟会给出怎样的决断。过了好半晌，他终于慢吞吞地开了腔。
“‘老夫本不乐意，’他说，‘老夫本不乐意冒这趟险……但老夫姑且赌上一把。天底下最惨烈的罪受，老夫横竖早已挨过个遍。”

===Original===

At the best’﻿—and he pointed towards the vast flock of sheep now a mass of glimmering white as they lay in the darkness﻿—‘at the best we should each acquire provision for many years.’
“ ‘Oh! fool!’ thought I, ‘provision for many years! Does he not know how money breeds?’ But in open speech I said, ‘Yes, we will divide the spoil and go our ways. I with my share and you with yours.’
“ ‘Precisely,’ he answered with a curious smile which, for the moment, intrigued me. ‘You with your share and I with mine.’
“As the night passed I entertained him with the details of my plan. Since it was I who had to do the work while he would have to command (from his knowledge of the trade), I proposed that he should be the master and I the man. To all this he nodded assent. He was also prepared to meet me in my suggestion that he should put on my fine clothes and I his rags, the better to carry out our parts. ‘This will,’ said I, ‘seem strange while you are driving the sheep, but as we approach the town and market to which you shall direct me, if there be one nearby, I will attempt to take over your task and under your direction I may at least complete it by bringing the flock to the place of sale. I will speak of you as the owner. My fine dress which you wear will carry out that deception and deceive all into thinking that it is an honest transaction. The sum upon which we shall agree with the purchaser shall be paid to *you*; and not until the whole transaction is over, and we well out of the gate, shall I ask for the division, which I take it should be in equal halves.’
“To all this also he agreed; only asking whether I would not like (as, after all, I had only just met him) to have the money paid to both?
“I urged him to keep to my plan. His receiving the money as master would seem natural and excite no surprise. We could divide in private at our leisure.
“To my surprise he made me a low bow at this; but I put it down to custom, and went on with my plan.
“For the sake of a rough calculation I asked him what sheep were fetching, and he said that in the neighbouring Ksar, which might be called a straggling market town or a large village, there was to be held, it so happened, the very next day a sheep market, where we must find ourselves shortly after sunrise. It was distant less than an hour across the uplands.

===Chinese===

“‘往好处想，’他抬手指了指在夜幕中泛着一片微茫白光的庞大羊群，‘往好处想，此番得手之后，你我各自皆能攒下受用多年的丰厚用度！’
“‘哼！蠢货！’老夫心底暗笑，‘仅仅是受用多年的用度？难道这蠢汉竟不知金钱是如何以钱生钱、利滚利的吗？’但嘴上老夫却满口应承道：‘正是！咱们变卖之后二一添作五瓜分赃银，各奔前程！老夫拿老夫的那份，你拿你的那份！’
“‘说得好极了，’他脸上浮现出一抹耐人寻味的古怪微笑，那一瞬间倒让老夫心中掠过一丝诧异，‘你拿你的一半，老夫拿老夫的一半。’
“漫漫长夜之中，老夫巨细靡遗地向他传授着老夫的周密谋略。鉴于具体鞍前马后的苦力杂务由老夫来承揽，而发号施令的大权则委诸于他（凭借他对牲口行情的熟稔），老夫提议由他充任东家主人，老夫则屈尊扮作仆役长工。对此他点头称是。老夫进一步提议由他换上老夫身上这套华贵锦袍，而老夫则换上他那身破烂褴褛的羊皮粗布，如此方能将这出好戏演得天衣无微；对此他也爽快配合。‘这样一来，’老夫盘算道，‘一路上由你赶羊或许显得古怪；但待行至你引路的市镇集市附近，若有市集，老夫便设法接手赶羊的苦差，在你的居高临下指引点拨下将羊群赶赴交易场。老夫在众人面前尊称你为牧场东家。你身上所穿的老夫这套锦衣华服将彻底坐实这一假象，叫全场所有人皆以为这是一桩堂堂正正的显贵买卖。与买家谈妥的全部金银银票由你亲自受领；直到整笔交割彻底落定、你我二人安然踏出城门关卡之后，老夫再向你讨要平分的分成，老夫以为二一添作五对半平分最为公道。’
“对此他也一概满口赞同；唯独反问老夫一句：鉴于老夫毕竟同他初次相逢，难道老夫就不希望让买家当庭将银钱同时分付给咱们二人？
“老夫极力劝说他务必恪守老夫的周密方略。由他以东家老爷的身份出面收受巨款，显得天经地义、绝不引人疑窦。至于平分赃银，待事成之后你我二人在私下从容交割即可。
“出乎老夫意料的是，听完这番话，他竟向老夫深深鞠了一躬；老夫只当是这乡野粗人的感恩成习，便继续敲定全盘计划。
“为了大体估算油水，老夫向他打听市面上羊只的行市价钱；他透露在邻近的一座克萨尔（Ksar）城堡集镇——那是一座半散落的市集小镇兼大村落——碰巧就在次日破晓有一场大型活羊牲畜交易市集，咱们必须在日出后不久赶到堂前。打这片高原横穿过去，路程不足一（1）个时辰。”

===Original===

The purchasers came from all parts, and as the bidding was likely to be brisk we might expect for the flock as a whole not less than 1,800 or even 2,000 pieces of gold. As he spoke I already felt that capital in my possession, or, at least, half of it, and I thought things would go hard with me if after our first successful transaction I could not carry on my partner to another and another, until at last I had manipulated him out of his share also.
“We discussed all further details through the night, rehearsed our parts, and had the whole perfect when the first glimmer of dawn showed in the East beyond the edges of the hills beneath a waning moon.
“We rose; the flock was gathered; our garments exchanged; and I could not but admire my companion, now that he was dressed in what seemed a manner so much more suitable to his carriage and features. He looked a very master, a prosperous lord of men, and I congratulated him upon the effect, which, I assured him would allay all suspicion, since all would take him for a very great lord indeed.
“Again he smiled that intriguing smile and bowed too low. But I was a little nettled at his affecting to play the part thoroughly by addressing me in sharp tones and with the air of a superior. I, for my part, shivered in the dawn in the miserable rags which I had taken from him, and holding his staff awkwardly enough tramped on in my character of the servant.
“Soon, just as the sun rose, the dusty hedges and the cracked yellowish walls of the Ksar appeared in a hollow through which ran a muddy stream. From the enclosures within, rose the bleating of many sheep which had been driven to the same market, and we observed upon the folds of land beyond several flocks arriving in convergence upon the same place.
“My companion told me while we were still out of earshot that an early arrival would allow us to watch the movement of prices and, what was of more value to us, might enable us to get away before opportunity for pursuit should arise.
“He gave me my last instructions, recalling all that we had arranged in the night.

===Chinese===

“买主们来自四面八方，鉴于竞价竞标向来极尽热络，整群羊通盘盘算下来，料想少说也能斩获不下一千八百（1,800）乃至两千（2,000）枚金币。听他这番估价，老夫只觉那笔巨额本钱已然落入囊中，至少有一半唾手可得；老夫更暗自盘算：倘若初战告捷之后，老夫岂能不诱使这位合伙人一桩接一桩继续做下去，直到最终连他自个儿的那份老本也全数被老夫玩弄于股掌之间、尽数榨干为止！
“整个漫漫长夜，我俩反复推敲一切细枝末节，将各自的分工排演得滚瓜烂熟；待到残月之下东方地平线的群山边缘泛起第一抹灰白晨曦之际，全盘谋划已然天衣无缝。
“我俩霍然起身；将羊群聚拢成队；彼此互换了衣裳行头；瞧着同伴穿戴齐整之后的模样，老夫忍不住暗自喝彩：换上这身锦袍，同他的轩昂气宇和苍鹰面孔简直浑然天成。他活脱脱便是一位执掌乾坤的东家大老爷、号令群雄的显贵领主；老夫连忙恭维他的卓然风采，向他保证这身气派定能打消任何市井看客的疑窦，任谁都会将他视作一方豪强巨贾。
“他又一次露出了那抹耐人寻味的神秘微笑，躬身还了一记过分谦卑的大礼。但他随即全情投入角色、用尖厉傲慢的语调摆出上等人架势冲老夫呼来喝去，这倒让老夫心头略感恼火。至于老夫自个儿，套着从他身上剥下来的褴褛破布，在破晓的寒风中瑟瑟发抖，笨拙地抓着他的弯头牧羊杖，老老实实扮演起贱役长工的苦命角色，在泥泞中踉跄前行。
“不久，正当红日破土喷薄而出之际，在那条流淌着浑浊泥溪的凹谷之中，隐隐露出了克萨尔土城灰蒙蒙的树篱和斑驳龟裂的土黄城垣。从城垣圈围的牲口围栏里，早已传来了无数早市羊群的嘈杂咩咩哀鸣；而在周遭起伏的褶皱山梁上，老夫望见数路羊群正从四面八方汇聚涌向同一座市集。
“趁着四下尚无耳目窥探，老夫的同伴低声关照老夫：尽早进场不仅能让咱们洞察市价涨跌走势，更要紧的是，便于咱们在追兵察觉之前脚底抹油尽早开溜。
“他向老夫交代了最后的行事规矩，将夜里商定妥帖的暗号全盘温习了一遍。”

===Original===

‘I, for my part,’ said he, as the first sheep of our great flock entered the narrow streets, ‘shall fall behind you and take my way to the pavement outside the Mosque reserved for the principal merchants, and there with all due dignity and honour await your report, while you go forward to the market place beyond the Mosque: when you have got a purchaser, come back and find me. For such is here the custom. It is the servant who negotiates, the master who confirms. The servant leads the purchaser to his superior, and that superior takes the purchase money. Do you, for your part, cast around until you hear the conversation of the bidders and see that you sell at a price not less than 2,000 pieces of gold.’ He then gave the number of our flock all told, which he made me repeat to a single point in ewes and in lambs. When he had said this he fell behind me and went up a street which led to the Mosque; while I, in some doubt of my capacity, but putting what bold face I could upon the matter, went straight before me along the broad way toward the market beyond. There I drove all the flock into a great pen, of which certain were reserved for the vendors in that mart.
“The market soon filled with buyers. They came in little groups, prodding the sheep, feeling the wool and sometimes looking into their mouths; and the flock which I had the honour of commanding was the most admired of all. I was asked by several if I would not sell singly; but estimating the eagerness of the buyers I shook my head and said I could not sell for less than a reserve sum of two thousand pieces of gold, nor could I break the flock. I added that it was a pity to do so as it was a pedigree flock, every single animal being descended from the famous ram which had spoken in a human voice to the Holy Hassan three hundred years ago by order of the Most High. I admitted that this origin made little difference to the mutton, but I pointed out its extraordinary effect upon the wool.
“With that the bidding began and I noticed with great pleasure one tall, dark, very thin man among the rest, slow of gesture, fixed of eye, who never took his looks from my face and who, just after each last bid, would raise it by fifty pieces of gold. He was not to be beaten. One competitor after another dropped out.

===Chinese===

“‘至于老夫自个儿，’当咱们浩浩荡荡的羊群第一批蹄爪踏入狭窄街巷之际，他关照道，‘老夫便落在你身后，径直前往清真寺门外专为顶流大商贾预留的石阶上落座；在那儿摆足威严体面恭候你的回音，你则径直把羊赶往清真寺后方的牲畜交易广场：一旦谈妥了称心如意的买家，你便折返回来向老夫通报。因这乃是本地世代沿袭的成规：向来由奴仆掌柜出面讨价还价，东家老爷亲自拍板定夺。奴仆引着买主前来拜见主子，由主子亲手收缴交割银两。你只管在市集上四处搭讪探听，摸清各路买家的竞标底牌，咬死整群羊出手总价决计不得低于两千（2,000）枚金币。’言毕，他报出了这批羊的总数口数，逼着老夫逐一复述确认，连母羊和羊羔各有多少只都分毫不差。交代完毕，他便脱离队伍，拐进了一条通往清真寺的上行街巷；而老夫虽对自个儿的赶羊本领心存几分忐忑，却也只得硬着头皮打肿脸充胖子，挥动皮鞭赶着羊群沿着宽阔大路直奔前方的广场。行至市集，老夫将全部牲口驱赶进一座专供各路货主圈禁牲口的巨大木栅围栏里。
“不出多时，集市上便挤满了各路买家。他们三五成群凑上前来，用木棍戳弄羊身，摩挲翻检羊毛，时不时掰开羊嘴查验牙口齿龄；而老夫有幸统领的这群肥羊，无疑成了全场最令人赞叹垂涎的极品。有好几位买主凑上前来打听能否拆零单卖；但老夫掂量着买主们饥渴难耐的抢购势头，傲慢地连连摇头，声称低于两千（2,000）枚金币的保底底价概不松口，且绝不拆散零卖。老夫更信口雌黄地吹嘘道：拆零卖委实暴殄天物，因这批羊乃是血统极其纯正的名门后裔，每一只皆是三百（300）年前奉至高真主神谕开口口吐人言点化圣徒哈桑（Hassan）的那只通灵神羊之嫡系后代！老夫坦言这等高贵血统或许对羊肉风味无甚差异，但对羊毛品质的拔群提升却堪称夺天地之造化！
“话音未落，竞价竞标便火爆开锣。老夫满心欢喜地注意到人群中立着一位身材修长、面色黎黑、瘦骨嶙峋的汉子；那人举止沉着缓慢，目光如磐石般死死咬住老夫的脸庞，每逢旁人喊出最新报价，他便不慌不忙地加价五十（50）枚金币。他大有志在必得、誓不罢休之势。各路竞争买家纷纷败下阵来，相继弃标。”

===Original===

At last when the magnificent sum of 2,832 pieces of gold had been bid by the mysterious stranger I clapped my hands together as the signal and used the formula ‘Heaven has decided.’ The stranger approached me, drawing from his girdle a reed and a small horn of ink. I thought we were about to sign the transfer﻿—it seemed to me an odd formality, seeing that he had but to drive the beasts away and leave me the bag of gold. I was undeceived. He presented me no charter of transfer, no deed, but a strange piece of writing such as I had not seen before and asked me for its counterpart. I was startled and a little confused. ‘What counterpart?’ said I.
“ ‘Do you mean,’ said he in clear tones, so that the curious bystanders should overhear, ‘that you have no permit?’
“At this the audience tittered, and others, scenting amusement, crowded round to gaze and follow.
“ ‘Have you no permit?’ he repeated severely.
“I felt myself growing hot and confused under the laughter which followed; and even alarmed when I heard one buyer say contemptuously to his neighbour, ‘They’ve caught another of ’em!’
“I confessed that I had never even heard of such an instrument.
“ ‘Follow me,’ said the stranger grimly, and whether from curiosity or from a conviction growing in me that he had authority, I followed humbly enough, leaving my large flock bleating in its pen.
“The stranger (for he now showed that he had upon him the keys of the market) locked the gate of the pen, appointed (another proof of his authority) a slave to stand by and see that no one interfered with the property I had transferred to him, and motioned to others to fall in behind us. He then led me away, and I was more concerned than ever to notice the strange smiles of those who saw the little procession which we made, he going first with his great staff, I treading behind.
“He led me to where the Sheiks of the neighbourhood, the principal sheep owners and magistrates, sat in solemnity before the Mosque; an awe-inspiring company. Grand and splendid among them, in their very centre and clearly the most revered of them all, I perceived my late companion the shepherd, all dressed up in my own fine clothes, but having now added ornaments reserved for him, and looking for all the world like the king of the place.

===Chinese===

“最终，当那位高深莫测的神秘买家喊出了两千八百三十二（2,832）枚金币的天价之际，老夫当空击掌为号，朗声唱出了定约的切口咒语：‘真主已作裁决！’那神秘买家随即阔步朝老夫走来，从腰带间抽出一支芦苇笔和一小截墨水角筒。老夫原以为这是要当场签署过户契约画押——在老夫看来这等文书过场纯属多此一举，因他只需把羊赶走、将那袋沉甸甸的金子留下即可。然而老夫大错特错了！他递给老夫的既非转让敕令，亦非过户契约，而是一张老夫生平从未谋面的古怪字据，并冷冷向老夫索要对应的存根副联。老夫当场怔住了，满头雾水地反问道：‘什么副联？’
“‘你的意思是，’他嗓门陡然拔高，洪亮得足以让周围好奇围观的市井看客听得一清二楚，‘你手里竟然没有官府批文准许令（permit）？’
“听到这话，围观的人群中爆发出一阵嗤笑，旁人嗅到了看热闹的乐子，纷纷潮水般涌上前来围观尾随。
“‘你身上究竟有没有批文？’他声色俱厉地逼问道。
“在四面八方的哄笑嘲弄声中，老夫只觉脸上火辣辣地发烫、局促难安；尤其是听到身旁一位买主用极其鄙夷刻薄的口吻对同伴嘲弄道：‘瞧啊，官家又逮着一个倒霉蛋！’老夫心底更是猛地咯噔一下，涌起一阵不祥的预感。
“老夫只得硬着头皮坦白承认，自个儿生平连听都未曾听说过这等劳什子公文。
“‘跟老夫走！’那神秘人冷酷阴沉地喝道。不知是出于探究究竟的好奇，抑或是心底越来越笃定此人乃是执掌公权的官差，老夫乖乖低眉顺眼地跟在他身后，抛下木栅围栏里咩咩哀鸣的大群肥羊。
“那陌生人（此时他亮出了随身携带着的集市大铁锁钥匙）咔哒一声锁死了栅栏大门，随后（再次彰显其官府特权）指派了一名黑奴侍卫严加把守，严禁任何人染指老夫刚刚转手给他的这批牲口，并挥手示意围观的看客们在后头紧跟随行。他便这般押解着老夫前行；瞧见沿途市井闲汉们脸上露出的古怪阴鸷冷笑，老夫心头愈发忐忑不安。他手持沉重的执法木杖昂首阔步在前，老夫亦步亦趋跟在后头。
“他将老夫押解到了邻近各大谢赫（Sheiks）、顶流大牧主与地方执政法官们在清真寺前威严齐聚的石阶高台之上；好一派令人肃然起敬的森严阵仗！就在这群显贵要人的正中央、万众瞩目且俨然最受尊崇的首席宝座之上，老夫惊恐万状地赫然认出了老夫先前的同伴——那位放羊倌！只见他身披老夫那套锦衣华服，此刻更增添了专属于部落首领的璀璨配饰，活脱脱宛如整座疆域的土皇帝一般！”

===Original===

“At our approach he turned an indignant glance upon me, rose to his feet, and addressing the stranger who had captured me, cried in a terrible voice:
“ ‘Officer! Do you bring me yet another of these evildoers? Whose sheep had he driven off? And is it a case of a forged permit or what?’
“I already saw how the land lay, and I quailed to think what was before me. The owners﻿—I rightly guessed﻿—had suffered from sheep stealing; had established permits, signed by them, in order to check fraudulent sales; had plotted to catch the thief culprits, and this perfidious man had disguised himself as a servant in order to catch such, and had caught *me*. The officer who had arrested me spoke:
“ ‘My lord,’ he said, ‘we have caught this ruffian’﻿—pointing to me﻿—‘selling *your* sheep without any permit at all! He must have driven them through the night. By the law which your council proclaimed last year, just after the Fast, his punishment lies in your hands. The owner has the determination of it.’
“To my astonishment and horror my former companion looked on me with a dreadful face of scorn and said: ‘Tell me all, that I may apportion the punishment due to him. I had had him in my employ for but a short time. I mistrusted him from the first. Tell me all!’
“ ‘I have so found him selling your lordship’s sheep. They fetched nearly 3,000 pieces of gold,’ answered the officer grimly. ‘He shall make an excellent example for, my lord, he is the first whom we have caught in this market trying to sell without a permit. There can be no doubt (I have witnesses to it) that he proposed to take the purchase money and (perhaps with some accomplice whom I have not traced) to fly.’
“On hearing this my former companion, clenching his hands and showing as intense a passion as his dignity would allow﻿—a magnificent figure in those clothes of his (my clothes)﻿—cried, ‘What? Is it possible that one whom I have nourished, tended and befriended should be guilty of so abominable a crime? How wise we were to make the regulation! How excellent and zealous are you in your office thus to have found the first culprit who attempted theft in this place! How admirable that he should be brought to justice before he could consummate his crime! How marked is the work of Providence’﻿—here he lifted his eyes to heaven﻿—‘which has given him up to us for an example!

===Chinese===

“见我们走近，他居高临下向老夫投来一道义愤填膺的凛冽怒视，霍然站起身来，转向擒拿老夫的那位差官，以雷霆万钧之势咆哮喝问道：
“‘差官！难道你又替老夫拿获了一名这等作奸犯科的鼠窃狗盗之徒吗？他盗赶的是哪一家的羊群？此案究竟是伪造官印批文，还是其他什么勾当？’
“此时老夫早已心如明镜，看清了眼前的杀局，一想到等待自个儿的命运，吓得浑身抖如筛糠。各大牧主——老夫推断得一点没错——苦于屡遭窃贼盗卖羊群；因而联合确立了由东家亲笔签署的准许批文制度，以遏制欺诈盗卖行径；并设下圈套专捕盗羊贼寇。而这个老谋深算、阴险狡诈的恶棍，竟然不惜自降身份乔装成破烂放羊倌四处钓鱼诱捕盗贼，结果恰恰将老夫当成了冤大头给钓上了钩！擒拿老夫的差官躬身启奏道：
“‘启奏老爷，属下人赃并获，当场拿获了这个恶徒，’他抬手指着老夫，‘他竟敢在身上毫无半纸批文的情形下，私自兜售老爷您的私产羊群！看光景定是他趁夜黑风高连夜盗赶出山的。依照去年斋月刚过由贵议事会颁布的成文律令，对他的生杀予夺全凭老爷乾纲独断。失主享有最终裁决大权！’
“在老夫惊恐骇然的注视下，昔日的放羊同伴脸上堆满了令人齿冷的厌恶鄙夷之色，冷冷说道：‘且将案情从头到尾细细禀来，好让老夫酌定该施以何等刑罚！老夫雇佣此人不过短短数日，打一开始老夫便觉察出他贼眉鼠眼心怀鬼胎。且从实招来！’
“‘属下查明，他正在公然兜售老爷的羊群，成交市价近乎高达三千（3,000）枚金币，’差官冷酷地回禀道。‘此人理当严惩以儆效尤，因老爷明鉴，他是咱们在这座集市上破天荒第一遭当场逮获的无证盗卖重犯！毫无疑问（属下手头有人证画押），他原打算卷走全部巨款，（或许伙同属下尚未查明的同谋党羽）远走高飞逃之夭夭！’
“听闻此言，老夫昔日的同伴双拳紧攥，在维持其尊贵身份所允许的限度内，极尽所能展现出暴跳如雷的滔天狂怒——穿着老夫那身华服锦袍，显得威风八面、不可一世——他失声痛斥道：‘什么？老夫悉心抚育、收留接济的一介家奴，竟然胆敢犯下如此大逆不道、丧心病狂的滔天罪愆！咱们当初订立这项规章是何等的高瞻远瞩啊！差官，你在司职之上又是何等的忠勇敏锐，竟在此地擒获了首名胆敢行窃的恶贼！在他彻底酿成滔天罪行之前将其绳之以法，又是何等的快慰人心！天道昭昭，天意又是何等的神威显赫！’——说到这里，他双眼虔诚地望向苍天——‘竟将此等恶贼亲手交托到咱们手中以儆效尤！”

===Original===

Come, let us cut off his head with a blunt saw.’
“The officer who had thus traitorously caught me bowed low and said, ‘Hearing and obedience! But if my lord will take council I would speak.’
“ ‘What is it you would say?’ said my companion, who resumed his seat but slowly and seemed displeased at the interruption.
“ ‘My lord,’ said the official, ‘I suggest that if you cut off his miserable head with a blunt saw, though doubtless it would have a good effect for the moment and strike terror into the hearts of those here who see it, so that never more shall sheep be stolen from this market, nor ever more shall we suffer as we have suffered in the past, yet it would be of slighter effect than what I shall propose. For to hear of a man’s execution is one thing, but to hear his own relation of his sufferings is another. I propose therefore that he shall be beaten at great length but not to the point of death; on the approaching of that consummation let him be released to crawl away and tell his story throughout our countries to whoever will listen. Such an example would be of far more service to the owners, my lord, than his death would be, and I promise that he shall be beaten in the most expert manner to the advantage of all posterity.’
“Even as he advised so was it done. I was given the bastinado without mercy until I thought I should have expired, and then under every circumstance of ignominy I was turned loose with a week’s provision of coarse meal into that deserted country, to spread terror among the servants of the wealthy owners and by my example to deter them from ever attempting to play tricks with their masters’ goods.
“I, who from my youth have abhorred the ill use of servants, I who had founded once so great a fortune and proved so kind a master to hosts of dependents, I in my ingenuousness and simple heart could not have believed that such trickery existed in the world! I had been wholly duped!
“As I limped from village to village, begging my bread, I heard the whole story and it exactly confirmed the conclusion I had reached when I first stood trembling before the Sheiks at the Mosque.
“The shepherd in his poor clothes was the richest sheep owner in the country.

===Chinese===

来人啊，速速拿一柄生锈的钝锯，将他的狗头活活锯下来！’
“那位出卖诱捕老夫的差官深深躬身施礼道：‘属下唯命是从！但若蒙老爷不弃，容属下进一言。’
“‘你还有何话要说？’老夫的同伴缓缓坐回宝座，对这一打岔显得颇为不悦。
“‘老爷，’差官启奏道，‘属下以为，倘若用钝锯锯下他可怜的狗头，虽说眼下一时大快人心、能让目睹惨状的市井之徒心惊肉跳，以保往后集市上绝无宵小再敢盗羊，免除咱们昔日蒙受的惨重亏蚀，但这等震慑终归稍逊于属下的另一策。盖因听闻某人被砍头不过是耳旁过客，而亲耳听闻受刑者自个儿痛说惨绝人寰的受刑经历，则完全是另一码撕心裂肺的震撼。因此属下建议：将此人严加拷打、大施笞刑杖毙之罚，但暂且留他一口游丝残喘；待其行将毙命之际，将他驱逐释放，任由他爬着逃窜四方，去往邻邦向所有过往看客哭诉自个儿的悲惨遭遇。老爷，对咱们全体牧主而言，留下这等活生生的前车之鉴，远比让他一死了之更有裨益；属下敢向您担保，定会指派最老练的刽子手施刑，好教这等威慑惠及千秋万代！’
“果不其然，言听计从，事情便这般照办了。老夫被施以惨无人道的严酷笞杖之刑（bastinado），皮开肉绽，直痛得老夫几度昏死过去，以为此番定要命丧黄泉；随后，在饱尝了一切奇耻大辱之后，他们赏给了老夫仅够维持一（1）周的粗劣麦粉，便将老夫驱逐抛弃在这片荒无人烟的荒原穷乡之中，好让老夫去在豪门牧主们的家奴仆役中传播无尽的恐怖，以老夫这副惨绝人寰的人肉活标本，警告他们永生永世休想再敢染指东家的一草一木！
“老夫自幼便对虐待仆役的行径深恶痛绝，老夫曾缔造过如此赫赫的泼天家业、待成群的依附食客如慈父般宽仁，凭着老夫那颗天真赤诚的赤子之心，老夫做梦也想不到这世间竟会有如此丧尽天良的阴毒诡计！老夫被人家彻头彻尾当猴给耍了！
“当老夫一瘸一拐地辗转行乞于一个个穷乡僻壤之间、沿门托钵讨要残羹剩饭之际，老夫方才探听到了整桩隐秘的全貌；这与老夫当初战战兢兢立在清真寺前诸位谢赫面前时所暗自推断的结论，严丝合缝分毫不差。
“原来，那位身披破烂褴褛衣裳的放羊倌，恰恰是整片疆土上腰缠万贯的最顶流大牧场主！”

===Original===

He and his fellow lords had for some years past suffered from surreptitious sales, they had appointed officers to watch the markets and even so had not always been able to recover the purchase money from their agents. They had therefore﻿—as I guessed﻿—instituted a system of permits so that no man could sell in the market without their signed licences and so that each man so selling could be detected as a thief by the officers of the markets. But how should I, a poor stranger from oversea, know anything of this? The blackness of the treason wounded me even more than the sufferings of my bastinado. I almost lost my faith in man; by the Everlasting Mercy I did not lose my faith in Heaven!﻿ ﻿… Nothing, my boys,” said the old man, his voice trembling, as he remembered this terrible passage of the past, “nothing but Religion supported me during the fearful days that followed. I think I can say with humility that one less founded in a firm reliance upon his Maker would have grown embittered. I might have turned into one of those useless people who, as the result of misfortune, become railers, nourishing a perpetual quarrel against mankind. But our Holy Religion stood me in good stead, and as my wounds healed and as my wanderings led me further from the scene of my torture I recovered so much of my spirits as once more to attempt what might have seemed impossible; I faced the world again. It would seem that those for whom Heaven has high designs, those for whom, like myself, it intends the highest positions among men, must in the divine scheme pass first through the fire and the ordeal. Happy the men who (like myself) profit by such visitations and retain unclouded their childlike trust in God.”
“Amen,” murmured the eldest of the nephews.
“What was that you said?” cried Mahmoud sharply?
“I said ‘Amen,’ Uncle,” answered the lad in humble tones. His uncle scanned him narrowly.
“Well,” he muttered, “I suppose you are too much of a fool to have meant it ill.﻿ ﻿…”
At this the strident nasals of the muezzin suddenly shrieked from the neighbouring minaret and the young lads with unaccustomed rapidity vacated the great merchant’s apartment.

===Chinese===

“数年来，他同他的牧主同僚们饱受家奴暗中私自盗卖牲口之苦；他们曾指派差官严密盘查市集，但即便如此，也屡屡无法从经手伙计手里追回卖羊的银两。因此——正如老夫所料——他们联手设立了一套严格的许可证批文制度，规定任何人未经东家亲笔签署许可证皆不得在集市上交易，好让市集稽查差官能当场将此类私卖者视作盗贼捉拿归案。然而，老夫一个打海外漂泊而来的凄惨外邦异乡客，又从何知晓这等内幕玄机呢？这等背信弃义的阴毒构陷，比起肉体遭受的皮肉笞刑杖击，更深深刺痛了老夫的心扉。老夫险些对人世间的良知彻底万念俱灰；然而全凭真主永恒浩荡的洪恩，老夫并未对苍天丧失信心！……孩子们啊，”忆及这段不堪回首的至暗岁月，老人的声音微微发颤，“在接踵而至的那些暗无天日的凄惨岁月里，唯有宗教信仰在支撑着老夫的残躯！老夫敢谦卑地断言：换作一个对造物主缺少坚定依归之人，心智早就彻底被仇恨怨毒所侵蚀了。老夫很可能会沦为那种因遭遇横祸便整日怨天尤人、同全人类陷入无休止龃龉攻讦的无用废人。然而咱们神圣的宗教拯救了老夫；随着伤口逐渐结痂愈合，随着老夫的流浪足迹渐渐远离那片受难酷刑的伤心地，老夫的心智精神渐渐复苏，竟再次萌生出了去挑战看似绝无可能的绝地翻盘之雄心；老夫重新昂首面对这个险恶的世界！看光景，凡是蒙苍天寄予厚望之人，凡是如老夫这般命中注定要君临尘世至高权位之人，依循神圣的天道机运，皆必须首先历经烈火与酷刑的淬炼洗礼。凡是能（如老夫一般）从天降灾厄中汲取智识、并对真主始终保持赤子般纯粹信任之人，才是天下间最有福报之人啊！”
“阿门（Amen），”长侄在底下喃喃应道。
“你刚才嘟囔什么？”马哈茂德眼神一凛，厉声喝问道。
“侄儿说的是‘阿门’，叔父，”少年毕恭毕敬、谦卑地答道。叔父眯起双眼，极其警惕严厉地上下打量着他。
“哼，”老人嘟囔道，“老夫料想你也不过是个蠢笨如猪的傻小子，谅你也没胆量敢存什么歹意！……”
话音未落，邻近宣礼塔上猛然爆发出了宣礼员尖厉刺耳的鼻音唤礼长啸，少年们以异乎寻常的飞快身手，顷刻间逃也似地退出了这位大富翁的华丽深宅。

## XIV *Itmi’nán al-Nafs*, or “The Peace of the Soul” / 第十四章 灵魂的安宁
===Original===

“We had arrived,” said the excellent old merchant to his nephews when they were once more seated round him for the last of these entertaining relations. “We had arrived, my dear boys, in the story of my life, at my considerable increase of fortune through the financial aid I had given to two states, one of which after a long and exhaustive war had conquered and annexed the other.
“My position (if you recollect) at the close of this adventure was that without having spent any money of my own I was now receiving permanently and forever a very large yearly revenue set aside from the taxes of both states.
“Not a man reaped or dug or carried heavy water jars under the hot sun, not a man groomed a horse or bent under the weight of a pack, not a man added brick to brick or mixed mortar, not a man did any useful act from one end of the state to the other, but some part of his toil was done for me, and this state of affairs was, as I have said, as fixed and permanent as human things can be.
“I was therefore what even financiers call well-to-do; one way with another I was now worth perhaps twenty million pieces of gold: but that is but guess work, it may have been twenty-five.
“You might imagine that I would have been content from that day onwards to repose in my opulence.
“I might well have been tempted to do so, for to that opulence was added a singular and fervid popularity. I was alluded to in public and private as the man who had saved the state by his financial genius during the Great War. Even the conquered remembered me gratefully for the aid I had extended to them in their need; while since I could not satisfy my personal desires without at least feeding a great host of dancers, bearers, artists, my kindness in affording employment was universally recognized; moreover (since among these people wealth is a test of greatness) I was admitted to their senate without the usual formality of a cash payment.
“The world was now indeed at my feet. But you must know,” continued Mahmoud with something of sadness in his voice, “you must know, my dear, innocent lads, that wealth will not stop still. The mere administration of a great fortune tends to increase it, and when one has for years found one’s occupation in the accumulation of money, it is difficult in middle age to abandon the rooted habit.

===Chinese===

“孩子们，”当侄儿们再次围坐在慈祥的老商人身边聆听这最后一段跌宕起伏的生动传奇时，老富翁开口说道，“在我生平的故事里，咱们上回讲到了我如何凭借向交战两国提供巨额金融资助，使我的财富实现了翻天覆地的暴增——彼时其中一国历经旷日持久、耗尽元气的血战，最终兼并吞并了另一国。
“倘若你们还记得的话，在那场豪赌大冒险收官之际，我个人的境况是：在未曾掏出自个儿半文钱真金白银的前提下，我自此享有了一份永生永世源源不绝的大笔岁入年金，直接从两国的赋税中先行扣除剥离。
“在烈日炙烤之下，普天之下没有一个农夫挥汗收割、破土开荒或挑运沉重的储水陶罐；没有一个马夫给坐骑刷洗梳毛、或在沉重的货捆重压下弯腰喘息；没有一个泥瓦匠添砖加瓦或搅拌灰浆泥沙；从这个国家的这一头到那一头，没有一个子民在从事任何有益的劳作生产，而其血汗成果里没有一部分是在为老夫白白打工效劳的！正如老夫所言，这套制度架构，在人类尘世所能企及的限度内，已然达到了最不可动摇、万世不竭的极致境界！
“因此，即便在金融巨鳄的眼光看来，老夫也算得上是腰缠万贯的大富翁了；零零总总通盘算下来，老夫当时的身家约莫值两千万（20,000,000）枚金币；不过这也仅仅是粗略估算，说不定足足有两千五百万（25,000,000）枚之巨。
“你们或许会以为，从那一刻起，老夫理当心满意足、高枕无忧地躺在金山银山中颐养天年了。
“老夫委实一度受到过这等诱惑，因除却那泼天富贵之外，老夫更享有着一种异乎寻常、狂热崇敬的崇高民望。无论在朝堂公堂还是市井私语之间，人人都将老夫奉为在殊死大战中凭借金融天才经天纬地拯救国家的救世功臣！即便那些沦为亡国奴的战败臣民，亦对老夫在他们水深火热之际伸出的借贷援手感恩戴德；加之老夫若要满足自个儿平日的奢靡享乐，少说也得养活成群结队的舞姬、轿夫、伶人画师，因而老夫提供就业活路的‘菩萨心肠’更是有口皆碑；再者（鉴于在这等蛮夷国度里，财富向来是衡量伟岸的唯一试金石），老夫甚至无需缴纳惯常的现银入门费，便破格荣膺跻身他们的元老院参政议事。
“整座大千世界已然彻底匍匐在老夫脚下！然而你们必须懂得，”马哈茂德说到此处，嗓音里隐隐泛起了一丝伤感与落寞，“我亲爱纯洁的孩子们，你们必须懂得：财富这头巨兽是绝不会止步不前的！单单是打理运作这笔惊天家业，便会促使它如同雪球般越滚越大；一个人若是年复一年以囤积金银财宝为毕生唯一营生，待到人入中年，想要斩断这深植骨髓的积习，简直比登天还难。”

===Original===

Therefore, though I now had all that life could give me, I proceeded henceforward for many years to increase that substance with which the Mercy of Allah had provided me; and I discovered at the outset of this new career that to be the financier I had become, and to have behind me the resources which I now possessed, made my further successes a matter not of hazard but of certitude. Shall I briefly tell you the various ways in which my efforts proceeded?”
“Pray, pray do so,” said his little nephews with sparkling eyes, each imagining himself in the dazzling position of his wealthy relative.
“Very well,” sighed Mahmond. “It will be of little use to any of you; but if it does no more than confirm you in your religion what I have to tell you will not have been told in vain.”
The merchant was silent for a moment, and then began the category of his financial proceedings:
“Neighbouring states which had heard of the powerful new methods I had introduced would approach me from time to time for financial assistance. To these I made the same invariable reply, that upon certain terms, which I myself would fix, I was content to ‘float their loans’; that is, the rich men of their country (or of any other) should pay into my office the sums they were prepared to lend to such a state, and I would pay back a part, but not the whole, of the amount so accumulated to the state in question. The enormous service I rendered by allowing my office to be used for this transaction was everywhere recognized, and by such operations my fortunes proceeded to grow.
“It was at this moment in my career that I married my wife, your dear aunt, who generally resides, as you know, in that one of my country palaces called Dar-al-Beida on the banks of the Tigris some four days’ journey from here. It is a delightful spot which I remember well though I have not seen it for years.﻿ ﻿… Some day, perhaps, I will visit it again, but not to sleep.
“Your dear aunt was, and is, my boys, a most remarkable woman: fit to compete with the master spirits of our time: yea! even with my own.
“Her birth﻿—I need not conceal it﻿—was humble. She was but a chance hireling in my offices, with the duty of sorting my papers and keeping indices of the same.

===Chinese===

“因此，虽说凡尘俗世所能给予老夫的一切享乐老夫皆已唾手可得，在此后漫长的悠悠岁月里，老夫依旧马不停蹄地去滚大由真主的仁慈（the Mercy of Allah）所赐予老夫的这笔泼天基业；而且在这段崭新生涯的起点，老夫便赫然领悟到：一旦蜕变为了老夫如今这等通天彻地的金融寡头，坐拥着身后这般排山倒海的雄厚底蕴，老夫往后的一切发迹掠夺，便再也不是碰运气的侥幸赌博，而是十拿九稳、铁板钉钉的必然因果！要不要老夫给你们扼要讲讲，老夫究竟是如何施展各路生财神技的？”
“求求叔父，快讲讲吧！”小侄儿们双眼闪烁着贪婪炽热的光芒，异口同声地央求道，每个人脑海中都在幻想着自个儿有朝一日能坐上这位豪富长辈那般光芒万丈的至尊宝座。
“好吧，”马哈茂德长叹了一口气。“虽说这对你们当中的任何一个皆毫无卵用；但倘若这番话能坚定你们对宗教的虔诚敬畏，老夫这番苦心便不算白费口舌。”
老商人沉吟片刻，随后如数家珍般列举起他的金融洗劫名目：
“周边邻近各国风闻老夫推行的一套威力无边的全新金融手段，时不时便有特使登门，乞求老夫施以金融援手。对此，老夫的回应历来千篇一律：在老夫亲自钦定的苛刻条款下，老夫倒乐意屈尊为他们‘承销发行国债’；亦即，由他们本国（或普天之下任何其他邦国）的富绅巨贾，将准备借给该国朝廷的银两尽数汇入老夫的钱庄票号，随后老夫再将其中的一部分——决计不是全额——抽头克扣之后转付给求贷的朝廷。老夫肯大发慈悲出借自个儿的钱庄招牌承揽这桩过桥交割，天下人皆将此视作天大的恩德；正是借由这等空手套白狼的翻云覆雨手，老夫的家底如滚雪球般疯狂暴涨。
“正是在老夫生涯的这一关头，老夫迎娶了你们敬爱的婶娘；如你们所知，她平日里常年幽居在底格里斯河（Tigris）畔老夫的一处名为达尔贝达（Dar-al-Beida）的郊野行宫之中，离咱们巴格达（Baghdad）足足有四（4）天的脚程。那委实是一处风景如画的绝妙胜境，老夫记忆犹新，虽说老夫已有多年未曾踏足半步了。……日后兴许哪天老夫还会再去散散心，但绝不在那儿过夜留宿。
“孩子们，你们敬爱的婶娘不论过去还是现在，皆是一位非同凡响的奇女子：其手腕魄力足以同当世最拔尖的枭雄巨擘一较高下——不错！甚至同老夫本人的才干亦是不分伯仲！
“她的出身——老夫大可不必讳言——其实甚是低微贫贱。她起初不过是老夫钱庄事务所里一名打杂的无名雇工，司职替老夫整理机要文书、编纂档案索引。”

===Original===

“Such was her interest in affairs that she was at the pains to take copies and tracings of many particularly private and important passages with her own hand, and keep these by her in a private place. I was struck beyond measure at such industry and preoccupation with business on the part of a woman (and one so poor!). I conceived an ardent desire to possess these specimens of her skill; but﻿—to my astonishment, and (at first) confusion﻿—she humbly replied that a profound though secret affection which she had conceived for me forbade her to part with these precious souvenirs of myself. I was so absorbed in their pursuit that, rather than lose them, I married this queen of finance﻿—recognizing in her an equal genius with my own. Our wedding was of the simplest. I took comfort from the consideration that it proved me superior to all the nobles of the court and indifferent to an alliance with their families.
“Immediately after the wedding my wife, your dear aunt, asked me for money wherewith to travel, a request I readily granted. She traversed for her pleasure I knew not what foreign lands, always, and gladly, furnished with the wherewithal from my cash box; but on my returning later to Baghdad, my native place, she unexpectedly appeared at my door, and I was happy to build for her that country Palace of Dar-al-Beida to the charm of which I have alluded. Unfortunately its air suits me ill, while she (your dear aunt) suffocates in the atmosphere of Baghdad. It is often thus in old age.﻿ ﻿…”
Mahmoud mused and continued:
“But let us return to my further activities in that far land:
“I next designed a scheme whereby every form of human misfortune, fire, disease, paralysis, madness, and the rest, might be alleviated to the sufferer by the payment of regular sums of money upon the advent of the disaster; weekly sums for his support if he were rendered infirm or ill, a lump sum to replace whatever he might have totally lost, and so forth. A short and easy survey of the average number of times in which such accidents took place permitted me to establish my system. I charged for 100 dinars worth of such insurance 110 dinars, and my benevolence was praised even more highly than my ingenuity.

===Chinese===

“然而她对商业利益的钻营嗅觉何其敏锐！她竟不辞辛劳，亲手将无数涉及绝密机要的账目信札与致命条款一字一句私下誊抄描摹成副页，秘密私藏在自个儿的隐秘居所之中。老夫简直惊得目瞪口呆：一个妇道人家（且又那般赤贫如洗！），竟能对金融机密展现出如此深沉的匠心执念！老夫心头顿时按捺不住，燃起了要将她手中的这批机要罪证墨宝占为己有的狂热渴求；然而——让老夫既惊愕又（起初）无地自容的是——她竟谦卑温婉地回绝道：她对老夫怀揣着一份刻骨铭心却秘而不宣的深情爱意，这令她决计不舍得割爱出让自己心上人的珍贵定情信物！老夫满心只想将那些致命软肋追讨收回，以至于两害相权取其轻，索性将这位金融女皇明媒正娶迎娶过门——老夫委实从她身上看到了与老夫自个儿旗鼓相当的天才魔性！我们的婚典办得极其朴素节俭。老夫心底倒也释怀，自慰道：此举恰好向世人证明了老夫凌驾于朝中一切庸碌贵族之上，根本不屑于同他们的显贵家族联姻结盟！
“新婚燕尔刚过，我的发妻、你们敬爱的婶娘，便开口向老夫索要游历天下的巨额盘缠，老夫毫不犹豫地慷慨应允。她随心所欲周游四海，去往老夫连名号都叫不出来的异国他乡游山玩水，一应开销尽数、且极尽欢欣地由老夫的钱柜全额实报实销；然而待到日后老夫荣归故里、落叶归根返回巴格达（Baghdad）故土之际，她却冷不防再次出现在老夫的门槛前！老夫只得满心欢喜地为她大兴土木，修建了适才提及的那座风景迷人的达尔贝达郊外行宫。极其不幸的是，那里的水土极其克老夫的体质，而她（你们敬爱的婶娘）只要一踏进巴格达的空气，便会胸闷憋闷得喘不过气来。人到了风烛残年，世道往往便是这般无奈啊！……”
马哈茂德陷入了片刻沉思，随后接茬道：
“言归正传，且让咱们回到老夫在那片蛮夷异域的后续雄图伟业：
“随后，老夫匠心独运地炮制出了一套绝妙机制：凡是世间一切形形色色的人间横祸——火灾、瘟疫、瘫痪中风、疯癫失心等等——皆可通过在灾难降临之际向受难苦主定期赔付银两来予以抚恤；倘若不幸伤残生病，便每周按期发放糊口救济金；倘若家产荡然无存，便一次性赔付一笔包圆巨款以资补偿，诸如此类。凭着对各类不幸灾祸发生概率的简明大体精算摸底，老夫的这套保险体系便赫然拔地而起。凡是保额价值一百（100）第纳尔的这等保险，老夫便索取一百一十（110）第纳尔的昂贵保费！世人对老夫仁义慈悲的狂热赞颂，甚至远远盖过了对老夫经商神机的激赏喝彩！”

===Original===

“Men flocked in thousands, and at last in millions, to secure themselves from the uncertainty of human life by giving me of their free will more money in regular payments than I could by any accident be compelled to pay out to any one of them upon his reaching old age, his suffering from fire, or his contraction of an illness. Nay, death itself at last entered into this design, and having found that young men just of age live upon the average for forty years, I asked them to pay for their heirs annual sums calculated as though that period were thirty, and thus I continued to accumulate wealth from a perennial source.”
“But why,” began one of his nephews excitedly.﻿ ﻿…
“Why *what*?” asked his uncle, severely.
“Why,” said the poor lad, a little abashed by his uncle’s tone, “why did they pay you more for a thing than it was worth?”
Mahmoud stroked his long white beard and looked up sideways towards the highly decorated vault of the gorgeous apartment. He remained thus plunged in thought for perhaps thirty seconds, and when he broke the silence it was to say that he did not know. “But no matter﻿—” he added hurriedly. “I paid for a law which compelled all slaves to insure and so was certain of a fixed revenue in this kind.
“And I had many other resources,” he continued cheerily. “If those who had made many such regular payments to me to insure against death, old age, disease, and the rest, happened to fall into an embarrassed condition and to need a loan, I was always ready to advance them their own money again at interest, or did I ever find them unwilling to subscribe the bond. Further I urged and tempted many to fall into arrears and so possessed myself of all they had paid in. The vast sums paid to me in these various fashions were sometimes too great for investment within the state, and I had to look further afield. But here again by the Mercy of Allah suggestions of the most lucrative sort perpetually occurred to my religious soul.
“Not infrequently I would lay out a million or two in the purchase of a great estate situated at some distance which, when I had acquired it, I would declare to be packed with gold, silver, diamonds, copper, salt, and china clay beneath the earth, and on its surface loaded with red pepper and other most precious fruits.

===Chinese===

“数以千计、乃至最终数以百万计的善男信女，为了在这吉凶未卜的人世间买得一份心安，如过江之鲫般争先恐后蜂拥而至；他们心甘情愿、按部就班地将大把真金白银持续奉缴到老夫手中，其累计总额之庞大，即便哪天遭逢大难老夫不得不向他们当中的某一个赔付养老、火灾或染病的理赔金，也绝不可能动摇老夫的分毫毫厘！到后来，甚至连凡人必至的死期寿终亦被老夫顺理成章纳入了这套算计之中：老夫通过摸底查明，刚刚成年的年轻壮丁平均尚有四十（40）年阳寿，老夫便哄骗他们为子孙后代按三十（30）年阳寿的年限折算年金每年缴纳寿险保费，借由这条川流不息的永动机财源，老夫的聚宝盆日夜不停地鲸吞天下之财。”
“可是为何……”一名侄儿忍不住激动地插话道。
“为何‘怎样’？”叔父眼神一凛，严厉地质问道。
“为何，”可怜的少年被叔父的威严吓得缩了缩脖子，怯生生地嘟囔道，“为何世人竟肯为一个根本不值那么多钱的物什，心甘情愿掏出超出其价值的巨款呢？”
马哈茂德伸手抚弄着胸前雪白的拂胸长须，斜起双眼，深邃地凝视着华丽殿堂上那精雕细琢的繁复穹顶。他就那般静默陷入了沉思，足足出神了约莫三十（30）秒之久；当他终于打破沉默时，吐出的回答竟是：他自个儿也压根闹不明白。“但这根本无关紧要——”他急忙改口岔开话头，“老夫后来花巨资买通官府颁布了一道成文法令，强制全天下所有的奴仆苦力皆必须强制投保，如此一来，老夫在这条门道上的旱涝保收年金更是固若金汤！
“老夫手头的敛财法宝还多着呢，”老富翁喜笑颜开、津津有味地继续炫耀道，“倘若那些年复一年向老夫按期奉缴巨款以投保死亡、衰老、伤病等等的储户，碰巧遭逢周转不灵的拮据窘境需要借贷周转，老夫向来极其乐善好施，随时乐意以高利贷的利息将他们自个儿存进来的血汗钱重新借贷给他们，而且他们签署抵押借据时从来都是感恩戴德、求之不得！再者，老夫更有意暗中诱惑、逼迫众多储户断缴逾期，借此堂而皇之地依法将他们此前奉缴的全部血汗钱连本带利尽数吞没充公！通过所有这些琳琅满目的门道聚敛而来的金山银海，委实太过浩瀚庞大，以致在这区区一国之内根本寻觅不到足够的投资蓄水池，老夫不得不将贪婪的目光投向更广袤的海外荒原。然而在此处，承蒙真主的仁慈（the Mercy of Allah），最暴利的生财灵感再次源源不绝地涌入老夫虔诚圣洁的灵魂深处！
“老夫时不时便会大笔一挥，砸下一、两百万（1,000,000或2,000,000）金币，在千里迢迢的偏远穷乡购置下一片广袤荒凉的大宗地产；地皮一俟过户到老夫名下，老夫便向全天下隆重昭告：此地地底深处蕴藏着数不尽的黄金、白银、钻石、紫铜、精盐与高岭土，而地面之上更漫山遍野盛产着名贵的红胡椒与各色旷世奇珍异果！”

===Original===

I have no doubt these estates were often of a promising nature, though travellers have assured me that some were mere desert; in one case, to my certain knowledge, the estate did not even exist. But it really mattered little whether I spoke truly or falsely with regard to such ventures, for my method of dealing with them made them, whether they were of trifling value or of more, invariably profitable to many besides myself and a blessing to the whole state.”
“But uncle,” interjected another nephew, “how could that be?”
“You will easily see,” said Mahmoud with a pitying smile, “when you hear the sequel. I was not so selfish as to retain these properties in my own hands. I would offer them to the public for sale, and being in a position to pay many poets, scribes, and public story tellers who should make general the praises of the estates in question, an active competition among many thousands would arise to become part purchasers.
